### [108] [SHIELDS: Automating OS Hardening with Iterative Multi-Agent Remediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05476)
*Andrew Hamara,Dwight Horne,Aldehir Rojas,Timothy Kurniawan,Sophie Lamothe,Vishal Suresh,Nicholas Turoci,Lawrence Wong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SHIELDS是一个基于多智能体和大语言模型的系统，通过迭代反馈驱动的方式自动修复OS安全配置违规问题，在多个VM配置下成功修复高达73%的扫描发现，且效果更多取决于工具使用和信息收集能力而非模型参数规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全配置错误是OS层被攻破的主因之一，手动维持系统符合DISA STIG等标准繁琐且昂贵，现有自动化工具依赖静态预写的修复动作，缺乏灵活性和适应性。

Method: 提出SHIELDS——一个多智能体系统，利用LLM将OS加固视为迭代的、基于反馈的过程：持续提出修复方案，并根据目标系统执行和验证扫描的反馈进行精炼优化。在多种VM配置下使用6种当代LLM（20B~400B参数）进行评估。

Result: SHIELDS成功修复了高达73%的扫描发现项；成功的关键更多依赖于有效的工具使用和信息收集能力，而非模型参数规模。

Conclusion: 该系统为在计算资源受限或安全隐私需求驱动使用本地模型的环境中，减轻安全合规负担提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Security misconfigurations remain a leading cause of OS-level compromise, and manually keeping systems compliant with standards like Defense Information Systems Agency (DISA) Security Technical Implementation Guides (STIGs) is a tedious and expensive process. Existing compliance automation tools can reduce some of this burden, but they depend on static, pre-written corrective actions. In this paper, we introduce SHIELDS, a multi-agent system that uses large language models (LLMs) to approach OS hardening as an iterative, feedback-driven process. Instead of applying fixed remediations, SHIELDS continuously proposes fixes and refines them based on feedback from target system execution and validation scans. We evaluate the system across multiple virtual machine configurations using six contemporary LLMs ranging from 20B to 400B parameters, and find that SHIELDS successfully remediates up to 73% of scan findings. Our results also suggest that success in this setting depends less on model size (parameter count) than on effective tool use and information gathering, paving a practical path toward reducing the burden of security compliance in environments where compute is limited or security and privacy needs drive local model use.

</details>


### [109] [Bitcoin After Block Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05503)
*Junhyuk Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了比特币区块奖励归零后，矿工仅依赖交易费时可能出现的偏离诚实挖矿行为的条件，并提出了提高偏离阈值、缓解激励崩溃的协议级机制组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币区块奖励将逐步降至零，这引发了网络安全性担忧：当矿工仅依赖交易费时，是否还能维持诚实挖矿的激励？

Method: 采用序贯决策模型分析诚实矿工与偏离矿工的收益，识别偏离阈值G_t，并评估三种协议级机制（基础费、费用下限、自适应最大区块大小规则）的组合效果。

Result: 2024年比特币减半前后未发现大规模结构性偏离；但区块奖励移除后，即使交易费占比极小，偏离也可能发生；所提出的机制组合能有效提高偏离阈值。

Conclusion: 为评估比特币区块奖励消失后的安全性提供了实用基准，表明适当协议设计可缓解激励崩溃问题。

Abstract: Bitcoin's block reward is scheduled to decline to zero, raising concerns about whether the network can remain secure once miners rely solely on transaction fees. This paper seeks to identify the conditions under which large-scale and persistent deviation from honest mining can arise.
  We analyze and compare the payoffs of honest and deviating miners in a sequential decision model, and identify a deviation threshold $G_t$ at which honest mining ceases to be privately optimal. Around the 2024 Bitcoin halving, we show that current mining behavior does not exhibit large-scale or structural deviation. However, when the block reward is removed, the $G_t$ criterion implies that deviation can arise even with a very small fraction of transaction fees.
  Finally, we evaluate three protocol-level mechanisms: Base Fee, Fee Floor, and an adaptive maximum block size rule, and show that their combination raises the deviation threshold and mitigates incentive breakdown in a fee-only regime. These results provide a practical benchmark for assessing Bitcoin's security as block rewards disappear.

</details>


### [110] [ZERO-APT: A Closed-Loop Adversarial Framework for LLM-Driven Automated Penetration Testing under Intelligent Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05567)
*Anlan Zheng,Tiantian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ZERO-APT是一个攻击者-防御者-裁判三方框架，通过嵌入LLM驱动的实时防御者、架构级因果一致性机制和结构化CTI报告，解决了自动化渗透测试代理在真实性、一致性和可审计性三方面的缺陷。在Windows Server 2022后渗透原型中，攻击成功率79%，因果一致性评分0.860，并实现端到端决策可审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自动化渗透测试代理存在三个关键缺陷：1）仅在静态目标上评估，缺乏智能防御的真实性；2）多步攻击链的因果一致性依赖于不稳定的LLM推理；3）代理决策对分析人员不透明。这三个问题通常被孤立修补，缺乏统一解决方案。

Method: 提出ZERO-APT框架，采用回合制攻击者-防御者-裁判架构：1）真实性：嵌入可配置的LLM Defender，消费Sysmon遥测数据实时检测攻击；2）一致性：通过三个架构机制将因果一致性从LLM推理迁移到系统架构中——规划与执行分离、多维ReAct反馈、硬约束过滤的动作库；3）可审计性：专用Judge代理裁决每轮博弈、维护全局状态、生成结构化CTI报告。

Result: 在Windows Server 2022后渗透原型上，跨五种场景、三种防御配置评估：攻击成功率79%（Aurora 22%，PentestGPT 39%），因果一致性评分0.860（Aurora 0.930，Claude Code 0.520），通过结构化CTI报告实现端到端决策可审计。

Conclusion: ZERO-APT在单一架构中统一解决了真实性、一致性和可审计性三个问题，并发布基准测试以支持在智能防御环境下评估渗透代理。

Abstract: LLM-driven automated penetration testing agents are typically evaluated against static targets that neither detect nor respond to attacks, so their behavior under intelligent defense remains untested. The causal consistency of multi-step attack chains likewise hinges on unstable LLM reasoning, and agent decisions remain opaque to human analysts. These three shortcomings, in realism, consistency, and auditability, are usually patched in isolation. We present ZERO-APT, a turn-based attacker-defender-judge framework that addresses them within a single architecture. For realism, ZERO-APT embeds a configurable LLM Defender that consumes Sysmon telemetry and detects attacks in real time, exposing the attacker to a live opponent rather than a passive target. For consistency, three architectural mechanisms move causal consistency from unstable LLM reasoning into enforced system architecture: separation of planning from execution, multi-dimensional ReAct feedback, and a hard-constraint-filtered action library. For auditability, a dedicated Judge agent adjudicates each round, maintains global state, and emits structured post-hoc CTI reports that make every decision traceable. We evaluate a Windows Server 2022 post-exploitation prototype across five scenarios with three Defender configurations. ZERO-APT reaches 79\% attack success rate (Aurora 22\%, PentestGPT 39\%), a Causal Consistency Score of 0.860 (Aurora 0.930, Claude Code 0.520), and end-to-end decision auditability through structured CTI reports. We release the benchmark to support evaluation of penetration agents under intelligent defense.

</details>


### [111] [Dimensionality Reduction for Cyberattack Classification: A Comparative Evaluation of PCA and Linear Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05584)
*Nelly Elsayed,Zag ElSayed,Navid Asadizanjani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了主成分分析（PCA）和线性预测编码（LPC）两种特征压缩技术在网络攻击检测分类中的应用。实验表明，PCA在激进压缩下仍能保持分类性能，LPC在略微性能损失下提供有竞争力的预测表示，证明大幅降维对分类精度影响甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维特征表示在网络攻击检测中广泛应用，但增加了计算复杂度，阻碍了在资源受限环境中的部署，因此需要有效的特征压缩技术。

Method: 采用PCA和LPC两种降维方法对特征进行压缩，生成不同维度的压缩表示，并在多个分类模型上进行评估比较。

Result: PCA在激进压缩下仍能保持分类性能；LPC提供了有竞争力的预测表示，但性能退化略大。大幅降低特征维度对分类精度影响极小。

Conclusion: 轻量级特征压缩技术能够有效降低特征维度同时保持分类性能，适用于资源受限环境下的网络安全分析，具有良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: High-dimensional feature representations are widely used in machine learning-based cyberattack detection systems. However, they increase computational complexity and may hinder deployment in resource-constrained environments. In this paper, we investigate feature compression techniques for cyberattack classification by comparing two dimensionality reduction approaches: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) and Linear Predictive Coding (LPC). Compressed feature representations with varying dimensionalities are generated and evaluated across several classification models. Experimental analysis demonstrates that PCA preserves classification performance even under aggressive compression. On the other hand, LPC provides competitive predictive representations with slightly larger performance degradation. The results show that substantial reductions in feature dimensionality can be achieved with minimal impact on classification accuracy, highlighting the potential of lightweight feature compression for efficient cybersecurity analytics.

</details>


### [112] [The Coverage Gap: Chile's Cyber Disclosure Framework versus the USA, EU and UK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05594)
*David Mellafe Z*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对智利915个关键基础设施运营商的漏洞披露能力进行全量普查，发现仅1.7%发布可验证的RFC 9116披露渠道，84%存在邮件认证配置错误，并与美英等国进行跨司法管辖区基准对比，提出四阶段改进路线图并开源审计工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施运营商公开暴露程度与其协调漏洞披露的能力之间存在可测量的差距，但目前缺乏系统性量化手段。智利2025年依据第21.663号法律指定了915个OIV，为全量普查提供了独特的制度窗口。

Method: 采用被动式OSINT方法，符合ISO/IEC 29147:2018和智利第21.459号法律安全港条款，对官方目录约98.7%的运营商进行全量普查，聚焦基础披露能力层（Layer 1，可验证的披露联系渠道）。使用RFC 9116、DMARC/DKIM/SPF邮件认证配置及已知漏洞组件等指标进行测量。

Result: 915个OIV中仅16个（1.7%）发布可验证的RFC 9116披露渠道；物理世界基础设施运营商中不足10个，四大银行和两家电信巨头完全缺失；766个（84%）存在邮件认证配置错误；约23.5%使用生命周期终止或已知漏洞组件。与美国联邦民事部门99%的合规率形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 智利OIV漏洞披露能力存在严重不足，以邮件认证强制要求衡量落后美国等约8年、落后丹麦约3年。提出基于BOD 18-01和英国公共部门DMARC工具包的四阶段路线图，并开源anci-oiv-resolver工具以支持独立复现和规模化审计。

Abstract: We introduce the Coverage Gap as a measurable distance between the observable public exposure of critical-infrastructure operators and their declared capability to coordinate vulnerability disclosure. We instantiate it against the 915 Chilean Operadores de Importancia Vital (OIVs -- Operators of Vital Importance) designated by the National Cybersecurity Agency (ANCI) under Ley 21.663 (Resolucion Exenta No. 87, 16 December 2025). Using a passive-only, OSINT-based method consistent with the principles of ISO/IEC 29147:2018 and Chile's computer-crimes safe harbour (Ley 21.459), we conduct a full-universe census of the foundational disclosure-capability layer (Layer 1, verifiable disclosure contact) across approximately 98.7% of the official catalogue. Only 16 of 915 OIVs (1.7%) publish a verifiable RFC 9116 disclosure channel; among operators of physical-world infrastructure -- energy, health, banking, telecommunications, fuel, water, transport, and state administration -- fewer than ten do so, and all four major banks and both telecommunications incumbents lack one entirely. This compares with over 99% adherence in the U.S. federal civilian branch under CISA Binding Operational Directive 18-01. Email-authentication misconfiguration affects 766 of 915 (84%) OIVs, and end-of-life or known-vulnerable stack components an estimated 23.5% (Wilson 95% CI [12%, 38%]). Cross-jurisdictional benchmarking situates Chile roughly eight years behind the USA, the UK, and the Netherlands on email-authentication mandates, and three years behind Denmark. We propose a four-stage roadmap modelled on BOD 18-01 and the UK Public-Sector DMARC Toolkit, and release the open-source tool anci-oiv-resolver (Apache 2.0) to enable independent reproduction of the OIV-domain mapping that underpins universe-scale auditing.

</details>


### [113] [SlotGCG: Exploiting the Positional Vulnerability in LLMs for Jailbreak Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05609)
*Seungwon Jeong,Jiwoo Jeong,Hyeonjin Kim,Yunseok Lee,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SlotGCG通过引入漏洞槽位评分（VSS）来识别提示中最易受攻击的插入位置，从而提升优化型越狱攻击的效果，相比GCG攻击成功率提高14%，防御鲁棒性提高42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化型越狱攻击（如GCG）将对抗性token限制在固定的插入位置（通常是提示后缀），忽略了其他位置插入token的影响，因此需要系统性地探索不同插入位置对越狱攻击效果的影响。

Method: 1) 实证研究提示中不同位置（slots）插入对抗性token对越狱效果的影响；2) 提出漏洞槽位评分（VSS）量化各位置的越狱脆弱性；3) 提出SlotGCG方法，先用VSS评估所有slot，选择最脆弱的slot进行插入，再在该位置执行定向优化攻击。

Result: SlotGCG相比GCG基线的攻击成功率提高14%，收敛速度更快，防御鲁棒性提升42%，且仅增加200ms预处理时间。

Conclusion: 提示中token插入位置对越狱攻击效果有显著影响，SlotGCG提供了一种攻击无关的位置搜索机制，可嵌入任何优化型攻击，以较小预处理代价显著提升攻击性能。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are widely deployed, identifying their vulnerability through jailbreak attacks becomes increasingly critical. Optimization-based attacks like Greedy Coordinate Gradient (GCG) have focused on inserting adversarial tokens to the end of prompts. However, GCG restricts adversarial tokens to a fixed insertion point (typically the prompt suffix), leaving the effect of inserting tokens at other positions unexplored. In this paper, we empirically investigate \emph{slots}, i.e., candidate positions within a prompt where tokens can be inserted. We find that vulnerability to jailbreaking is highly related to the selection of the \emph{slots}. Based on these findings, we introduce the \textit{Vulnerable Slot Score} (VSS) to quantify the positional vulnerability to jailbreaking. We then propose SlotGCG, which evaluates all slots with VSS, selects the most vulnerable slots for insertion, and runs a targeted optimization attack at those slots. Our approach provides a position-search mechanism that is attack-agnostic and can be plugged into any optimization-based attack, adding only 200ms of preprocessing time. Experiments across multiple models demonstrate that SlotGCG significantly outperforms existing methods. Specifically, it achieves 14\% higher Attack Success Rates (ASR) over GCG-based attacks, converges faster, and shows superior robustness against defense methods with 42\% higher ASR than baseline approaches. Our implementation is available at \href{https://github.com/youai058/SlotGCG}{https://github.com/youai058/SlotGCG}

</details>


### [114] [Membrane: A Self-Evolving Contrastive Safety Memory for LLM Agent Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05743)
*Minseok Choi,Seungbin Yang,Dongjin Kim,Subin Kim,Jungmin Son,Yunseung Lee,Jaegul Choo,Youngjun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Membrane是一种自演进的护栏，通过对比安全记忆（CSM）将有害查询与其相似良性查询配对，在不重训练的情况下蒸馏交互经验，实现高精度的安全决策，在HarmBench和AgentHarm上取得最高F1分数，且良性拒绝率极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全分类器无法适应持续演变的越狱攻击，而基于记忆的护栏对与存储攻击相似的良性查询过度拒绝。需要一种既能适应新攻击又不牺牲良性查询通过率的自适应护栏。

Method: 提出Membrane，基于对比安全记忆（CSM），每个单元将阻止有害查询的条件与允许表面相似良性查询的条件配对。通过蒸馏有害交互及其良性对应物到对比单元中，按攻击策略索引，使单个单元泛化到同一机制的不同主题变体。推理时检索作为安全决策的上下文。

Result: 在HarmBench和AgentHarm上，Membrane在所有六种越狱攻击上取得最高F1。AgentHarm上良性拒绝率仅7-14%，远低于先前方法的28-85%。记忆单元在跨攻击迁移下保持87-88% F1，且对记忆投毒攻击保持稳定。

Conclusion: Membrane通过对比安全记忆机制，在不重训练的情况下实现自演进，有效平衡了对新攻击的适应性和对良性查询的容忍度，在模型级和智能体级安全任务上均表现优异。

Abstract: Despite advances in safety alignment, large language models remain vulnerable to continuously evolving jailbreaks. Existing fine-tuned safety classifiers cannot adapt to these evolving attacks, while adaptive memory-based guardrails tend to over-refuse benign queries that resemble stored attacks. We propose Membrane, a self-evolving guardrail built on Contrastive Safety Memory (CSM): each cell pairs the conditions for blocking a harmful query with those for permitting a superficially similar benign request. Without retraining, Membrane evolves CSM by distilling each harmful interaction and its benign counterpart into a contrastive cell indexed by the underlying attack strategy, so that one cell generalizes across topical variants of the same mechanism. At inference, retrieved cells serve as grounding context for precise safety decisions. Across model-level safety on HarmBench and agent-level safety on AgentHarm, Membrane achieves the highest F1 on all six jailbreak attacks. Notably, benign refusal on AgentHarm stays at 7-14%, well below the 28-85% range of prior guards. Memory cells also retain 87-88% F1 under cross-attack transfer and remain stable under memory poisoning.

</details>


### [115] [An Improved CNN-LSTM Based Intrusion Detection System for IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05776)
*Mohammad Tariq Ikhlas,Pohanyar Khowaja Khil,Malik Muhammad Mueed Aslam,Muhammad Khuram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于改进CNN-LSTM的物联网入侵检测模型，通过多分类、数据集集成和时间特征学习，在网络流量数据上实现了约97%的准确率，有效检测多种攻击类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备快速普及导致安全威胁急剧增加，现有入侵检测系统在捕捉网络流量的时空特征方面存在不足，亟需更高效的检测方法。

Method: 构建改进的CNN-LSTM混合模型，结合卷积神经网络（CNN）提取空间特征和循环神经网络（LSTM）学习时序特征，采用多分类策略与数据集集成方法进行训练和评估。

Result: 在物联网网络流量数据集上，模型达到约97%的准确率，在多种攻击类别的检测中表现良好，训练和验证性能稳定。

Conclusion: CNN与LSTM的集成有效捕获了网络流量的空间和时间特征，显著提升了物联网环境下的入侵检测能力，验证了混合深度学习模型在网络安全领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: With the rapid proliferation of IoT devices, security concerns have dramatically escalated and intrusion detection systems have become critical for protecting networked environments. This paper presents an improved CNN-LSTM based intrusion detection model that combines multi-class classification, dataset integration, and temporal feature learning to enhance detection performance in IoT networks. Using network traffic data, the proposed approach is evaluated on intrusion detection tasks and achieves an accuracy of approximately 97%. Experimental results demonstrate that the model effectively detects multiple attack categories while maintaining stable training and validation performance. The integration of convolutional and recurrent neural network components enables the framework to capture both spatial and temporal characteristics of network traffic, improving overall intrusion detection capability in IoT environments.

</details>


### [116] [TinyML-Driven Cybersecurity for Autonomous Spacecraft: Latency-Accuracy Analysis for SPARTA RF and Cyber Threat Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05779)
*Van Le,Trevor Tran,Tan Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于TinyML的轻量级机器学习模型（随机森林、逻辑回归、SVM、MLP）在航天器自主检测射频网络威胁（如上行干扰、欺骗、载荷操纵等）中的延迟-准确率权衡。结果表明，逻辑回归在微秒级推理速度下仅比随机森林低1%准确率，是适合星上自主处理的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主航天器需要快速、轻量、可靠的星上射频网络威胁检测能力，但现有方法在计算资源受限的星载环境下难以兼顾延迟与准确率。

Method: 基于SPARTA攻击模型，对四种TinyML兼容经典模型（随机森林、逻辑回归、SVM、MLP）进行物理信息驱动的理论分析（计算复杂度、VC维、Lipschitz连续性、延迟缩放），并通过对抗性RF频谱图（BandErasure、FakeNR、NoiseBurst三种攻击模式）进行实证测量验证。

Result: 逻辑回归在微秒级推理延迟下，准确率仅比随机森林低1%，成为最优TinyML基线；同时指出了通过特征编码器和多时间尺度学习架构进一步提升航天器网络安全的潜在方向。

Conclusion: 逻辑回归凭借极低延迟和可接受的准确率下降，适用于自主航天器的星上威胁检测；未来的研究应关注更丰富的特征表征和边缘智能、可信AI领域的最新进展。

Abstract: Autonomous spacecraft require rapid, lightweight, and reliable onboard detection of cyber-RF threats. Using the SPARTA attack model, we analyze the latency-accuracy trade-offs of TinyML-compatible classical models -- Random Forest, Logistic Regression, SVM, and MLP -- for detecting uplink jamming, Fake-NR spoofing, payload manipulation, ground-segment compromise, and unauthorized command injection. We present a physics-informed theoretical analysis of each model's computational complexity, VC dimension, Lipschitz continuity, and latency scaling, supported by empirical measurements on adversarial RF spectrograms generated via BandErasure, FakeNR, and NoiseBurst corruption modes. Results show that Logistic Regression achieves microsecond-level inference with only a 1\% accuracy drop relative to Random Forest, making it an effective TinyML baseline for onboard autonomy. The study also identifies opportunities for advancing spacecraft cybersecurity through richer feature encoders and multi-timescale learning architectures, building on recent progress in edge intelligence and trustworthy AI.

</details>


### [117] [SentinelRAG: Synthetic Sentinel Knowledge for RAG Database Copyright Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05787)
*Tsun On Kwok,Xi Yang,Ki Sen Hung,Chang Liu,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SentinelRAG提出了一种针对RAG数据库的防未授权分发的水印框架，通过注入风格一致但虚构的知识条目，仅0.1%注入率即可实现p<10^{-5}的统计显著检测，同时大幅降低误检率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG数据库水印方法存在两大缺陷：要么在真实实体间注入虚假关系，污染知识库并引入错误信息；要么嵌入脆弱的词汇模式，容易被对抗性改写移除。亟需一种既不影响合法查询质量又能可靠检测未授权重分发的水印方案。

Method: SentinelRAG框架向RAG数据库中注入风格一致但内容虚构的知识条目。核心洞见在于：这些描述虚构实体的合成知识不会被合法查询检索到，但可通过数据所有者已知的定向探针可靠触发，从而实现水印嵌入与检测。

Result: 在4个数据集（2.9k至8.8M文档）上的实验表明，仅需0.1%的注入率，所有配置下均实现p<10^{-5}的统计显著检测。相比现有最优方法，误检率显著降低，且对合法用户查询的干扰可忽略不计。

Conclusion: SentinelRAG有效解决了RAG数据库水印中知识污染与鲁棒性之间的权衡，为保护专有RAG数据库知识产权提供了一种实用且可靠的方案。

Abstract: Protecting proprietary RAG databases from unauthorized redistribution is challenging: existing watermarking methods either inject fabricated relations between real entities, polluting the knowledge base with misinformation, or embed fragile lexical patterns that adversarial paraphrasing easily removes. We propose SentinelRAG, a watermarking framework that embeds style-consistent but fictitious knowledge entries into the RAG database. Our key insight is that synthetic knowledge describing fictitious entities is unlikely to be retrieved by legitimate queries, yet can be reliably triggered through targeted probes known only to the data owner. Experiments on four datasets ranging from 2.9k to 8.8M documents demonstrate that SentinelRAG achieves statistically significant detection $p < 10^{-5}$ across all tested configurations at only a 0.1% injection rate. Compared to the state-of-the-art, our method significantly reduces the false detection rate while maintaining negligible interference with legitimate user queries.

</details>


### [118] [GCD: Garbled, Corrected, Demonstrandum -- Fixing and Proving Go's Extended GCD Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05796)
*Linard Arquint*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文验证了Go标准库中'extendedGCD'实现，发现其与BoringSSL的两个偏差并进行了修复和证明。修复后平均加速24%，并使用Gobra和Lean完成了形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Go标准库中的extendedGCD实现虽声称直接移植自BoringSSL，但可能存在未被发现的实现偏差，这些偏差会破坏算法的关键不变量，进而影响RSA密钥对生成的安全性和正确性。

Method: 1) 对比分析Go实现与BoringSSL实现的差异；2) 修正Go实现的系数更新方式和输入域限制；3) 使用Gobra（Go的演绎程序验证器）进行正确性和终止性证明；4) 对于非线性算术的关键引理，使用Lean辅助证明并导入Gobra。

Result: 发现两个偏差：系数更新方式不同以及允许更大的输入域。修复后实现平均加速24%。成功使用Gobra和Lean完成了修正后实现的形式化验证。

Conclusion: 即使是经过严格审查的代码也可能存在细微错误；形式化验证是发现这类错误的有力工具；AI智能体可以通过迭代优化不变量和引理来辅助验证过程。

Abstract: We verify the 'extendedGCD' implementation in Go's standard library ('crypto/internal/fips140/bigmod'), which plays a crucial role in the generation of RSA key pairs. Even though the Go implementation is supposedly a direct port from BoringSSL's implementation, we uncovered two deviations that each break the algorithm's invariants: (1) the Go implementation deviates in the way coefficients are updated, and (2) it permits a larger input domain. We address both deviations; the first by fixing the Go implementation, which results in an on average 24% speedup, and the second deviation by porting an existing proof for BoringSSL and extending it to cover the larger input domain. We prove correctness and termination of the fixed Go implementation using Gobra, a deductive program verifier for Go. Where necessary, we used Lean to prove key lemmata on non-linear arithmetic, which we import into Gobra.
  Our verification effort reveals three key insights: subtle bugs can slip into even well-reviewed code with surprising ease; formal verification is a powerful tool for uncovering them; and AI agents can facilitate the verification process by iteratively refining invariants and lemmata based on Gobra's error messages.

</details>


### [119] [PriSrv: Privacy-Enhanced and Highly Usable Service Discovery in Wireless Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05821)
*Yang Yang,Robert H. Deng,Guomin Yang,Yingjiu Li,HweeHwa Pang,Minming Huang,Rui Shi,Jian Weng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PriSrv，一种基于匿名凭证匹配加密（ACME）的双层隐私保护服务发现协议，在主流移动设备上可实现亚秒级安全发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务发现协议隐私保护有限，会泄露敏感设备信息并招致网络攻击。

Method: 设计双层匹配架构（外层公共属性过滤、内层私有属性认证），核心引入匿名凭证匹配加密（ACME）原语及快速匿名凭证（FAC）方案，实现单步双层匹配、双边策略控制、选择性属性披露和多次展示不可关联性。

Result: 集成EAP、mDNS、BLE、AirDrop等框架；形式化安全证明证明隐私保障；在桌面、笔记本、智能手机、树莓派上评估，主流移动设备上发现时间<1秒。

Conclusion: PriSrv在增强隐私保障的同时保持高可用性，适用于实际无线通信场景。

Abstract: Service discovery is essential in wireless communications. However, existing protocols provide limited privacy protection, leaking sensitive device information and opening routes to network attacks. This paper proposes a private service discovery protocol, called PriSrv, which enables both service providers and clients to specify fine-grained authentication policies before establishing connections. PriSrv achieves this via a dual-layer matching architecture: an outer layer filters mismatched entities using public attributes, while an inner layer handles mutual authentication using selectively disclosed private attributes. As a core component, we introduce the primitive of anonymous credential-based matchmaking encryption (ACME), which enables dual-layer matching in a single step to achieve bilateral policy control, selective attribute disclosure, and multi-show unlinkability. To instantiate ACME, we design a fast anonymous credential (FAC) scheme providing constant-size credentials and efficient verification. We demonstrate PriSrv's interoperability by integrating it with popular wireless frameworks including EAP, mDNS, BLE, and AirDrop. Detailed formal security proofs and extensive performance evaluations across desktop, laptop, smartphone, and Raspberry Pi platforms demonstrate that PriSrv provides enhanced privacy guarantees with high usability, achieving secure discovery in less than one second on mainstream mobile devices.

</details>


### [120] [Towards Worst-case Hardness for Low-Noise LPN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05834)
*Divesh Aggarwal,Rishav Gupta,Hai Hoang Nguyen,Kel Zin Tan,Prashant Nalini Vasudevan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的最坏情况到平均情况的归约方法，用于证明LPN问题的平均情况困难性。与现有依赖统计平滑的方法不同，该方法仅要求计算不可区分性，从而获得了适用于公钥加密的噪声率参数（逆多项式噪声率n^{-α}，α<1），包括Alekhnovich公钥加密方案所需的参数区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LPN问题是密码学的基础假设，但其平均情况困难性能否基于最坏情况复杂性假设仍是一个开放问题。现有归约方法[BLVW19, YZ21]依赖线性码的统计平滑，导致平均情况噪声率被限制在1/2 - 1/poly(n)，不足以支持公钥加密应用。

Method: 提出了一种新的归约框架：不要求生成矩阵的随机稀疏行组合在统计上接近均匀分布，而仅要求其在计算上不可区分。通过构造一个win-win结构，证明任何高效的LPN求解器都可以转化为一对高效算法(S, D)，使得对于任意矩阵A，要么S能从随机噪声中解码A生成的码，要么D能区分对偶码的随机噪声码字与均匀分布。

Result: 获得了逆多项式噪声率n^{-α}（任意常数α<1）的LPN平均情况困难性，基于最坏情况下同时解码码字和区分对偶码码字的困难性。特别地，当α=1/2时，归约得到了Alekhnovich公钥加密方案所需的参数区间，这是此前最坏情况归约无法达到的。

Conclusion: 通过放松统计接近要求为计算不可区分性，成功克服了现有归约方法在噪声率上的根本限制，为基于最坏情况假设的LPN平均情况困难性提供了新的归约途径，并首次使公钥加密所需的LPN参数区间可通过最坏情况归约得到。

Abstract: The hardness of the Learning Parity with Noise (LPN) problem is a foundational assumption in cryptography, forming the basis of constructions ranging from symmetric-key primitives to public-key encryption and beyond. A central open question is whether the average-case hardness of LPN can be based on worst-case complexity assumptions, as has been achieved for the analogous Learning With Errors (LWE) problem.
  Existing worst-case-to-average-case reductions for LPN [BLVW19, YZ21] rely on statistical smoothing of linear codes, which inherently limits the resulting average-case hardness to noise rates as large as $1/2 - 1/\mathrm{poly}(n)$, which is insufficient for public-key applications.
  We explore a new approach towards obtaining such reductions: rather than requiring that random sparse combinations of the rows of the generator matrix of a code be statistically close to uniform, we only require that they be computationally indistinguishable from uniform. This leads to a clean win-win structure: we show that any efficient LPN solver can be transformed into a pair of efficient algorithms $(S, D)$ such that for every matrix $A$ of appropriate dimensions over $\mathbb{F}_2$, either $S$ decodes the code generated by $A$ from random noise, or $D$ distinguishes random noisy codewords of the dual of this code from uniform.
  By instantiating this reduction with appropriate parameters, we obtain the average-case hardness of LPN with inverse-polynomial noise rate $n^{-α}$ for any constant $α< 1$, assuming the worst-case simultaneous hardness of decoding a code from random noise and distinguishing random noisy codewords of its dual from uniform. In particular, setting $α= 1/2$, our reduction yields LPN hardness in the parameter regime required for Alekhnovich's construction of public-key encryption [Ale03], a regime that was previously inaccessible via worst-case reductions.

</details>


### [121] [GenTI: Benchmarking LLMs for Autonomous IDPS Rule Generation for Unseen Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05844)
*Hassan Jalil Hadi,Rehana Yasmin,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GenTI框架，一个基于LLM的IDPS规则自动生成基准系统。该框架包含GTI数据集（超过20万条检测与防御规则，涵盖Snort、Suricata、Emerging Threats、YARA等格式，并附有协议行为、载荷签名、上下文关系、CTI映射及响应类型标注），以及基于LLM的流水线（通过结构化提示工程、思维链推理和链式验证循环，将分析人员提示和代表性载荷转化为可部署规则）。实验表明，GenTI在规则质量评分达89.4%，CTI覆盖率达94.8%，未知攻击检测率从45%提升至87.4%，误报率从8.5%降至2.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于规则的入侵检测与防御系统（IDPS）依赖人工编写的签名规则，难以适应新兴和零日威胁；同时，现有公共数据集（如CICIDS2017、UNSW-NB15）主要关注流量分类，缺乏支持自动规则合成或防御逻辑的结构化信息。

Method: 构建GTI数据集（包含150K+ Snort/Suricata规则、50K YARA规则，每项标注协议行为、载荷签名、上下文关系、CTI映射及响应类型）；设计LLM流水线，通过结构化提示工程、Chain-of-Thought推理和Chain-of-Verification循环，将分析人员提示和代表性载荷转化为可部署规则。

Result: 规则质量评分89.4%，CTI覆盖率94.8%，未知攻击检测率从45%提升至87.4%，误报率从8.5%降至2.3%。

Conclusion: GenTI建立了首个将规则级CTI与LLM自动化紧密结合的大规模基准，能够实现自适应、自演进的IDPS。

Abstract: Rule-based Intrusion Detection and Prevention Systems (IDPS) offer precise attack detection as well as mitigation, however their manually crafted, signature-driven rules limit adaptability to emerging and zero-day threats. Additionally, existing public datasets (e.g., CICIDS2017, UNSW-NB15) focus on traffic classification and provide little structured information to support automatic rule synthesis or prevention logic. To address this gap, we propose Generative Thread Intelligence (GenTI) \footnote{GenTI refers to the proposed framework, and GTI refers to the dataset.} an LLM-driven benchmark for automatic generation of IDPS rules targeting unseen attacks. The dataset (GTI) aggregates over 150k detection and prevention rules from Snort, Suricata, Emerging Threats, as well as 50k YARA, each annotated with protocol behavior, payload signatures, contextual relationships, mappings to Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI), along with actionable response types (alert, drop, reject). Moreover, on top of this corpus we design an LLM-based pipeline that transforms analyst prompts and representative payloads into deployable rules via structured prompt engineering, Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, as well as a Chain-of-Verification (CoVe) loop for syntactic, semantic, and security validation. The generated rules are executed in real time on (Snort/Suricata) and evaluated by syntax accuracy, semantic similarity, CTI coverage, security effectiveness as well as unseen attacks detection. Furthermore, our GenTI instantiation achieves a composite rule-quality score of 89.4\%, with 94.8\% CTI coverage, improving unseen attacks detection from 45\% to 87.4\% and reducing the false-positive rate from 8.5\% to 2.3\%. Overall, GenTI establishes the first large-scale benchmark that tightly couples rule-level CTI with LLM-based automation, enabling adaptive, self-evolving IDPS.

</details>


### [122] [PriSrv+: Privacy and Usability-Enhanced Wireless Service Discovery with Fast and Expressive Matchmaking Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05902)
*Yang Yang,Guomin Yang,Yingjiu Li,Pengfei Wu,Rui Shi,Minming Huang,Jian Weng,HweeHwa Pang,Robert H. Deng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PriSrv+ 是一个面向现代无线网络和资源受限环境的隐私增强型服务发现协议，通过提出首个支持无界属性宇宙和表达性访问控制的 Fast and Expressive Matchmaking Encryption (FEME) 方案，在隐私性、表达能力、可扩展性和效率方面显著优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务发现协议（包括 PriSrv）在表达能力、隐私保护、可扩展性和效率方面存在关键局限性，无法同时满足现代无线网络（如5G、IoT）和资源受限环境对灵活访问控制和高效加密的需求。

Method: 提出 Fast and Expressive Matchmaking Encryption (FEME)，这是首个支持无界属性宇宙的表达性访问控制匹配加密方案，允许任意字符串作为属性；在此基础上构建 PriSrv+ 协议，兼容 mDNS、BLE、Wi-Fi 等标准无线协议。

Result: 相比 PriSrv，加密速度提升 7.62 倍，解密速度提升 6.23 倍；密文大小降低 87.33%；服务广播通信成本降低 87.33%；匿名互认证通信成本降低 86.64%。

Conclusion: PriSrv+ 通过 FEME 方案在安全性、性能、可扩展性和效率上均优于现有协议，形式化安全证明确认了其安全性，多平台评估验证了其在实际场景中的优越性。

Abstract: Service discovery is a fundamental process in wireless networks, enabling devices to find and communicate with services dynamically, and is critical for the seamless operation of modern systems like 5G and IoT. This paper introduces PriSrv+, an advanced privacy and usability-enhanced service discovery protocol for modern wireless networks and resource-constrained environments. PriSrv+ builds upon PriSrv (NDSS'24), by addressing critical limitations in expressiveness, privacy, scalability, and efficiency, while maintaining compatibility with widely-used wireless protocols such as mDNS, BLE, and Wi-Fi.
  A key innovation in PriSrv+ is the development of Fast and Expressive Matchmaking Encryption (FEME), the first matchmaking encryption scheme capable of supporting expressive access control policies with an unbounded attribute universe, allowing any arbitrary string to be used as an attribute. FEME significantly enhances the flexibility of service discovery while ensuring robust message and attribute privacy. Compared to PriSrv, PriSrv+ optimizes cryptographic operations, achieving 7.62* faster for encryption and 6.23* faster for decryption, and dramatically reduces ciphertext sizes by 87.33%. In addition, PriSrv+ reduces communication costs by 87.33% for service broadcast and 86.64% for anonymous mutual authentication compared with PriSrv. Formal security proofs confirm the security of FEME and PriSrv+. Extensive evaluations on multiple platforms demonstrate that PriSrv+ achieves superior performance, scalability, and efficiency compared to existing state-of-the-art protocols.

</details>


### [123] [Exploring the connection between coding habits and cognitive styles in malware developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05945)
*Vasilis Vouvoutsis,Constantinos Patsakis,Fran Casino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模分析泄露的恶意软件源代码，结合静态应用安全测试和软件度量，从认知心理学和犯罪学视角揭示了恶意软件开发者与良性软件开发者之间存在显著的编码行为差异：恶意软件代码通常更小、文档更少、圈复杂度更高、抽象机制使用更少，且安全漏洞更多，反映其追求便捷性、隐蔽性和规避性的开发策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恶意软件研究主要关注恶意软件的结果、方法和影响，即使从攻击安全视角也仅研究方法而非攻击者的开发策略。本文旨在从行为特征和编码模式角度，通过量化分析恶意软件源码，揭示恶意软件开发者的动机结构、风险容忍度和开发策略。

Method: 收集大规模泄露的恶意软件源码库与精心挑选的良性开源软件进行比较，应用静态应用安全测试（SAST）并计算多种软件度量指标，基于认知心理学和犯罪学理论将代码结构和质量差异解释为行为指标。

Result: 恶意软件代码通常更小、文档更少、每函数圈复杂度更高，类/闭包等抽象机制使用更少；漏洞分析显示恶意软件存在更多良性代码通常避免的问题类型，表明其在安全开发实践上投入极少；但代码质量度量显示其与良性软件尚未达到足够显著的可区分程度。

Conclusion: 恶意软件编码模式反映了一种追求便捷性、操作隐蔽性和规避性的开发风格，而非长期可维护性；将代码度量作为行为信号和策略选择的代理变量，展示了量化软件分析如何丰富行为网络安全研究，为网络犯罪者的行为画像研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Malware research primarily studies the results, the methods, and the impact. Even from an offensive security perspective, what is examined is the method, not the development strategy of the offender. This study investigates the behavioral signatures and coding patterns embedded in the malware source code. By analyzing a large corpus of leaked malware code and comparing it with carefully selected benign open-source software, we apply static application security testing and compute multiple software metrics. Based on cognitive psychology and criminological theories, our work interprets differences in code structure and quality as behavioral indicators, reflecting distinct motivational structures, risk tolerances, and development strategies of malware authors compared to benign software developers. Our findings reveal that malware code is generally smaller, less documented, and exhibits higher cyclomatic complexity per function, with reduced use of abstraction mechanisms such as classes and closures. Vulnerability analysis further reveals that malware exhibits more issues of the types that benign code typically avoids, suggesting a minimal investment in secure development practices. These patterns imply a development style optimized for expedience, operational secrecy, and evasion rather than long-term maintainability. Nonetheless, the code quality metrics indicate that it does not deviate significantly from benign software enough to be distinctive. By framing code metrics as proxies for behavioral signals and strategic choices, we demonstrate how quantitative software analysis can enrich behavioral cybersecurity research, offering new insights into the practices and priorities of malware developers. Our results pave the way for further research in the behavioral profiling of cyber offenders.

</details>


### [124] [AttackPathGNN: Cross-function vulnerability detection in smart contracts using state interference graphs and conjunction pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05986)
*Gabriela Dobrita,Simona-Vasilica Oprea,Adela Bara*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AttackPathGNN是一种基于图神经网络的Solidity智能合约漏洞检测方法，通过构建状态干扰图和合取池化机制，将漏洞检测重构为显式攻击路径推理，在多个基准测试上达到92.3% F1值，并能生成结构化修复报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的智能合约检测器将漏洞检测简化为单个函数内的语法模式匹配，但大量重大漏洞（如The DAO、Cream Finance）并不存在于单个函数中，而是源自函数间的关系和多重条件的组合。因此需要一种能推理函数间攻击路径的检测方法。

Method: 提出AttackPathGNN图神经网络，包含两个核心设计：(1)状态干扰图——连接共享可变存储的每对函数，使用带类型和权重的边以及由五条件谓词定义的有向重入路径边；(2)合取池化——一种可微分的AND聚合器，对八个命名的利用前提条件进行聚合，采用log-sigmoid形式使得当任一缓解措施（重入锁、访问控制修饰符或SafeMath）存在时，单个函数的利用得分会骤降。

Result: 在五次独立训练中，AttackPathGNN在SmartBugs Wild测试集上达到92.3±0.2% F1（假阴性率4.3±0.3%），在独立人工标注的SmartBugs Curated基准上检测率90.8±2.5%，在DASP10的6个类别上达到100%召回率，重入检测率达98.7±1.8%。

Conclusion: AttackPathGNN通过显式建模函数间的攻击路径而非单函数模式匹配，显著提升了智能合约漏洞检测的效果，且每个预测附带结构化修复报告，将检测结果转化为可操作的函数级审计发现。

Abstract: Existing learning-based detectors for Solidity smart-contracts reduce vulnerability detection to syntactic pattern matching within single functions, yet many of the most consequential exploits (The DAO, Cream Finance) exist not in any individual function but in the relationship between functions and in the combination of conditions that made the attack feasible. Thus, we propose AttackPathGNN, a graph neural network (GNN) that reframes detection as reasoning over explicit attack paths. Two architectural choices distinguish it from prior GNN-based detectors: (1)a State Interference Graph that links every pair of functions sharing mutable storage through typed, weighted edges and through directed reentrancy-path edges defined by an explicit five-condition predicate; (2)conjunction pooling, a differentiable AND-aggregator over eight named exploit preconditions whose log-sigmoid form causes the per-function exploit score to collapse whenever any single mitigation (a reentrancy guard, an access-control modifier or SafeMath) is in place. Across five independent training runs, AttackPathGNN attains 92.3+/-0.2% F1 on the SmartBugs Wild held-out test partition (4.3+/-0.3% false-negative rate, 90.8+/-2.5% detection rate on the independently human-labelled SmartBugs Curated benchmark), recovering 6/10 DASP10 categories at 100% on every seed and Reentrancy at 98.7+/-1.8%. Each prediction is emitted with a structured remediation report, turning each verdict into an actionable, function-level audit finding.

</details>


### [125] [Cheating in Multiplayer Online Games: a Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06013)
*Hugo Bertin,Marc Dacier,Yérom-David Bromberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个包含网络流干扰作弊（如网络延迟操纵）以及常见作弊（如自瞄、透视）的多人在线游戏数据集，该数据集通过结合游戏框架和插件收集真实玩家与自动客户端的网络层和应用层日志，为解决作弊检测中缺乏代表性标注数据的问题提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作弊行为严重破坏多人在线游戏的公平性和玩家满意度，尤其是基于网络流干扰的作弊难以检测和预防，而现有研究缺乏包含此类作弊的代表性标注数据集。

Method: 利用一个实验框架，将多人在线游戏与可复现作弊攻击并收集网络层和应用层日志的插件相结合，记录真实玩家和自动化游戏客户端在游戏会话中的作弊行为。

Result: 构建了一个包含网络流干扰作弊、自瞄、透视等多种作弊类型的数据集，该数据集具有可扩展性，并提供了公开的实验框架供他人补充数据。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了网络流干扰作弊检测研究中缺乏标注数据的空白，可为学术界和工业界开发在线游戏作弊检测机制提供支持，且框架支持社区持续扩展数据。

Abstract: Cheating poses a significant threat to the Multiplayer Online Games (MOG) industry by degrading player satisfaction and undermining the fairness in competitive gaming. Despite efforts to develop mitigation techniques, cheating remains difficult to detect and prevent in practice. In particular, a class of cheats based on network flow disruption remains unsolvable. To find out how to detect such attacks we need access to representative labelled data. However, no such dataset exists.
  To address this gap, we leverage an experimental framework that combines a multiplayer online game with a plug-in capable of both reproducing cheating attacks and collecting logs at two levels: network and application-layer.
  This paper presents a dataset compiling records of game sessions played by both real players and automated game clients, with cheating actions explicitly logged. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first dataset that provides logs of network flow disruption cheats. While it includes such network-based cheats, it is not limited to them and also contains records of more commonly studied cheats, such as aimbots and wallhacks. This dataset can be used by researchers in academia and industry seeking to develop cheating detection mechanisms for online games. Furthermore, it is designed to be evolutive and can be enriched by others creating their own data traces with the proposed framework.

</details>


### [126] [RedEdit: Agentic Red-Teaming of Image Safety Classifiers via MCTS-Guided Photo-Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06140)
*Weilin Lin,Ziqi Lin,Zhenxing Zhou,Jianze Li,Tong Zhang,Hui Xiong,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RedEdit，一种基于黑盒红队攻击的视觉语言模型与蒙特卡洛树搜索结合的框架，通过平均不到两次的图片编辑即可使76.2%的不安全图片逃避检测器，同时保留93.0%恶意语义，揭示了当前内容审核系统在面对用户风格恶意编辑时的严重脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前图像安全分类器对用户风格的恶意图片编辑（如日常场景中常见但难以完全复现的编辑行为）的鲁棒性尚未被充分探索，因此需要系统性地研究这一被忽视的实际威胁。

Method: 将照片编辑逃逸建模为编辑工具序列上的组合搜索问题，采用基于VLM的提议器生成语义定向的候选编辑，结合蒙特卡洛树搜索规划器优先选择有前景的编辑路径并对无效路径进行回溯，模仿人类攻击者的领域知识和迭代回溯能力。

Result: 在UnsafeBench数据集上，平均不到两次编辑即可使76.2%的不安全图像逃避检测器，同时保留93.0%的恶意语义，即编辑后的内容对人类仍具有感知上的恶意性但能轻易绕过自动化审核。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区更多关注这一被忽视的实际威胁，因为现有的内容审核系统在面对用户风格恶意编辑时存在严重的系统性漏洞。

Abstract: Image safety classifiers serve as a critical component of contemporary content moderation systems on the internet. However, their resilience against user-style malicious image editing remains underexplored. Such behaviors are highly prevalent in daily scenarios but difficult to fully reproduce. To explore this vulnerability, we introduce RedEdit, a novel black-box red-teaming agent that formulates photo-editing evasion as a combinatorial search problem over edit-tool sequences. It adopts a Vision-Language-Model (VLM)-based proposer to generate semantically targeted candidate edits and a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) planner to prioritize promising edit paths while backtracking from ineffective ones. Together, the proposer and planner instantiate two key capabilities of human attackers, i.e., domain knowledge and iterative backtracking, respectively, to reproduce this practical threat. Our extensive experiments on UnsafeBench reveal profound systemic vulnerabilities: fewer than two edits on average enable 76.2% of unsafe images to evade detectors, while retaining 93.0% malicious semantics, meaning that such manipulated content remains perceptually malicious to humans while easily bypassing automated moderation. We therefore appeal to the community for more attention to this overlooked practical threat.

</details>


### [127] [Opportunities and Challenges in Securely Reusing and Repurposing Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06181)
*Adelin Roty,Jan Tobias Mühlberg,Jean-François Determe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能手机在脱离原始生态系统被重新利用时，其硬件安全机制（启动链完整性、可信执行环境隔离、硬件绑定秘密保护）是否仍然有效。通过在PinePhone上的实验，揭示了重建信任锚点的复杂性，并指出供应商锁定机制阻碍了大多数废弃设备的安全再利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2022年约有53亿部手机成为电子垃圾，许多设备本可通过重新利用延长寿命、减少生态影响，但智能手机中依赖供应商控制预配的硬件安全机制在设备离开原始生态系统后的有效性尚不明确，这一被忽视的网络安全问题亟待研究。

Method: 以开源硬件智能手机PinePhone为实验平台，聚焦启动链完整性、可信执行环境隔离、硬件绑定秘密保护三个核心安全方面，通过模拟现实的重新利用场景进行实验分析。

Result: 实验揭示了在重新利用场景下重建信任锚点的复杂性，证明了供应商锁定机制使得大多数废弃设备无法被安全地重新利用。

Conclusion: 供应商锁定的安全机制阻碍了设备的重新利用，需要重新设计安全架构以支持设备的安全重用，从而减少电子垃圾并降低生态影响。

Abstract: An estimated 5.3 billion mobile phones became electronic waste in 2022. Many of these devices can be repurposed and used in different contexts to extend their lifetime and to reduce ecological impacts. An often overlooked aspect of smartphone reuse is cybersecurity: these devices embed hardware-backed security mechanisms that rely on vendor-controlled provisioning and are designed for a fixed device lifecycle. In this paper, we investigate whether security mechanisms and guarantees remain effective when devices are repurposed outside their original ecosystem. We explore security features in a PinePhone, an open-hardware smartphone, and focus on three core security aspects: boot chain integrity, isolation provided by the Trusted Execution Environment, and the protection of hardware-bound secrets. Our experiments simulate realistic repurposing scenarios and highlight the complexity of reconstructing trust anchors. We generalize our observations to infer requirements for secure repurposing and illustrate how vendor locked mechanisms hinder the repurposing of a majority of discarded devices.

</details>


### [128] [Steering LLM Viewpoints through Fabricated Evidence Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06244)
*Xi Yang,Chang Liu,Zhenglin Huang,Haoran Li,Weiming Zhang,Jian Weng,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Ghostwriter攻击框架，通过伪装成可信来源的虚假证据诱导LLM产生误导性输出，实验表明商业LLM对此高度脆弱，即使GPT-5.4等前沿模型也无法完全防御，而定制安全策略可将检测率提升至81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聊天机器人日益影响日常决策，但其对带有可信标志的虚假证据存在认知脆弱性，可能产生严重误导用户的响应。

Method: 提出Ghostwriter两阶段攻击框架：第一阶段将误导性陈述与伪造理由打包成可信证据；第二阶段指示目标LLM在回答相关查询时采纳这些观点。

Result: 商业LLM在没有外部安全分类器时高度脆弱；GPT-5.4等前沿分类器防护模型虽能降低但无法完全消除攻击；定制安全策略使gpt-oss-safeguard实现81%检测率。

Conclusion: 现有LLM对上下文中的虚假可信证据存在显著的认知脆弱性，需要更有效的防御机制来保障用户安全。

Abstract: As chatbots increasingly influence daily decision-making, their potential to produce misleading responses poses substantial risks to users. This paper investigates a critical cognitive vulnerability in LLMs: their tendency to uncritically trust external context when presented with fabricated evidence bearing markers of credibility. We introduce Ghostwriter, a two-phase attack framework that first repackages misleading statements with fabricated rationales, then instruct target LLMs to incorporate these viewpoints when responding to relevant queries. Experiments on BBQ, ToxiGen, and our specialized dataset reveal that commercial LLMs without external safety classifiers remain highly vulnerable, while even frontier classifier-guarded models (e.g., GPT-5.4) reduce but do not eliminate the attack. Building on this, we explore multiple defense strategies, among which a tailored safety policy enables gpt-oss-safeguard to achieve 81% detection rate.

</details>


### [129] [SecRL-Prune: Structured Reinforcement Learning-Based Pruning of CodeLLMs for Preserving Adversarial Code Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06254)
*Parsa Memarzadehsaghezi,Pooria Madani,Khalil El-Khatib*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SecRL-Prune框架，通过强化学习对CodeLLM的MLP/FFN通道进行结构化剪枝，发现剪枝后的轻量模型仍能保留代码变异能力，可生成恶意软件变体逃避检测，表明压缩CodeLLM具有安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究CodeLLM在经过模型压缩（结构化剪枝）后，其功能保持的代码变异能力是否仍然存在，以及这种能力是否足以生成多样化的恶意软件变体以逃避基于签名的检测，从而评估压缩CodeLLM的安全风险。

Method: 提出SecRL-Prune框架，基于强化学习学习逐层剪枝策略，使用教师-学生KL散度作为奖励函数。为提高效率，缓存教师模型top-P预测结果作为紧凑目标，避免同时加载教师和学生模型。在7B规模CodeLLM上以10-30%压缩率进行评估。

Result: 在HumanEval上，SecRL-Prune在pass@k（执行正确性）和var@k（代码多样性）上均优于现有结构化剪枝基线。在真实恶意软件案例中，20%剪枝模型的语义保持变异显著降低了检测率。

Conclusion: 代码变异能力可以在显著结构化剪枝后幸存，证明压缩后的CodeLLM具有安全相关性，可能被用于生成恶意软件变体以规避检测。

Abstract: Large code language models (CodeLLMs) can generate and rewrite programs, enabling functionality-preserving code mutation that may be used to create diverse malware variants and evade signature-based detection. A key security question is whether this mutation capability survives model compression, which would make deployment feasible under limited hardware budgets. We propose SecRL-Prune, a structured pruning framework for CodeLLMs that operates on feed-forward (MLP/FFN) channels. Starting from a pretrained teacher, it learns a layer-wise pruning policy with reinforcement learning using a teacher-student KL-divergence reward. To improve efficiency, we cache the teacher's top-P predictions once and compare the pruned student against this compact target, avoiding simultaneous teacher-student residency in GPU memory. We evaluate SecRL-Prune on HumanEval using pass@k for execution correctness and var@k for code diversity across three 7B CodeLLMs at 10-30% compression. SecRL-Prune consistently preserves higher pass@k and var@k than recent structured pruning baselines under aggressive pruning. In a case study on real malware samples, semantics-preserving mutations from 20%-pruned models substantially reduced detections. These results show that code mutation capability can survive significant structured pruning, highlighting the security relevance of compressed CodeLLMs.

</details>


### [130] [Robust Ensemble of Selectively Strengthened and Augmented Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06265)
*Parsa Memarzadehsaghezi,Zahra Hashemi,Pooria Madani,Mehran Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出RESSAP框架，通过将单一分类器转化为鲁棒集成分类器（基于特征选择、随机子集推理和噪声数据增强），显著提升对逃避攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持干净数据上的高准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对ML分类器的防御机制泛化能力有限，无法在不同攻击场景下系统性地提升模型鲁棒性，亟需一种通用且可扩展的防御策略。

Method: RESSAP框架：1) 基于韧性度量（综合考虑特征重要性和鲁棒性）进行特征选择，训练多个分类器组成集成；2) 推理时随机选取子集分类器进行预测，增加不可预测性；3) 训练中引入噪声数据增强以强化决策边界。

Result: 在保持干净数据高准确率的同时，显著提升了对对抗性逃避攻击的鲁棒性，且框架具有模型无关性。

Conclusion: RESSAP是一种可扩展、灵活的模型无关防御框架，无需重大架构修改即可增强ML系统的安全性。

Abstract: Evasion attacks present a significant challenge to the robustness of machine learning (ML)-based classifiers, particularly in critical applications such as fraud detection and cybersecurity. Although existing defense mechanisms are effective in some settings, they often suffer from limited generalizability and do not systematically improve model robustness across diverse attack scenarios. To address these limitations, we introduce Robust Ensemble of Selectively Strengthened and Augmented Predictors (RESSAP), a novel framework that transforms a single classifier into an ensemble of robust classifiers. Each classifier in the ensemble is trained on a carefully selected subset of features, where feature selection is guided by a resilience metric that accounts for both feature importance and robustness. During inference, a random subset of these classifiers is used to make predictions, increasing unpredictability and improving resistance to adversarial manipulation. In addition, noise-based data augmentation is applied during training to strengthen decision boundaries and improve generalization. Our experimental results demonstrate that RESSAP significantly improves robustness against adversarial evasion attacks while maintaining strong accuracy on clean data. Overall, this model-agnostic framework provides a scalable and flexible defense strategy for enhancing the security of machine learning systems without requiring major changes to existing architectures.

</details>


### [131] [Credential Disclosure in (EU) Digital Identity Wallets: Privacy Risks and Practical Mitigations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06354)
*Sheila Zingg,Daniele Lain,Yoshimichi Nakatsuka,Kari Kostiainen,Stefan Bechtold,Srdjan Čapkun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了欧盟EUDI钱包中用户凭证披露决策问题，发现用户存在过度分享行为（如约20%向新闻网站泄露官方ID），并提出了能够将用户凭证披露错误率从约15%降至约7%的凭证助手，但无法完全消除错误决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EUDI钱包允许用户持有数字凭证并在网站上进行安全隐私的属性披露，但用户可能做出糟糕的凭证披露决策，导致隐私泄露、身份盗窃等风险，因此需要研究用户的披露行为并探索干预措施。

Method: 开展了大规模用户与专家的凭证披露调查，并评估了显示专家建议和用户意见的凭证助手（Credential Assistant）的有效性和可行性。

Result: 约20%的用户向新闻网站披露了官方ID，表明用户难以保护隐私；凭证助手将用户凭证披露错误从约15%显著降低至约7%。

Conclusion: 凭证助手能有效减少但无法完全消除不良凭证披露决策，对敏感属性可能需要更强的干预措施。

Abstract: The European Union will introduce the EUDI Wallet by late 2026, which allows users to hold digital credentials (i.e., representations of physical official identity documents) on their devices. This will allow users to securely and privately disclose identity attributes to websites. Although such a system has many benefits, it also introduces risks caused by poor credential disclosure decisions. In this paper, we (i) conduct a large-scale survey on credential disclosure with users and experts and (ii) evaluate the effectiveness and feasibility of our Credential Assistant that displays expert recommendations and user opinions. Our results show that users are likely to overshare (e.g., ~20% of users disclosed their official ID to news websites). This indicates that users struggle to protect their privacy, which will impact the usability of the EUDI Wallet and lead to privacy violations, identity theft, and other abuses of leaked credentials. Finally, we show that our Credential Assistant significantly reduces users' credential disclosure mistakes from ~15% to ~7%. However, it does not fully eliminate poor credential disclosure decisions, indicating that stronger interventions may be necessary, especially for sensitive attributes.

</details>


### [132] [WebMCP Tool Surface Poisoning: Runtime Manipulation Attacks on LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06387)
*Lin-Fa Lee,Yi-Yu Chang,Chia-Mu Yu,Kuo-Hui Yeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现WebMCP协议存在一种名为"会话中工具注入(MSTI)"的新安全威胁，攻击者可利用第三方脚本在活跃会话中注入恶意工具。MSTI分为工具劫持(Tool Hijacking)和工具框架(Tool Framing)两类，前者通过AbortSignal API或竞态条件修改工具集合，后者通过元数据字段操控AI代理对工具角色的认知。实验证实两种攻击均能破坏WebMCP的预期功能，并提出了绑定工具身份与来源、确保生命周期一致性等缓解建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebMCP协议允许网站直接向AI代理暴露工具，绕过了传统用户界面，引入了新的安全风险。第三方脚本的参与进一步扩大了攻击面，因此需要系统性地识别和防御这类新型威胁。

Method: 对MSTI威胁进行分类，区分工具劫持（利用AbortSignal API或工具注册竞态条件修改工具集）和工具框架（操纵工具名称、描述、readOnlyHint、inputSchema等元数据影响代理对工具角色的认知）。通过实现原型验证两类攻击的有效性。

Result: 两类MSTI攻击（工具劫持和工具框架）均能成功破坏WebMCP的预期功能，证实了该协议存在严重安全缺陷。

Conclusion: MSTI威胁源于WebMCP独特的工具生命周期和结构化元数据设计，工具面本身已成为新兴安全关注点。建议包括绑定工具身份与来源、确保生命周期一致性、强制第三方工具数据边界、维护工具注册和调用的可追溯日志。

Abstract: WebMCP is a newly emerging protocol that enables websites to expose tools directly to AI agents, bypassing traditional user interfaces and introducing new security risks. The dynamic exposure of agent-accessible tools in WebMCP expands the attack surface of web sessions, especially when third-party scripts are involved. In this study, we identify a new potential threat, termed Mid-Session Tool Injection (MSTI), in which attackers leverage third-party scripts to inject malicious tools during an active session. To better characterize this threat, we classify MSTI based on the stage and target of manipulation, distinguishing between Tool Hijacking and Tool Framing. Tool Hijacking modifies the set of tools visible to the agent through mechanisms such as the AbortSignal API or race conditions during tool registration. In contrast, Tool Framing influences the agent's perception of tool roles through metadata fields such as tool name, description, readOnlyHint, and inputSchema. Our implementation demonstrates that both Tool Hijacking and Tool Framing can successfully disrupt the intended functionality of WebMCP. Based on these results, we outline potential mitigation directions and provide security design recommendations for WebMCP, including binding tool identity to its origin, ensuring lifecycle consistency, enforcing data boundaries for third-party tools, and maintaining traceable logs of tool registration and invocation. These findings indicate that MSTI arises from WebMCP's unique tool lifecycle and structured metadata, making the tool surface itself an emerging security concern.

</details>


### [133] [Will the Agent Recuse Itself? Measuring LLM-Agent Compliance with In-Band Access-Deny Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06460)
*Thamilvendhan Munirathinam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"Recuse Signal"的轻量级、公开的带内拒绝信号，作为LLM自治代理的协作治理控制机制（类似robots.txt但用于实时访问），并通过实验验证了LLM代理会主动遵守该信号，在无信号时100%完成任务，有信号时100%退出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自治LLM代理持有真实凭证并操作基础设施，但缺乏标准的带外机制告知代理哪些资源不可访问。现有访问控制要么完全放行（代理有有效凭证），要么硬拒绝（与其他客户端无异），缺少一种让代理"自愿退出"的协作治理模式。

Method: 定义Recuse Signal为开放小标准，在SSH横幅/PAM钩子和PostgreSQL有线协议代理上实现两个零或低足迹适配器，在真实生产主机上部署，进行受控实验：给新代理布置良性运维任务，观察其是否退出。实验包括OpenAI GPT-4o、GPT-4o-mini和Claude Code代理，对比有信号和无信号条件下的行为。

Result: 有信号时100%触发代理退出（recusal），无信号时100%完成任务。且该信号表现为协作信号而非绝对信号：在明确的操作员授权框架下，最强大的模型会继续执行操作，而其他代理仍服从主机策略。

Conclusion: Recuse Signal作为一种轻量级、公开的带内拒绝信号，能有效诱导LLM代理自愿退出，是一种实用的协作治理机制。作者已发布标准、适配器和实验工具供复现。

Abstract: As autonomous LLM agents increasingly hold real credentials and operate infrastructure without a human in the loop, operators have no standard way to tell an agent that a resource is off-limits. Access controls either let the agent in (it has valid credentials) or hard-fail it (indistinguishable from any other client). We propose a third mode: a lightweight, published in-band deny signal -- the Recuse Signal -- that a server emits over a protocol's existing channels (an SSH banner, a PostgreSQL NOTICE) asking a connecting automated agent to voluntarily withdraw. This is a cooperative governance control, the robots.txt analogue for live access; it is explicitly not a security boundary. Its value is entirely empirical and, to our knowledge, unmeasured: do compliant LLM agents actually honor such a signal? We define the signal as an open mini-standard, implement two zero- or low-footprint adapters (an SSH banner/PAM hook and a PostgreSQL wire-protocol proxy), deploy them on a live production host, and run a controlled experiment in which fresh agents are given a benign operations task and observed for recusal. In a pilot (SSH; OpenAI GPT-4o and GPT-4o-mini; and Claude Code as a deployed agent), the signal cleanly induces recusal -- 100% recusal when present versus 100% task completion in a no-signal control -- and, revealingly, behaves as a cooperative rather than absolute signal: an explicit operator-authorization framing flips the most capable model to proceed, while other agents continue to defer to the on-host policy. We release the standard, adapters, and experiment harness for reproduction.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Deterministic Envelopes for Tamed SGLD: Decoupling Stochastic-Gradient Noise and Localizing Taming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05242)
*Yiwei Zhou,Ziheng Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文发现随机梯度Langevin算法中，当分母与分子使用同一随机梯度实现时，taming步骤会改变随机Oracle本身并产生平稳偏差。提出了一种结构保持的确定性包络框架，避免梯度依赖分母带来的偏差，并设计了软硬混合的尾部控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机梯度Langevin算法使用tamed分母来稳定非全局Lipschitz漂移项，但忽略了当分母与分子依赖同一随机梯度实现时，taming步骤会改变随机Oracle并引入平稳偏差。需要一种能保持结构且避免这一偏差的框架。

Method: 提出结构保持的确定性包络框架：在Oracle噪声采样前固定分母，利用局部确定性包络避免在典型区域进行不必要的taming。该框架将平稳误差分解为Oracle依赖taming带来的偏差和确定性稳定化引入的剩余误差。进一步分析识别出远尾条件，揭示局部软包络的局限性，并提出软包络（典型区域）与硬尾控制（稀有偏离）相结合的混合设计。

Result: 实验证实了随机分母导致的平稳失真、确定性包络设计的偏差减小效果，以及混合构造的稳定作用。

Conclusion: 确定性包络框架能有效消除随机梯度依赖分母引入的平稳偏差，混合软硬包络设计在保持稳定性的同时进一步减少了误差。

Abstract: Stochastic-gradient Langevin algorithms often use tamed denominators to stabilize non-globally Lipschitz drifts. This paper shows that when the denominator depends on the same stochastic-gradient realization as the numerator, the taming step changes the stochastic oracle itself and can create a stationary bias even if the original stochastic gradient is unbiased. We propose a structure-preserving framework for designing tamed denominators. It fixes the denominator before the oracle noise is sampled and uses localized deterministic envelopes to avoid unnecessary taming in typical regions. These kernels keep the stabilizing effect of taming while avoiding the bias introduced by a gradient-dependent denominator. Our theory explains how the stationary error splits into the bias caused by oracle-dependent taming and the remaining error introduced by deterministic stabilization. Within this deterministic-envelope family, the analysis identifies a far-tail condition that explains the limitation of local soft envelopes and motivates a hybrid member: soft in the typical region, but protected by hard-tail control on rare excursions. Experiments confirm the predicted stationary distortions of random denominators, the bias reduction of deterministic-envelope designs, and the stabilizing effect of the hybrid construction.

</details>


### [135] [Environment-Robust Representation Learning with Empirical Bayes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05365)
*Yuli Slavutsky,Matthew Shen,Bohan Wu,David M. Blei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多环境预测的贝叶斯模型，假设环境改变潜变量分布而观测-目标机制条件稳定，通过变分推理和实证贝叶斯方法实现跨环境预测，在天文源识别、微生物组疾病检测和ICU脓毒症预测中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同环境（如医院、临床队列）中潜变量（如患者状态）的分布可能不同，但潜变量与观测数据、目标变量之间的条件关系保持稳定。现有方法未能充分利用这种结构先验进行跨环境预测。

Method: 构建贝叶斯模型，推导变分目标函数（分解为环境内项和跨环境平衡项）；采用经验贝叶斯设定先验；开发摊销变分推理算法进行后验近似，利用学习到的潜变量在新环境中进行预测。

Result: 在天文源识别、微生物组疾病检测和ICU脓毒症预测三个真实场景中，该方法在跨环境预测任务上均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模环境间的潜变量分布差异并保持条件机制稳定，所提方法能有效实现跨环境泛化，具有广泛的临床应用和科学发现价值。

Abstract: We consider multi-environment prediction problems. We assume the environments change the distribution of a latent variable, while the mechanisms generating observed covariates and targets remain stable conditional on that variable. For example, hospitals or clinical cohorts may differ in the prevalence of latent patient states, even though the relationships between those states, physiological measurements, and outcomes remain unchanged. Given a dataset from multiple environments, we formulate a Bayesian model for such problems and derive the corresponding variational objective. We show that this objective decomposes into per-environment terms and an additional cross-environment balancing term induced by the model's structure. We use an empirical Bayes method to set the prior and incorporate it into the objective. Based on this objective, we develop an amortized variational algorithm for posterior approximation, and use the resulting learned latent variables to form predictions in new environments.We study our approach through simulations and real-world studies of astronomical source identification, microbiome-based disease detection, and ICU sepsis prediction. Across these settings, our method outperforms previous approaches for prediction in new environments.

</details>


### [136] [Sparse Functional Singular Value Decomposition for Biclustering and Triclustering Longitudinal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05488)
*Yue Zhao,Thierry Chekouo,Sandra Safo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Tri-SfSVD，一个统一的功能性稀疏奇异值分解框架，用于从稀疏、不规则采样的纵向高维数据中发现双聚类与三聚类结构，避免传统方法依赖插值或形状同质性假设的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂疾病（如IBD）的亚型识别需从纵向组学数据中捕捉潜在模式，但现有（双）聚类方法和功能数据分析方法难以处理高维、稀疏、不规则采样数据，且现有功能双聚类方法依赖插值或强制形状同质性假设，缺乏同时进行轨迹估计与多维度选取的统一框架。

Method: 提出Tri-SfSVD框架，将连续轨迹估计与受试者、特征、时间三个维度的稀疏选择集成到单一优化框架中；通过对受试者、变量和时间子区域施加稀疏惩罚，直接在观测数据上发现受试者级、受试者-特征级和受试者-特征-时间级局部结构。

Result: 模拟实验表明Tri-SfSVD在高维场景下优于现有方法。在IBD多组学数据中识别出三个双聚类，将样本簇与IBD临床特征关联到特定菌群对应的微生物通路群；在多通道EEG数据中识别出三个三聚类，将酒精相关表型与局部脑活动模式关联，包括同一脑区内由时间子区域分隔的亚组差异。

Conclusion: Tri-SfSVD能够有效从稀疏纵向高维数据中揭示可解释的受试者-特征和受试者-特征-时间关联，为疾病异质性表征提供新的分析工具。

Abstract: Identifying subtypes of complex conditions, such as Inflammatory Bowel Disease (IBD), often requires capturing latent patterns in longitudinal omics data. However, these data are typically high-dimensional, sparsely sampled, and irregularly observed over time, posing substantial challenges for conventional (bi)clustering and functional data analysis methods. We propose Tri-SfSVD, a unified sparse functional Singular Value Decomposition framework for discovering biclusters and triclusters in longitudinal data. Unlike existing functional biclustering methods that rely on ad hoc imputation or enforce restrictive shape-homogeneity assumptions, Tri-SfSVD integrates continuous trajectory estimation with simultaneous subject, feature, and temporal selection within a single optimization framework. By imposing sparse penalties across subjects, variables, and temporal subregions, the proposed method works directly on observed data to uncover localized structures at the subject, subject-feature, and subject-feature-time levels. Extensive simulations demonstrate that Tri-SfSVD outperforms existing approaches in high-dimensional settings. Applied to IBD multi-omics data, the method identified three biclusters linking sample clusters with distinct IBD-related clinical characteristics to microbial pathway groups associated with specific bacterial taxa, providing interpretable subject-pathway associations for characterizing disease heterogeneity. Applied to multi-channel EEG data, the method identified three triclusters linking sample clusters with distinct alcohol-related phenotypes to localized brain activity patterns, including subgroup differences separated by temporal subregions within the same spatial region.

</details>


### [137] [Conformal Risk-Averse Decision Making with Action Conditional Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05551)
*Zihan Zhu,Shayan Kiyani,George Pappas. Hamed Hassani*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出动作条件共形预测（Action-Conditional Conformal Prediction），将共形预测的边际安全保证扩展到动作条件性保证，通过pinball损失最小化算法实现有限样本下的风险规避决策优化，实验验证了该方法在动作条件性能上的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形预测方法仅提供边际安全保证，无法满足风险规避决策者对特定动作条件下的风险控制需求，亟需一种能够显式条件于决策动作的不确定性量化方法。

Method: (i) 提出动作条件共形预测，将安全保证显式条件于决策者每个动作；(ii) 证明动作条件预测集可作为风险规避决策者优化动作条件风险价值（CVaR）的可行决策空间代理；(iii) 提出基于pinball损失最小化的有限样本算法，将Gibbs等人(2025)的框架与动作条件保证相连。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在动作条件性能上显著优于共形预测基线方法。

Conclusion: 动作条件共形预测为风险规避决策提供了更精细的安全保证，有效弥补了边际保证在动作层面上的不足。

Abstract: Reliable decision making pipelines powered by machine learning models require uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods that come with explicit safety guarantees. Conformal prediction provides such UQ by wrapping ML predictions into prediction sets, and recent work by Kiyani et al. (2025b) established that these sets can be translated into optimal risk-averse decision policies -- yet only inheriting marginal safety guarantees. We generalize and strengthen their results by (i) introducing action-conditional conformal prediction, which yields safety guarantees conditioned explicitly on each action taken by the decision maker, (ii) showing that action-conditional prediction sets serve as a proxy for the feasible decision space for risk-averse decision makers aiming to optimize action-conditional value-at-risk, and (iii) proposing a principled finite-sample algorithm based on pinball-loss minimization, connecting the framework of Gibbs et al. (2025) to action-conditional guarantees. Experiments on two real-world datasets confirm that our approach significantly improves action-conditional performance over conformal baselines.

</details>


### [138] [Finding Most Influential Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05919)
*Lucas D. Konrad,Nikolas Kuschnig*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Dinkelbach方法将最影响集（MIS）选择问题归约为一个单参数top-k序列，实现了O(n)每轮迭代的算法，可精确求解过去计算上不可达的MIS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别最影响集（MIS）——即移除后能最大程度改变目标估计量的size-k子集——通常因需要搜索C(n,k)个子集而计算不可行，亟需高效方法。

Method: 将具有线性分数留出集效应的估计量的MIS选择归约为一个单参数top-k问题序列；采用Dinkelbach方法实现每轮迭代O(n)复杂度的算法，具有有限终止性；对固定残差化输入得到全局最优集，对估计 nuisance 函数的情形通过均匀分母和生成得分稳定性实现一阶近似。

Result: 算法可恢复精确MIS，这些MIS在之前计算上不可达；在固定残差化输入下对单变量比率目标返回全局最优集；在分离条件下实现精确集恢复。

Conclusion: 论文提出的方法大幅降低了MIS选择问题的计算复杂度，使得之前不可行的精确求解成为可能，具有重要的理论和应用价值。

Abstract: Identifying most influential sets (MIS) - size-$k$ subsets whose removal maximally changes a target estimand - is typically infeasible because it requires searching over $\binom{n}{k}$ subsets. For estimands with linear-fractional leave-set-out effects, we show that MIS selection reduces to a one-parameter sequence of top-$k$ problems. Dinkelbach's method yields an algorithm with $\mathcal{O}(n)$ cost per iteration and finite termination. For fixed residualized inputs, the algorithm returns a globally optimal set for the univariate ratio objective, including the oracle-residualized partial linear model. With estimated nuisance functions, uniform denominator and generated-score stability imply approximation to the first-order oracle orthogonal-score objective; exact set recovery follows under a separation condition. Simulations and applications show that the method recovers exact MIS that were previously computationally inaccessible.

</details>


### [139] [EML-CD: Causal Mechanism Recovery via EML Symbolic Trees in Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05942)
*Sota Asanuma*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出EML-CD框架，将可解释的初等函数组合算子集成到因果结构学习中，能从神经网络权重中恢复可解释的闭式因果方程，在多个数据集上验证了其机制发现能力，结构恢复性能与基线相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经网络的非线性因果发现方法虽能恢复DAG结构，但每个因果机制仍是黑盒，且Waxman等人论证了从NN权重提取因果机制是不适定问题。因此需要一种既能发现因果结构、又能恢复可解释因果机制的方法。

Method: 提出EML-CD框架，将EML算子（能从单一二元算子组合初等函数）集成到因果结构学习中。每个边机制表示为门控EML二叉树，自动发现闭式因果方程，可直接计算解析雅可比矩阵以定量理解因果效应。

Result: 在Sachs蛋白质信号数据上（d=11），EML-CD的SHD=11.2±0.4，与PC/GES相当且低于CAM；检测到的边精度0.756、召回0.365。双变量测试中恢复10/11个初等函数族（held-out形状相关≥0.96）。符号合成基准上机制f-MSE远低于固定SINDy字典（均值3.67 vs 7644），结构恢复SHD=14.0与字典相当。Causal Chambers数据集上深度2模型F1优于线性OLS-BIC（0.444 vs 0.273）。

Conclusion: EML-CD成功实现了以可解释机制恢复为主要目标的因果结构学习，能发现闭式因果方程，在机制恢复质量上显著优于传统方法，结构发现性能与现有方法相当，为非线性因果发现提供了可解释的新范式。

Abstract: Neural network (NN)-based nonlinear causal discovery methods recover DAG structure but leave each causal mechanism as a black box. Waxman et al. argued that extracting causal mechanisms from NN weights is ill-posed. We propose EML-CD, a framework that integrates the EML operator (capable of composing elementary functions from a single binary operator) into causal structure learning, with interpretable mechanism recovery as the primary objective. EML-CD represents each edge mechanism as a gated EML binary tree and automatically discovers closed-form causal equations. Analytical Jacobians can be directly computed from the output equations, enabling quantitative understanding of causal effects. On real data (Sachs protein signaling, d=11), EML-CD achieves SHD=11.2 +/- 0.4 (5-seed mean; baselines are single deterministic runs), on par with PC/GES within seed variance and below CAM, while attaching closed-form equations to each detected edge (precision 0.756, recall 0.365). In a controlled bivariate test with known mechanisms, EML-CD recovers 10 of 11 elementary function families faithfully (held-out shape correlation >= 0.96; only high-frequency sine is partial). On a symbolic synthetic benchmark, EML-CD attains a substantially lower and more stable held-out mechanism f-MSE than a fixed SINDy dictionary (mean 3.67 vs. 7644, the latter inflated by catastrophic extrapolation on one seed), although its structure recovery (SHD 14.0) only matches the dictionary and stays below specialized optimizers; on the Causal Chambers light-tunnel subset, a depth-2 model improves F1 over linear OLS-BIC (0.444 vs. 0.273).

</details>


### [140] [Fast and Robust Convergence Rate for TD(0) with Linear Function Approximation, Universal Learning Steps and I.I.D. Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05967)
*Ziad Kobeissi,Éloïse Berthier*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了带线性函数逼近的TD(0)时序差分方法在有限时间内的收敛性，建立了MSE的O(1/k)收敛速率，该速率具有最优迭代依赖、对病态问题鲁棒且下界常数小于11，并提出了改进变体PCTD(0)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TD(0)的O(1/k)收敛速率依赖于线性参数化非中心协方差矩阵的最小特征值（即病态依赖），缺乏鲁棒性且不够尖锐，本文旨在建立不依赖该特征值的快速、鲁棒且尖锐的收敛速率。

Method: 采用独立同分布（i.i.d.）on-policy采样、恒定学习步长和Polyak-Juditsky平均方法；提出PCTD(0)变体，在马尔可夫链强混合假设下进一步改善收敛性质。

Result: 建立了MSE的O(1/k)收敛速率，具有最优迭代依赖、仅依赖于初始误差和模型无关常数、下界乘性常数小于11等特点；PCTD(0)在强混合条件下收敛性更优。

Conclusion: TD(0)的收敛速率首次摆脱对最小特征值的病态依赖，实现了更鲁棒且更尖锐的收敛保证，PCTD(0)进一步拓展了应用场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the finite-time behavior of the TD(0) temporal-difference method with linear function approximation (LFA). We consider on-policy independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) samples, a constant learning step, and the Polyak-Juditsky averaging method. We establish a new convergence rate, for the Mean-Square Error (MSE) on the approximated function, that is (i) fast in the sense that it admits an optimal dependency in the number of iterations k (i.e., of order 1/k), (ii) robust to ill-conditioning: it only depends on an initial error and modelindependent constants and (iii) sharp up to a multiplicative constant lower than 11. In particular, it does not depend on the smallest eigenvalue of the uncentered covariance matrix of the linear parametrization, unlike all pre-existing O(1/k) rates in the TD(0) literature. We also introduce PCTD(0), a variant of TD(0), which benefits from better convergence properties under an additional assumption of strong mixing on the Markov Chain.

</details>


### [141] [Adaptive Learning Rates with Surrogate Probability for Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06043)
*Jongyeong Lee,Junya Honda,Shinji Ito,Chansoo Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于替代概率函数的自适应学习率方法，使得计算高效的FTPL算法能够实现最佳双世界（BOBW）保证，解决了其因优化无关性而难以设计概率相关学习率的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FTPL作为FTRL的计算高效替代方案，其优化无关的特性使得设计自适应、概率相关的学习率变得困难，导致其BOBW保证相对有限。现有结果仅局限于特定参数选择（如α=2），缺乏更一般的泛化。

Method: 引入替代概率函数，仅利用可用量计算而不需要精确概率；在此基础上设计自适应学习率；将方法应用于带Pareto扰动的FTPL（任意形状参数α>1）以及专家建议的赌博机问题。

Result: 为FTPL提供了任意α>1的BOBW保证，推广了先前仅限α=2的结果；在专家建议的赌博机问题中也实现了BOBW保证；保持了FTPL的计算简洁性。

Conclusion: 替代概率方法使得FTPL能够在不牺牲计算效率的前提下获得概率依赖的自适应性，该方法可能对FTPL之外的其他算法框架和学习率设计具有独立参考价值。

Abstract: Follow-the-regularized-leader framework has shown effectiveness and flexibility in online learning problems, where the choice of learning rates are known to be crucial. Recently, adaptive learning rates defined in terms of the arm-selection probabilities, obtained by solving convex optimization, have achieved improved best-of-both-worlds (BOBW) guarantees in various bandit problems. In contrast, BOBW guarantees for its computationally efficient alternative, follow-the-perturbed-leader (FTPL), remain relatively limited since its optimization-free nature ironically makes the design of adaptive, probability-dependent learning rates non-trivial. To address this challenge, we propose an adaptive learning rate for FTPL by introducing surrogate probability functions that can be computed only from the available quantities, without requiring the exact probabilities. Based on these learning rates with surrogate functions, we provide the BOBW guarantee for FTPL with Pareto perturbations for any shape parameter $α>1$, generalizing prior results restricted to specific choices of $α=2$. We further show the BOBW guarantees for FTPL with adaptive learning rates in the bandit problem with expert advices. Our approach preserves the computational simplicity of FTPL while enabling probability-dependent adaptivity, and the surrogate-based methodology may be of independent interest in other algorithmic frameworks beyond FTPL and learning rate designs.

</details>


### [142] [Effective Dimensionality as an Operator Invariant for Physics-Preserving Constraint Adaptation in Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06171)
*Cornelius Otchere,Michael Shields*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文利用Fisher信息矩阵量化物理约束模型的有效自由度（d_eff），证明了对于有限维核的微分算子，d_eff收敛于核维度（独立于网络架构），并提出了子空间投影策略，可在不重新训练的情况下高效适应新边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（PINNs）因共享参数空间而面临任务干扰（物理方程与边界条件的冲突），现有方法缺乏对结构冲突的量化分析，需要一个先验的结构诊断指标来理解约束如何吸收网络的自由参数方向。

Method: 1）利用Fisher信息矩阵量化物理约束模型的有效自由度d_eff；2）从理论上证明对有限维核算子，d_eff收敛于核维度，成为连续算子的结构不变量；3）提出子空间投影策略，将参数更新投影到预训练物理算子的零空间中，实现边界条件的高效适应。

Result: d_eff对有限维核算子收敛到核维度（独立于网络宽度、深度或激活函数）；对无限维核算子，d_eff衡量网络的有限维表示带宽。子空间投影方法可在数秒到数分钟内实现与梯度微调近似的边界适应质量，且无需重新训练。

Conclusion: d_eff可作为一种先验结构诊断工具，将其驱动为零可验证问题适定性；子空间投影策略为PINNs的边界适应提供了高效替代方案，在保持物理约束的同时快速适应新的边界条件。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks inherently suffer from task interference because they rely on a shared parameter space to satisfy both governing differential equations and boundary conditions. We analyze this structural conflict using the Fisher Information Matrix to quantify the effective degrees of freedom ($d_{eff}$) in a physics-constrained model. Unlike the classical $d_{eff}$ which measures how many parameter directions are informed by data against a statistical prior, our $d_{eff}$ measures the dimension of the parameter directions unconstrained by the differential operator. For operators with finite-dimensional kernel, we show that $d_{eff}$ converges to the kernel dimension exactly, independent of network width, depth, or activation function, recasting it from a fit diagnostic into a structural invariant of the underlying continuous operator. For operators with infinite-dimensional kernel, $d_{eff}$ instead measures the network's finite-dimensional representational bandwidth for that kernel rather than recovering an integer invariant. Importantly, $d_{eff}$ also serves as an a priori structural diagnostic. Driving $d_{eff}$ of a well-posed problem to zero certifies that the physics and boundary constraints have absorbed the network's free directions. Building on this characterization, we introduce subspace projection strategies for boundary adaptation. Rather than retraining from scratch, we project parameter updates into the null space of the pre-trained physics operator so that new boundary conditions are satisfied without disturbing the learned physics. Gradient-based fine-tuning can match or exceed this but needs more wall-clock time and tuning, whereas subspace projection delivers near-equivalent quality in seconds to minutes. We validate on linear and nonlinear operators, demonstrating accurate adaptation to initial and boundary shifts and unencountered constraint types.

</details>


### [143] [Diffusion Models Observe Only Gradients: A Geometric Perspective on Score Matching Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06179)
*Naïl B. Khelifa,Richard E. Turner,Ramji Venkataramanan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明L^2分数匹配误差并非衡量扩散模型边缘分布质量的正确指标，通过Helmholtz-Hodge分解将分数误差分为梯度分量和螺线管分量，指出仅有梯度分量影响边缘Fokker-Planck动力学，并给出了新的理论界限和可估计算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型训练最小化L^2分数匹配误差，但理论分析表明该指标与生成样本质量之间可能存在不一致，需要更精确的几何理解来建立分数误差与分布质量之间的正确关系。

Method: 利用Helmholtz-Hodge分解将分数误差分解为梯度分量（无旋）和螺线管分量（无散），分析二者在Fokker-Planck方程中的不同作用；基于此推导KL散度的上界，并提出通过Sobolev对偶恒等式可估计梯度分量的方法。

Result: (1) 证明L^2分数误差的任何单调函数都无法一致地作为分布散度的下界（ impossibility）；(2) 导出仅依赖梯度分量的KL散度上界，改进了标准Girsanov界；(3) 给出梯度分量的可估计算子，经验表明其与样本质量的相关性显著优于完整L^2误差。

Conclusion: L^2分数匹配误差包含对边缘分布质量无贡献的"不可见"分量，应转而关注梯度分量作为更可靠的模型评估指标。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models are typically trained by minimizing the $L^2$ score matching error, and standard theoretical analyses rely on this quantity to bound the sampling discrepancy between the learned and target distributions. We show the $L^2$ score error is not the right intrinsic measure of marginal distributional quality: a learned diffusion model can incur arbitrarily large $L^2$ score error while perfectly matching the target distribution. By decomposing score errors into a gradient and a solenoidal component (a Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition), we identify the geometric reason behind this: only the gradient component enters the marginal Fokker-Planck dynamics, while the solenoidal component is structurally invisible. We make this precise in three results. First, building on the corrected geometry, we prove an impossibility result: no monotone function of the $L^2$ score error can uniformly lower bound any divergence between the learned and target distributions. Second, we derive an upper bound on the Kullback-Leibler divergence that depends only on the observable gradient component of the error, tightening the standard Girsanov bound and identifying its looseness as the cost of operating on path-space rather than marginal-space dynamics. Third, we give a tractable estimator of the gradient component via a dual Sobolev identity, which is shown to empirically correlate substantially better with sample quality than the full $L^2$ error.

</details>


### [144] [Anchor PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06233)
*Benedikt Seiter,Anya Fries,Julius von Kügelgen,Jonas Peters*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Anchor PCA是一种针对多领域数据的鲁棒主成分分析方法，通过在共享与领域特定低秩嵌入间权衡，实现对未见领域更具解释力的降维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PCA对多领域数据进行池化降维时，可能关注仅在少数领域具有高方差的虚假方向。作者希望找到一种能同时解释多数领域变异、对未见领域仍保持鲁棒的共享低秩嵌入方法。

Method: 提出Anchor PCA，通过优化整体解释方差与共享/领域特定低秩嵌入之间一致性的权衡，转化为对修正目标矩阵的PCA求解，计算高效；理论上证明其可恢复最大不变子空间，并在领域特定协方差膨胀下具有极小极大重构解释。

Result: 在模拟数据和具有时间漂移的真实气体传感器数据上，Anchor PCA能够恢复最大不变子空间，并且在未见领域上比池化基线和最坏情况替代方案解释更多方差。

Conclusion: Anchor PCA为多领域数据的鲁棒无监督降维提供了一种有前景的解决方案，兼顾了计算效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: Principal component analysis (PCA) is one of the most widely used unsupervised dimension reduction techniques. We study PCA for data from multiple related domains. Since principal components generally differ across domains, one way to obtain a shared low-rank embedding is to perform PCA on the pooled data. However, this approach can focus on spurious directions that exhibit high variation in only a few domains.
