To find a robust embedding that still explains most variance in unseen but similar domains, we propose instead to focus on shared directions of variation. To this end, we introduce Anchor PCA which trades off overall explained variance with agreement between the shared and domain-specific low-rank embeddings. Anchor PCA amounts to PCA on a modified target matrix and thus can be solved efficiently. Moreover, we show that Anchor PCA recovers a maximal invariant subspace and admits a minimax reconstruction interpretation under bounded domain-specific covariance inflations. On simulated and real-world gas sensor data with temporal drift, we demonstrate, respectively, that Anchor PCA recovers the maximally invariant subspace and yields embeddings that explain more variance on unseen domains than the pooling baseline and a worst-case alternative. Taken together, these findings establish Anchor PCA as a promising approach to robust unsupervised dimension reduction from multi-domain data.

</details>


### [145] [Discrete Causal Representations from Heterogeneous Domains: A Bayesian Approach with Social Survey Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06288)
*Ankur Garg,Michael Stettler,Aaron Schein,Julius von Kügelgen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯方法，用于从多环境数据中学习离散因果概念的高层潜在因果表示，通过序贯蒙特卡洛采样近似多模态后验，并在社会调查数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果表示学习旨在从低层观测数据中推断高层潜在因果概念，但现有工作多关注可辨识性，缺乏实用的不确定性感知方法及真实世界用例。本文旨在填补这一空白，针对多环境异构数据中的分布偏移问题，提出一种贝叶斯因果表示学习方法。

Method: 将因果假设和可解释性需求转化为层次模型中的先验和参数选择，针对离散因果概念和未知多节点软干预场景，设计基于序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）采样的推断方案来近似多模态后验分布。

Result: 在社会调查数据（文化价值观、政治观点等）上的案例研究表明，该方法能够推断出有意义的高层概念及其合理的因果结构，验证了模型在真实复杂数据中学习因果表示的实用性。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯方法结合序贯蒙特卡洛推断能够有效处理多环境数据中离散因果概念的学习问题，为因果表示学习在社会科学等真实场景中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Causal representation learning aims to infer the high-level latent causal concepts that give rise to observed low-level measurements. This is particularly relevant for heterogeneous data from different environments or domains since distribution shifts often arise through sparse, localized changes in some of the underlying causal mechanisms, while other parts of the generative process remain unchanged. Whereas identifiability of causal representations has been studied extensively, practical uncertainty-aware methods and real-world use cases remain less explored. In this work, we propose a Bayesian approach to learning causal representations from multi-environment data, focusing on the case of discrete causal concepts and unknown multi-node soft interventions. To this end, we translate causal assumptions and interpretability desiderata into suitable priors and parametric choices within a hierarchical model. We then devise an inference scheme based on sequential Monte Carlo sampling to approximate the resulting multimodal posterior. We showcase our approach through case studies on social survey data, where latent causal concepts correspond to cultural values or political opinions, measurements to survey responses, and environments to different countries or states. Our model infers meaningful high-level concepts and plausible causal relations among them, demonstrating its utility for learning causal representations of complex real-world data.

</details>


### [146] [Symmetric Divergence and Normalized Similarity: A Unified Topological Framework for Representation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06342)
*Yan Wang,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的拓扑数据分析工具包，包含对称表示拓扑散度（SRTD）用于细粒度结构诊断，以及归一化拓扑相似度（NTS）用于跨场景标准化评估，解决了现有拓扑散度非对称、无界且依赖样本大小的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有配对拓扑散度（如RTD）存在启发式非对称性以及无界分数依赖样本大小的问题，阻碍了可靠的跨场景基准测试。

Method: 1) 提出对称表示拓扑散度（SRTD）及其高效变体SRTD-lite，解决理论非对称性，将诊断信息整合为单一交叉条码签名。2) 提出归一化拓扑相似度（NTS），通过测量层级合并顺序的秩相关，得到在[-1,1]之间尺度不变的度量。

Result: 在合成和真实深度学习实验中，该工具包能够捕捉CNN中被几何度量遗漏的功能性变化，并在距离饱和条件下稳健地映射LLM谱系。

Conclusion: 该工具包提供了严格的拓扑感知视角，补充了CKA等度量方法。

Abstract: Topological Data Analysis (TDA) offers a principled, intrinsic lens for comparing neural representations. However, existing paired topological divergences (e.g., RTD) are limited by heuristic asymmetry and, more critically, unbounded scores that depend on sample size, hindering reliable cross-scenario benchmarking. To address these challenges, we develop a unified topological toolkit serving two complementary needs: fine-grained structural diagnosis and robust, standardized evaluation. First, we complete the RTD framework by introducing Symmetric Representation Topology Divergence (SRTD) and its efficient variant SRTD-lite. Beyond resolving the theoretical asymmetry of prior variants, SRTD consolidates diagnostic information into a single, comprehensive cross-barcode signature. This allows for precise localization of structural discrepancies and serves as an effective optimization objective without the overhead of dual directional computations. Second, to enable reliable benchmarking across heterogeneous settings, we propose Normalized Topological Similarity (NTS). By measuring the rank correlation of hierarchical merge orders, NTS yields a scale-invariant metric bounded between -1 and 1, effectively overcoming the scale and sample-dependence of unnormalized divergences. Experiments across synthetic and real-world deep learning settings demonstrate that our toolkit captures functional shifts in CNNs missed by geometric measures and robustly maps LLM genealogy even under distance saturation, offering a rigorous, topology-aware perspective that complements measures like CKA.

</details>


### [147] [Function-Space Priors for Bayesian Neural ODEs with Application to Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06351)
*Jaeyeong Lee,Wonmo Koo,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出结合函数空间正则化与概率多步打靶法的贝叶斯神经常微分方程框架，通过在向量场上施加高斯过程核先验来预测船舶轨迹，同时提供校准的不确定性估计，有效处理了AIS数据的不规则采样和复杂动力学问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯神经常微分方程使用各向同性高斯权重先验，无法编码船舶动力学的平滑性和局部性等结构特性；而现有函数空间贝叶斯神经网络方法不直接适用于神经常微分方程，因为主要关注轨迹而非向量场本身。直接在ODE解上放置高斯过程先验需要将分布通过非线性ODE求解器传播，这在分析上难以处理。

Method: 在有限测量点上对向量场施加基于高斯过程核先验的正则化项，扩展标准权重空间变分目标；结合概率多步打靶法将推理解耦到时间片段中，同时保持全局一致性，以处理长序列和不规则的AIS轨迹。

Result: 该方法能够对船舶轨迹进行准确的点预测并提供校准良好的不确定性估计，有效应对AIS数据的不规则采样、缺失报告和复杂动力学挑战。

Conclusion: 通过函数空间正则化与概率多步打靶法的结合，所提框架为连续时间轨迹建模与不确定性量化提供了原则性且实用的方案，显著提升了海事态势感知中轨迹预测的可靠性。

Abstract: Vessel trajectory prediction from Automatic Identification System (AIS) data is essential for maritime situational awareness, yet it remains challenging due to irregular sampling, missing reports, and complex dynamics. Beyond accurate point forecasts, maritime applications also demand well-calibrated uncertainty estimates for reliable decision-making. Bayesian Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) offer a principled framework for continuous-time trajectory modeling with uncertainty quantification by placing a prior over the neural vector field parameters. However, the commonly used isotropic Gaussian weight prior fails to encode informative structural properties of vessel dynamics, such as smoothness and locality. Existing function-space Bayesian neural network methods address this limitation for static mappings, but do not transfer directly to Neural ODEs, where the primary quantity of interest is the trajectory rather than the vector field itself. In principle, one could place a Gaussian process (GP) prior directly over ODE solutions, but this requires propagating distributions through a nonlinear ODE solver, which is analytically intractable. To address this challenge, we adopt a practical approach that imposes a GP-kernel-based prior directly on the vector field evaluated at a finite set of measurement points. Specifically, we augment the standard weight-space variational objective with a kernel-based regularizer that penalizes deviations of the vector field from the structure implied by a GP prior. To handle long and irregular AIS trajectories, we further combine this function-space regularization with probabilistic multiple shooting, which decouples inference across temporal segments while maintaining global consistency.

</details>


### [148] [Conformal Risk Sharing: Certified Cost Allocation with Participation Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06391)
*Ieva Kazlauskaite*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Conformal Risk Sharing框架，通过将可解释的分担策略与分割共形校准相结合，解决认证分配问题：在无分布假设条件下，从有限数据中设计再分配规则、为每个参与者提供可信的义务上限，并验证无人受损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见不良事件的财务冲击在群体中分担可减轻极端个体负担，但任何因该安排而处境更差的参与者都有理由退出。因此需要一个可信机制，为每个主体提供未来义务的可靠上限，并确保参与者之间的总损失有界。

Method: 提出Conformal Risk Sharing框架：将可解释的分担策略与分割共形校准相结合。分担强度在训练数据上调优，保留的校准数据在可交换性假设下生成分布无关的每个主体保证。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据（包括降水和能源合作社数据）上的实验表明，该框架能大幅降低高风险主体的极端义务，同时控制对其他主体的损害。

Conclusion: Conformal Risk Sharing有效解决了认证分配问题，为风险分担机制提供了分布无关的个体级保证，兼顾公平性与效率。

Abstract: Sharing the financial impact of rare adverse events across a group can soften extreme individual burdens, but any participant made worse off by the arrangement has reason to leave. A credible mechanism must therefore provide each agent with a trustworthy cap on their future obligation and should be deployed only if the aggregate harm across participants is bounded. We formalise this as the Certified Allocation Problem: from finite data and without distributional assumptions, find a redistribution rule, produce obligation caps for every participant, and verify that no participant is made materially worse off. We propose Conformal Risk Sharing, which solves this problem by pairing an interpretable sharing policy with split conformal calibration. The sharing intensity is tuned on training data, while held-out calibration data produces distribution-free per-agent guarantees (valid under exchangeability). Experiments on synthetic and real-world data, including precipitation and energy-cooperative data, confirm that the framework can substantially reduce extreme obligations for high-risk agents while controlling harm to others.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Trace-Class Results for MCMC Algorithms for Student-t Regression Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05672)
*Yasuyuki Hamura*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究学生-t回归模型中MCMC算法的迹类性质，发现数据增广算法在无信息先验下不是迹类，而压缩Gibbs算法是迹类；在正态-逆伽马先验下标准算法是迹类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究学生-t回归模型中不同MCMC算法的马尔可夫链效率，特别是从迹类性质（trace-class）的角度分析链的收敛性能。

Method: 理论分析两种MCMC算法（标准数据增广算法和压缩Gibbs算法）在两种先验设定（无信息先验和正态-逆伽马先验）下的马尔可夫算子是否具有迹类性质。

Result: 无信息先验下，标准数据增广算法不是迹类，压缩Gibbs算法是迹类；正态-逆伽马先验下，标准算法是迹类。

Conclusion: 算法选择和先验设定对MCMC链的迹类性质有显著影响，压缩Gibbs算法在无信息先验下具有更好的理论收敛性质。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider MCMC algorithms for Student-$t$ regression models. We investigate the efficiency of Markov chains based on the algorithms in terms of whether trace-class results hold or not. We first consider the case where the regression coefficients and error variance follow the invariant improper prior distributions. The Markov operator associated with a standard data augmentation algorithm is not trace-class but that associated with a collpased Gibbs algorithm is trace-class. We next consider the case where the parameters follow a normal-inverse gamma distribution. In this case, the standard Markov operator is trace-class.

</details>


### [150] [Local increment inference for time-inhomogeneous drift in Gaussian processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05681)
*Yasutaka Shimizu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了高频观测下高斯过程模型中确定性漂移结构的统计推断，提出基于一阶增量的最小二乘对比估计量，并证明了其在弱依赖条件下的相合性和渐近正态性。估计量的收敛速度同时依赖于高斯噪声的局部粗糙度和漂移的长期信息积累结构，涵盖了可积、多项式和周期等多种漂移族，并揭示了不同漂移密度导致的非标准收敛速率和加速速率等不同统计机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高频观测的高斯过程模型中，对确定性漂移结构进行统计推断，特别是处理时间非齐次的确定性漂移，并理解估计量收敛速度如何受噪声粗糙度和漂移结构共同影响。

Method: 提出基于一阶增量的最小二乘对比估计量（least squares-type contrast），在中心平稳高斯分量与时间非齐次确定性漂移的组合模型下进行参数估计，并在弱依赖条件下建立渐近理论。

Result: 建立了估计量的相合性和渐近正态性；发现收敛速度联合依赖于高斯噪声的局部粗糙度和漂移的长期信息积累结构；不同漂移族（可积、多项式、周期等）产生不同的统计机制，包括非标准收敛速率和加速速率。

Conclusion: 该框架统一处理了广泛的漂移结构，揭示了漂移密度与估计效率之间的深层联系，为高频高斯过程模型的推断提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study statistical inference for deterministic drift structures in Gaussian process models under high-frequency observations.The observed process consists of a centered stationary Gaussian component combined with a broad class of time-inhomogeneous deterministic drifts. To estimate the drift parameter, we introduce a least squares-type contrast based on first-order increments. We establish consistency and asymptotic normality under weak dependence conditions on the Gaussian component. A central feature of the framework is that the rate of convergence of the estimator depends jointly on the local roughness of the Gaussian noise and the long-time information accumulation structure generated by the drift. The theory accommodates a wide range of drift families, including integrable, polynomial-type, and periodic structures. In particular, different drift densities produce qualitatively different statistical regimes, including non-standard rates of convergence and accelerated rates for persistent or growing deterministic structures.

</details>


### [151] [On statistical inference for non-linear dynamical systems evolving in their global attractor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06018)
*Dimitri Konen,Richard Nickl*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究二维周期反应扩散系统，证明全局吸引子上的反向Poincaré不等式，进而得到L²-Lipschitz稳定性估计，并实现初始条件和状态的高效统计恢复与预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维周期反应扩散系统中，理解全局吸引子上的动力学行为以及初始条件恢复与状态预测的可行性，是动力系统反问题与统计学习的重要问题。

Method: 首先证明全局吸引子$\mathcal A$上反向Poincaré不等式成立；基于该不等式推导映射$\theta\mapsto u_θ(t)$在$\mathcal A$上的L²-Lipschitz稳定性估计；进而利用此稳定性实现从离散测量中以接近参数化的收敛速度进行统计恢复与预测。

Result: 全局吸引子上反向Poincaré不等式成立；L²-Lipschitz稳定性估计成立；初始条件及状态可在快速收敛率下统计恢复与预测。

Conclusion: 反向Poincaré不等式是连接动力系统理论、反问题稳定性与统计推断的关键工具，使得二维反应扩散系统在吸引子上的逆问题具有快速收敛性质。

Abstract: We consider a two-dimensional periodic reaction-diffusion system under natural conditions on the reaction function and with initial condition $θ$. We show that on the global attractor $\mathcal A$ of the resulting dynamical system $(u_θ(t):t>0)$, a reverse Poincaré inequality holds true, and that as a consequence the map $θ\mapsto u_θ(t)$ satisfies a $L^2$-Lipschitz stability estimate on $\mathcal A$ for any $t>0$ fixed. We then show that statistical recovery of an initial condition $θ$ in the attractor $\mathcal A$, as well as prediction of the states $u_θ$, is possible from discrete measurements of the system at `fast' near parametric convergence rates.

</details>


### [152] [An Adaptive Upper One-Sided Cumulative Sum Control Chart with Joint Parameter Optimization for Monitoring the Ratio of Two Normal Variables in Short Production Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06137)
*Kim Duc Tran*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对短生产过程中两个相关正态变量比值Z=X/Y的监控问题，提出了一种具有完全自适应联合优化参数k和决策区间h的上单侧CUSUM控制图（CUSUM-RZ⁺），通过双层优化（内层根搜索满足受控TARL₀约束，外层线搜索最小化失控TARL₁）解决了固定k设计在有限时间范围和低变异情境下的边界不可行问题，性能优于Shewhart-RZ、EWMA-RZ及固定k CUSUM-RZ⁺基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆型比值控制图主要针对长生产过程开发，其有限时间范围版本依赖从指定偏移导出的固定参考值k，这种固定k设计在给定失控幅值下并非最优，且在低变异情境下产生边界解导致受控截断平均运行长度(TARL₀)无法实现，需要一种自适应优化方法。

Method: 提出CUSUM-RZ⁺控制图，采用双层优化：内层通过有限状态马尔可夫链框架和精确比值近似进行根搜索确定h(k)以满足TARL₀=I约束，外层通过线搜索选择最优k*以最小化失控TARL₁；与Shewhart-RZ、EWMA-RZ和固定k CUSUM-RZ⁺进行匹配时间范围基准比较、蒙特卡洛稳健性研究和Phase I估计分析。

Result: 所有记忆型控制图均优于Shewhart-RZ基线；自适应设计在稳定相关条件下与基准方法性能相当，在Phase I到Phase II相关度上升时性能显著提升；对对称重尾分布不敏感，但在污染分布下轻微反保守；m≥100个子组可使TARL₀相对偏差接近1%。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应CUSUM-RZ⁺控制图有效解决了短生产过程中比值监控的固定k设计局限，通过联合优化参数提供了更优且更稳健的监控性能。

Abstract: Monitoring the ratio of two correlated normal variables is increasingly important in statistical process control, since many quality characteristics are expressed in relative rather than absolute form. Memory-type ratio charts have mostly been developed for long production runs, while their finite-horizon counterparts rely on a fixed reference value $ k $ derived from a specified shift. Such fixed-$ k $ designs are not optimal at a given out-of-control magnitude and, in low-variability regimes, yield boundary solutions for which the in-control truncated average run length (TARL$ _0 $) is unattainable. This paper proposes an upper one-sided cumulative sum (CUSUM) control chart for the ratio $ Z = X/Y $ in short production runs, denoted CUSUM-RZ$ ^+ $ (RZ standing for the ratio $ Z $), with fully adaptive joint optimization of $ k $ and the decision interval $ h $. Given a target TARL$ _0 = I $ and a target shift $ τ$, a bilevel problem calibrates $ h(k) $ by inner root-finding to satisfy the TARL$ _0 $ constraint and selects $ k^* $ by outer line search to minimize the out-of-control TARL$ _1 $. Both use a finite-state Markov-chain framework with an accurate ratio approximation; the inner step recovers boundary cases that fixed-$ k $ designs cannot. The chart is assessed through matched-horizon benchmarks against Shewhart-RZ, exponentially weighted moving average (EWMA-RZ), and fixed-$ k $ CUSUM-RZ$ ^+ $ charts, Monte Carlo robustness studies, and a Phase I estimation analysis. All memory-type charts outperform the Shewhart-RZ baseline; the adaptive design matches them under stable correlation and improves appreciably when correlation rises from Phase I to Phase II. It is insensitive to symmetric heavy tails yet mildly anti-conservative under contamination, and $ m \geq 100 $ subgroups keep the TARL$ _0 $ relative bias near 1%.

</details>


### [153] [Effect of the measurement errors on one-sided Synthetic-RZ control charts for monitoring the ratio of two normal variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06149)
*Kim Duc Tran,Thi Hien Nguyen,Kim Phuc Tran*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了测量误差对监控两个正态随机变量比率的单侧合成控制图（Synthetic-RZ图）性能的影响，发现测量误差会削弱图的检测能力，且对每个样本单元多次测量并非有效的补救措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业生产中常需要监控两个正态分布随机变量的比率，但现有研究较少考虑测量误差对该类控制图性能的影响，且通常假设偏移大小与测量误差无关，这不符合实际。

Method: 采用线性协变量误差模型将测量误差引入控制图操作，逐步描述过程从受控到失控状态下的参数演变，通过马尔可夫链方法计算零状态和稳态平均运行长度（ARL）。

Result: 测量误差会削弱Synthetic-RZ图的检测能力；对每个检查单元采集多个测量值并不能有效缓解测量误差带来的不良影响。

Conclusion: 测量误差会显著降低Synthetic-RZ图的性能，且多次测量不是有效的应对策略。

Abstract: In numerous industrial production settings, keeping track of the ratio formed by two normally distributed random variables is a task of considerable practical interest. The present work examines how measurement errors influence the behaviour of a pair of one-sided Synthetic control charts designed to monitor such a ratio (referred to here as Synthetic-RZ charts), with the analysis covering both the zero-state and the steady-state average run length ($ARL$). To incorporate measurement error into the operation of these charts, we adopt a linear covariate error model. We describe, step by step, how the parameters of the underlying model evolve as the process moves from an in-control to an out-of-control state, and we deliberately avoid the restrictive premise that the observed shift magnitude is unrelated to the measurement errors. The run length characteristics of the charts are obtained by means of a Markov chain formulation. A series of numerical experiments makes clear that measurement error erodes the detection capability of the charts. A particularly useful outcome of the investigation is that collecting several measurements on each inspected unit does not constitute an efficient remedy for the adverse influence of measurement error on the performance of the Synthetic-RZ charts.

</details>


### [154] [Monitoring the Ratio of two Normal Variables using EWMA Type Control Charts in Short Production Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06161)
*Thi Hien Nguyen,Jean-Michel Masereel,Guillaume Tartare,Kim Duc Tran*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对短生产周期中两个正态分布质量特性之比（比率）的监控问题，提出了一种指数加权移动平均（EWMA）控制图。该图采用Nadarajah (2020)的精确闭合密度而非近似分布，通过马尔可夫链校准有限检测次数内的受控截断平均运行长度（TARL₀），并基于平滑常数、相关系数、变异系数等参数进行大因子实验评估。结果表明，所提EWMA-RZ图在检测中小漂移（尤其|τ−1|≤0.05）时显著优于现有的Shewhart型短运行比率图（ShRZ），并通过饮料灌装过程示例展示了实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业与工程应用中，过程性能常由两个正态分布质量特性的比率来表征。短生产周期下传统控制图因可用检测次数少而灵敏度不足，亟需针对比率监控且适用于短运行场景的高效控制图方法。

Method: 提出EWMA控制图用于监控两个正态随机变量之比；采用Nadarajah (2020)的修正精确闭合密度而非早期近似；控制限通过马尔可夫链表示EWMA递归，校准至指定受控截断平均运行长度（TARL₀）；通过大因子实验评估平滑常数λ、受控相关系数ρ₀、变异系数(γ_X, γ_Y)、样本量n及漂移幅度τ等参数对检测性能的影响。

Result: 数值结果显示，所提出的EWMA-RZ图在中小漂移检测方面显著优于近期开发的Shewhart型短运行比率图（ShRZ），尤其在|τ−1|≤0.05的漂移范围内优势明显。

Conclusion: EWMA-RZ控制图为短生产周期中比率监控提供了一种更灵敏的统计方法，能有效提升中小漂移的检测能力，并通过饮料灌装过程示例验证了其实用可行性。

Abstract: In many industrial and engineering applications, process performance is characterized by the ratio of two normally distributed quality characteristics. Monitoring such ratios is particularly challenging in short production runs, where conventional control charts often suffer from limited sensitivity due to the small number of available inspections. This paper proposes an exponentially weighted moving average (EWMA) control chart for monitoring the ratio of two normally distributed random variables under short production run (SPR) conditions. The statistical distribution of the ratio is first reviewed, adopting the corrected closed-form density of Nadarajah (2020) rather than the approximation used in earlier studies. The control limit of the proposed chart is calibrated to a prescribed in-control truncated average run length (TARL$ _0 $) over a finite horizon $ I $ of inspections, using a Markov-chain representation of the EWMA recursion. The detection performance of the chart is then assessed through a large factorial study covering the smoothing constant $ λ$, the in-control correlation $ ρ_0 $, the coefficients of variation $ (γ_X, γ_Y) $, the sample size $ n $, and the magnitude of the shift $ τ$. Numerical results show that the proposed EWMA-RZ chart provides substantially better detection of small and moderate shifts than the recently developed Shewhart-type short-run ratio chart (ShRZ) of Tran et al. (2021), especially for $ |τ- 1| \le 0.05 $. An illustrative example based on a beverage filling process is included to demonstrate the practical implementation of the method.

</details>


### [155] [Bentkus-type asymptotic e-values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06332)
*Diego Martinez-Taboada,Ben Chugg,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对渐近e值存在的“缺失因子”导致推断过于保守的问题，基于Bentkus近优集中不等式理论，提出Bentkus型渐近e值，理论证明了其消除了缺失因子，并在事后置信区间宽度和多重检验拒绝率上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近e值虽在事后推断和多重检验中比渐近p值更具优势（允许数据依赖的显著性水平），但现有渐近e值存在“缺失因子”问题，导致缩放效率低下、推断过于保守。该问题限制了渐近e值的实际应用效果。

Method: 借鉴Bentkus在2000年代提出的近优集中不等式框架，构造Bentkus型渐近e值。通过理论推导证明该类e值消除了缺失因子，并采用理论分析和实证对比考察其在事后置信区间和多重检验中的表现。

Result: Bentkus型渐近e值成功消除了缺失因子；相比现有替代方案，其事后置信区间更紧致，多重检验的拒绝率更高，推断结果始终更为锐利。

Conclusion: Bentkus型渐近e值解决了渐近e值的核心效率瓶颈，为事后推断和多重检验提供了一种更优的推断工具，具有显著的理论和实践优势。

Abstract: Asymptotic e-values are emerging as a powerful alternative to asymptotic p-values, particularly in post-hoc inference and multiple testing, where significance levels may be data-dependent. Existing asymptotic e-values, however, suffer from the ``missing factor,'' a scaling inefficiency resulting in overly conservative inference. Drawing on the framework of near-optimal concentration inequalities developed by Bentkus in the 2000s, we introduce Bentkus-type asymptotic e-values and prove that they successfully eliminate the missing factor. We also demonstrate both theoretically and empirically that Bentkus-type e-values consistently deliver sharper inference than existing alternatives, leading to tighter post-hoc confidence intervals and higher rejection rates in multiple testing procedures.

</details>


### [156] [Unified formulas for conditional quantities and transportation functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06346)
*Roberto Vila,Eduardo Nakano,Chang C. Y. Dorea*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于分布导数与狄拉克δ表示的统一概率框架，用于分析条件量和输运相关量。该框架统一了条件期望、条件分布、风险函数等经典概念，并结合copula方法导出Wasserstein距离等输运泛函的尖锐界，应用于泊松、二项、负二项等计数分布的正态逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的条件概率、输运理论、相依性建模等概念通常分别在不同框架下处理，缺乏统一视角。本文旨在通过分布导数和狄拉克δ表示建立统一框架，揭示这些经典概念背后的共同局部化机制，并建立条件结构、相依性建模、离散度度量、正态逼近与最优输运之间的显式联系。

Method: 1) 引入分布导数和狄拉克δ表示，建立适用于任意分布（绝对连续、离散、混合）的统一恒等式；2) 结合copula方法与Fréchet-Hoeffding界和Δ-反序函数的期望不等式，导出绝对差矩在固定边缘分布下的尖锐界；3) 通过上述框架推导Wasserstein距离的分位数表示和上输运泛函，以及广义绝对差矩的生存函数表示与界；4) 应用至Poisson、Binomial、Negative Binomial等计数分布的正态逼近中的Wasserstein型泛函。

Result: 1) 建立了条件期望、条件分布、风险函数、非正常分布的统一公式，揭示了共同的局部化机制；2) 推导了绝对差矩在固定边缘分布下的尖锐界；3) 获得了Wasserstein距离的简洁分位数表示及上输运泛函的表示；4) 得到了广义绝对差矩的生存函数表示与界；5) 作为特例，获得了二元Gini均值差和二元Gini指数的新表示；6) 得到了Poisson、Binomial、Negative Binomial模型的正态逼近中Wasserstein型泛函的显式分位数表示。

Conclusion: 本文建立的统一框架成功连接了条件结构、相依性建模、离散度度量、正态逼近与最优输运等多个概率论与数理统计学中的基础构造，为这些领域提供了统一的视角。该框架具有广泛的适用性，尤其为计数分布的正态逼近问题提供了新的工具和显式表示。

Abstract: This paper develops a unified probabilistic framework based on distributional derivatives and Dirac delta representations for the analysis of conditional and transportation-related quantities. General identities are established for arbitrary random variables, encompassing absolutely continuous, discrete, and mixed distributions. The proposed approach yields unified formulas for conditional expectations, conditional distributions, hazard functions, and improper distributions, revealing a common localization mechanism underlying these classical concepts.
  The framework is further combined with copula methods to investigate transportation and dispersion functionals through dependence structures. Exploiting the extremal properties of the Fréchet--Hoeffding bounds together with expectation inequalities induced by $Δ$-antitonic functions, sharp bounds are derived for absolute difference moments under fixed marginals. These results lead to concise derivations of quantile representations for the Wasserstein distance and a corresponding upper transportation functional, as well as survival-function representations and bounds for generalized absolute difference moments. As a particular case, new representations are obtained for the bivariate Gini mean difference and the associated bivariate Gini index.
  Applications are given to Wasserstein-type functionals arising in the normal approximation of standardized counting distributions, including Poisson, Binomial, and Negative Binomial models, for which explicit quantile representations are derived. Overall, the results establish explicit links among conditional structures, dependence modeling, dispersion measures, normal approximation, and optimal transport, providing a unified perspective on several fundamental constructions in probability and mathematical statistics.

</details>


### [157] [Optimally taming biases in black-box models for efficient semiparametric estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06368)
*Yihong Gu,Qishuo Yin,Tianxi Cai,Jianqing Fan*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究半参数估计中黑箱机器学习估计 nuisance 函数时误差的传播问题。针对部分线性模型，提出新估计器达到 $n^{-1/2}+δ^a_μ+(δ_μ^s)^2$ 的速率，比传统DML更优，证明一阶随机误差可在无额外假设下消除，并提出欠平滑调参策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双机器学习(DML)中nuisance估计误差以乘积形式进入目标参数估计误差界，但这种乘积率依赖是否对黑箱模型最优尚不明确。本文旨在回答这一基础性问题。

Method: 在部分线性模型结构无关设定下，将nuisance函数$μ_0$的估计误差分解为逼近误差$δ^a_μ$和随机误差$δ_μ^s$，提出新估计器并推导其收敛速率，建立匹配下界证明最优性，并扩展至半参数线性泛函估计。

Result: 新估计器达到$n^{-1/2}+δ^a_μ+(δ_μ^s)^2$的速率，在辅助函数$\mathbb{E}[T|X=x]$无法一致估计时显著优于标准DML。证明了nuisance估计的一阶随机误差可在无额外假设下被消除。

Conclusion: 揭示新原理：nuisance估计的一阶随机误差可消除。提出应选择欠平滑策略($δ^a_μ\asymp(δ_μ^s)^2$)而非传统偏差-方差权衡($δ^a_μ\asymp δ_μ^s$)。方法可推广到ATE估计等广泛问题，表明正交评分方法可被显著改进。

Abstract: Modern semiparametric estimation often relies on flexible black-box machine learning methods to estimate nuisance functions, raising a fundamental question: how do nuisance estimation errors propagate into inference for low-dimensional target parameters? The dominant paradigm, exemplified by double machine learning (DML), yields error bounds in which nuisance estimation errors enter multiplicatively. While widely adopted, it remains unclear whether this multiplicative-rate dependence is optimal for black-box models. In this paper, we start by revisiting the partial linear model $Y = μ_0(X)+T\cdotβ_0+\varepsilon$ under a structure-agnostic setting, where the nuisance function $μ_0$ is estimated using a generic machine learning model, with approximation error $δ^a_μ$ and stochastic error $δ_μ^s$. We show that the standard DML rate is not optimal in the regime where the auxiliary function $\mathbb{E}[T|X=x]$ cannot be consistently estimated. We propose a new estimator for $β_0$ that achieves a sharper rate of $n^{-1/2}+δ^a_μ+(δ_μ^s)^2$ and establish a matching lower bound demonstrating its optimality. Our results reveal a new principle: the first-order stochastic error of nuisance estimation can be eliminated without imposing any additional assumptions. This also leads to a revised tuning strategy favoring under-smoothing, where $δ^a_μ\asymp(δ_μ^s)^2$, rather than the classical bias-variance trade-off $δ^a_μ\asymp δ_μ^s$. Under mild additional conditions, the estimator is asymptotically normal with minimal asymptotic variance. The proposed method extends to a broad class of semi-parametric linear functional estimation problems, including average treatment effect estimation. Our results imply that popular orthogonal score methods in semiparametric estimation with black-box nuisance learners can be substantially improved.

</details>


### [158] [Estimation of the sub-Gaussian parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06384)
*Jason Liu,Min Xu,Jinchuan Xing*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了次高斯随机变量的方差代理（子高斯参数）ξ²_*的估计问题。提出基于经验加权累积生成函数约束最大化的自然估计量，证明了相合性并给出了收敛速率。在L存在最大值点时为O_p(n^{-1/2+ε})，若argmax有界则改进为O_p(n^{-1/2})。通过极小化极大下界证明了假设的必要性，并展示了估计量在非次高斯分布下发散的特性。最后应用于基因本体论富集研究中的置换检验p值计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次高斯随机变量在概率论与统计中应用广泛，但其方差代理参数ξ²_*的估计问题尚未得到充分研究，现有方法缺乏系统的理论保证。

Method: 基于经验加权累积生成函数L_n(λ)的约束最大化构造估计量；通过经验过程理论推导相合性与收敛速率；利用极小化极大分析建立下界；将估计量应用于GO富集分析的大规模置换检验。

Result: 估计量相合；L有最大值点时速率O_p(n^{-1/2+ε})；argmax有界时改进为O_p(n^{-1/2})；极小化极大风险下界为Ω(1)，随尾部分布条件增强在Ω(1/log n)到Ω(1)之间插值；非次高斯分布下估计量发散；在GO富集分析中作为峰值超阈值方法的可靠替代。

Conclusion: 建立了次高斯参数估计的理论框架，给出了不同条件下的最优收敛速率，证明了方法的实际有效性，特别是在峰值超阈值方法可靠存疑的场景中具有优势。

Abstract: The sub-Gaussian parameter (also called the variance proxy) of a mean-zero random variable $X$ is defined as $ξ^2_* = \sup_{λ\in \mathbb{R}} L(λ)$ where $L(λ) = \frac{2}{λ^2} \log \mathbb{E} e^{λX}$ is a weighted cumulant generating function. Despite the ubiquity of sub-Gaussian random variables, the estimation of $ξ^2_*$ has received little attention and is not yet well understood. In this work, we study a natural estimator of $ξ^2_*$ based on constrained maximization of the empirical analogue of $L$. We prove that the estimator is consistent bound the rates of convergence under assumptions on $L$: if $L$ has an maximizer, then our bound is $O_p(n^{-1/2 + \varepsilon})$ for any $\varepsilon > 0$; if the argmax of $L$ is also bounded, then the bound improves to $O_p(n^{-1/2})$. We show that our assumptions on $L$ are necessary by proving that the minimax risk over all sub-Gaussian distributions is $Ω(1)$; imposing increasingly strong assumptions on the tail growth of $L$ yields a continuum of classes whose minimax lower bound interpolates between $Ω(1/\log n)$ and $Ω(1)$. Root-n rate is possible if we restrict to a subclass of distributions where $L$ attains its supremum in a bounded region, in which case our estimator is minimax optimal. If the underlying distribution is not sub-Gaussian, we show that our estimator goes to infinity with a divergence rate controlled by the tail of the distribution. Finally, we apply our estimator in a Gene Ontology (GO) enrichment study to construct p-values for a large-scale permutation test, showing that it can serve as a reliable alternative to the peaks-over-threshold approach, particularly in regimes where the peaks-over-threshold method is of uncertain validity.

</details>


### [159] [How abundant are good interpolators?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06469)
*August Y. Chen,Ahmed El Alaoui*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文在高维比例极限下，研究了随机均匀选择的插值线性分类器的泛化误差的大偏差原理，发现几乎所有插值分类器的泛化性能趋于一致，且梯度下降和线性规划等高效算法显著优于随机插值器，体现了良性过拟合现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维过参数化场景下，随机均匀选择的插值分类器的泛化性能分布，以及高效算法为何能实现良性过拟合。

Method: 在两种数据生成分布（高斯混合模型和逻辑斯蒂模型）下，在比例极限 n/d → α 且 α 足够小的条件下，建立了随机均匀选择的插值分类器泛化误差的大偏差原理，并求解了速率函数的最大化问题。

Result: 所有指数级小比例之外的插值分类器具有几乎相同的泛化性能，该性能由速率函数的唯一最大值给出；梯度下降和线性规划找到的插值器的性能显著优于绝大多数随机插值器。

Conclusion: 在过参数化的小 α 区域，高效算法选择的插值器并非典型，而是具有特异的良性过拟合性质，远超随机插值器的平均表现。

Abstract: Let $S$ be the set of unit norm linear classifiers $θ\in \mathbb{R}^d$ which correctly classify every point of a labeled dataset $(X_i,y_i)_{i=1}^n$, $X_i \in \mathbb{R}^d$, $y_i \in \{-1,+1\}$, with a possibly negative margin $κ$ fixed in advance. Under two natural data-generating distributions of the $(X,y)$ pairs -- a Gaussian mixture model and a logistic model with Gaussian features -- and in the proportional regime $n/d \to α$ with small enough $α$, we establish a large deviation principle on the event that a point $θ$ chosen uniformly at random from $S$ achieves a given generalization error, with high probability over the choice of the data. The associated large deviation rate function is deterministic and describes the proportion, at the exponential scale in $d$, of interpolating classifiers having a given desired performance. As a consequence, we establish the following concentration phenomenon: all but an exponentially small fraction of interpolating classifiers have approximately the same generalization performance given by the unique maximizer of this rate function.
  We numerically compare this maximizer to the performance of empirical risk minimization by gradient descent and to the performance of a natural linear program, both finding a point in $S$, and deduce that in the overparametrized regime of small $α$, these efficient procedures outperform the vast majority of interpolators, pointing to their nontrivial benign overfitting in this setting.

</details>


### [160] [Statistically and Computationally Optimal Estimation and Inference of Common Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06483)
*Joshua Agterberg*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了多个低秩矩阵共享公共子空间的统计与计算极限，提出了基于投影梯度下降的估计器，并刻画了弱、中、强估计和强推断四种信噪比区间下的最优误差率、渐近正态性与自适应置信区间，揭示了在估计计算可行时推断仍可能信息论不可行的新现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多数据矩阵中公共子空间估计的统计与计算极限问题，明确不同信噪比区间下估计和推断的最优性边界。

Method: 采用谱和平方初始化后的投影梯度下降估计器，结合统计与计算下界分析，以及渐近正态性推导和自适应置信区间构造。

Result: 在强估计信噪比下达到最优sinΘ误差率；在强推断信噪比下估计量渐近正态，置信区间自适应极小最优；弱/中信噪比下估计不可行或计算困难；强推断信噪比以下自适应推断信息论不可行。

Conclusion: 揭示了估计与推断在信噪比需求上的本质差异：即使信噪比超过估计的计算极限，自适应推断仍可能信息论不可行。

Abstract: Given multiple data matrices, many problems in statistics and data science rely on estimating a common subspace that captures certain structure shared by all the data matrices. In this paper we investigate the statistical and computational limits for the common subspace model in which one observes a collection of symmetric low-rank matrices perturbed by noise, where each low-rank matrix shares the same common subspace. Our main results identify several regimes of the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) such that estimation and inference are statistically or computationally optimal, and we refer to these regimes as weak SNR, moderate SNR, strong estimation SNR, and strong inference SNR. First, we propose an estimator based on projected gradient descent initialized via spectral sum of squares and show that it achieves the optimal $\sinΘ$ error rate under strong estimation SNR. These results are complemented by both statistical and computational lower bounds identifying the weak and moderate estimation SNR regimes. Next, we turn to statistical inference for the $\sinΘ$ distance itself, and we show that our estimator has an asymptotically Gaussian distribution in the strong inference SNR regime. Based on this limiting result we propose confidence intervals and show that they are adaptively minimax optimal in the strong inference SNR regime, where adaptivity is measured in terms of the SNR. Finally, we show that adaptive confidence intervals are information-theoretically impossible below the strong inference SNR regime. Consequently, our results unveil a novel phenomenon: despite the SNR being ``above'' the computational limit for estimation, adaptive statistical inference may still be information-theoretically impossible.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [The Evaluation Blind Spot: A Stereological Theory of Benchmark Coverage for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05169)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM基准覆盖的体视学理论，发现主流排行榜的有效维度d_eff在2.86-4.80之间，结构盲区比实际分数差距大两个数量级；通过子模贪心算法可找到4个稳定核心基准，7/12即可达到90%覆盖；同时独立解决了Gardner问题1.5，建立了C^2支撑函数的最优恢复 minimax 率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准测试缺乏系统的覆盖理论，无法量化基准集对模型能力的表征完整性，存在评估盲区。

Method: 体视学理论框架、有效维度估计、chi-squared投影模型、子模贪心算法（含Nemhauser (1-1/e)保证）、最优恢复理论。

Result: 三个独立排行榜d_eff∈[2.86,4.80]；结构盲区比分差大两个数量级（52-127倍统计噪声）；4个基准组成稳定核心，7/12达90%覆盖；跨季度迁移保留93-97%；特征结构可预测基准可替代性（ρ=-0.69, p=0.013）；解决了Gardner问题1.5，得到minimax率Θ(R/(κ m^{2/(D-1)}))。

Conclusion: LLM基准覆盖存在显著结构盲区，可通过低维有效维度和子模选择构建高效稳定的评估集；理论结果可迁移到一般凸几何的最优恢复问题。

Abstract: We give a stereological theory of LLM benchmark coverage. For any suite with effective dimensionality d_eff, the visible Hausdorff distance between two convex capability profiles consistent with the same scores is bounded by epsilon + C R m^(-1/(d_eff-1)), with matching Lipschitz lower bound. Empirically, three independent leaderboards (Open LLM v2, an extended 12-benchmark suite, LiveBench) all have d_eff in [2.86, 4.80] on their competitive frontier; the structural blind spot exceeds the observed runner-up score gap by two orders of magnitude and dominates statistical noise by 52-127x. Under a chi-squared projection model, the isotropic prior is the optimistic case; across six hidden-capability priors and four ambient dimensions the simulated half-split swap rate of the top two models stays in [0.38, 0.49], and a 500-trial random visible/held-out split shows that 92% of trials swap the top-1 ranking with on average 2.83 of 5 top-5 models changing. A submodular greedy algorithm with the Nemhauser (1 - 1/e) guarantee finds a stable core of 4 benchmarks; 7 of 12 suffice for 90% coverage, and the trained subset transfers across temporal quarters with 93-97% retention. A counterfactual validation across 12 internal benchmarks and 27 Chatbot Arena categories confirms that the eigenstructure predicts which evaluations are irreplaceable (rho = -0.69, p = 0.013 for removal disruption) and which external evaluations bring new information (rho = +0.38). As a second, independent theoretical contribution, we resolve Gardner's Problem 1.5 (1995) for C^2 support functions, establishing the minimax rate Theta(R/(kappa m^(2/(D-1)))) in general dimension via optimal recovery theory on S^(D-1).

</details>


### [162] [ERRORQUAKE: Heavy-Tailed Error Severity Distributions in Open-Weight Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05170)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Errorquake-10k基准，以连续0-4严重性评分替代传统标量错误率，发现不同开源LLM在相同准确率下错误严重性分布存在显著差异，证明严重性分布与错误率信息非冗余，应作为独立指标报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统幻觉基准将所有错误视为等价（如错误日期与捏造法庭裁决严重性相差数个数量级），仅报告单一错误率无法捕捉模型在错误严重性分布上的差异，缺乏对错误严重性细粒度的量化评估方法。

Method: 构建Errorquake-10k基准（10k查询、8领域、5难度层级），采用0-4连续严重性评分；为21个开源模型拟合Gutenberg-Richter上尾斜率b作为严重性分布指标；通过519项三评价者人类验证研究确认可靠性（ICC=0.85），证明LLM裁判排名与人类一致（rho=0.89）；提出非归约性定理证明严重性分布与错误率信息非冗余；建立严重性机制分类学（kappa=0.83）。

Result: 210个模型对中有85对在匹配准确率下具有不相交的95%b置信区间；严重性分布与错误率信息非冗余（I(b; model | epsilon)=1.56 bits，64.5%的b方差无法被epsilon解释）；低严重性错误71%为检索错误，高严重性错误39%为捏造，且模型大小显著影响此构成（p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: 严重性分布应作为准确率之外的独立指标报告，它携带错误率无法提供的判别性信息。

Abstract: At matched accuracy, open-weight LLMs differ substantially in the shape of their error severity distribution -- a difference invisible to the scalar error rate. Hallucination benchmarks report a single error count and treat all errors as equivalent, yet a wrong date and a fabricated court ruling differ by orders of magnitude. We introduce Errorquake-10k, a 10,000-query benchmark scoring each response on a continuous 0-4 severity scale across 8 domains and 5 difficulty tiers, and we fit per-model severity distributions for 21 open-weight models. For each model we estimate a severity distribution index (b, the Gutenberg-Richter upper-tail slope) with 95% bootstrap confidence intervals. Headline: across the 210 model pairs, 85 have disjoint 95% b confidence intervals at matched accuracy (|Delta epsilon| < 0.05) on human-consensus scoring, e.g. deepseek-v3.2 vs. ministral-14b at epsilon = 0.586 and Delta b = 0.47. A 519-item three-rater human validation study confirms measurement reliability (ICC(2,k=3) = 0.85), validates the LLM-judge ranking (rho = 0.89), and confirms the dense-model scaling correlation on human data (rho_s = -0.86). We prove a Non-Reducibility Theorem showing that severity profile and error rate are informationally non-redundant (I(b; model | epsilon) = 1.56 bits; 64.5% of cross-model b variance is unexplained by epsilon). A severity mechanism taxonomy (kappa = 0.83) reveals that error type shifts categorically with severity: low-severity errors are retrievals (71%); high-severity errors are fabrications (39%) -- and this composition differs by model size (p < 0.0001). Severity distribution should be reported alongside accuracy; it carries discriminative information that the error rate cannot.

</details>


### [163] [Staged Factorial Screening for Budget-Constrained Micro-Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05186)
*Felipe Chavarro Polania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究预算受限的微预训练场景下，采用分阶段分数因子设计工作流在共享加速器上对候选配方进行筛选。通过613次实验，证实短时设计屏幕可识别高惩罚方向，确认有前景的锚点，并在缩减空间内局部优化。证据支持以桥接为中心的24小时双主机推荐，而非硬件不变的排名或通用超参数优化优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预算受限的微预训练中，通常需要在共享加速器上筛选大量候选配方，之后再投入更大搜索预算。现有方法缺乏对分阶段分数因子设计能否在该场景下恢复稳定的早期效应结构的研究。

Method: 在固定单GPU训练循环上，运行613次实验，包括不同时长（2、5、10分钟）的试点和随访屏幕、全因子种子重跑、锚点检查、贪婪和随机基线、60分钟桥接包、以及24小时内两种GPU架构的锚点延续实验。采用Benjamini-Hochberg校正进行多重假设检验。

Result: 总批量、深度和宽度的主惩罚在短预算时最大，随预算增加而减弱；在预声明的全屏幕系列中，D、A、B、C在5和10分钟经校正后保留非零估计，而E不显著；随机搜索可在32条件空间中达到强候选项，但反复落在同一低惩罚区域且无法归因因子；60分钟桥接锚点均值最低，但无法分离工作流改进与模型容量优势；24小时三锚点延续中桥接样本均值最低，非桥接排序对宿主敏感。

Conclusion: 推荐使用短时设计屏幕识别高惩罚方向，在重复运行下确认有前景锚点，并在缩减空间内局部优化。证据支持桥接中心推荐在24小时内两个宿主的适用性，而非硬件无关的排名或通用超参数优化优越性。

Abstract: Budget-constrained micro-pretraining often requires triaging many candidate recipes on a shared accelerator before larger search budgets are spent. We study whether a staged fractional-factorial workflow can recover stable early effect structure in this setting. On a fixed autoresearch-derived single-GPU training loop, we run 613 experiments across pilot and follow-up screens at 2, 5, and 10 minutes; full 16-condition seeded reruns at 5 and 10 minutes; targeted seeded anchor checks; same-host greedy and matched-cost random baselines; a 60-minute bridge package; and bounded Windows A100 and Linux L40S anchor continuations through 24 hours. Main penalties from total batch, depth, and width are largest at short budgets and relax as budget increases. Within the predeclared seeded full-screen families, D, A, B, and C retain non-zero estimates at 5 and 10 minutes after within-budget Benjamini-Hochberg correction, while E does not. Random search can reach strong incumbents in this 32-condition space, but repeatedly in the same low-penalty region and without factor attribution. The 60-minute bridge anchor has the lowest mean, although that package does not separate workflow refinement from the larger bridge model's capacity advantage. In bounded 12-hour and 24-hour three-anchor continuations on both hosts, the bridge has the lowest sample mean while the non-bridge ordering stays host-sensitive. We therefore present a bounded methods result: use short designed screens to identify high-penalty directions, confirm promising anchors under repeated runs, and refine locally inside the reduced space. The evidence supports a bridge-centered recommendation through 24 hours on two hosts, not hardware-invariant ranking or general hyperparameter-optimization superiority.

</details>


### [164] [PyCC.id: A package for hypothesis-driven equation discovery with structural identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05191)
*Federico J. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PyCC库，一种基于特征曲线（CC）骨架假设驱动的方法，用于从时间序列数据中发现ODE，通过整合领域知识先验来缓解逆问题的不适定性，并支持多种方程发现范式（神经网络、符号回归、稀疏回归）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动方程发现中的逆问题通常是不适定的，会产生多个拟合数据的候选模型。现有方法虽通过引入先验假设缩小了搜索空间，但仍需人工后验筛选。本文旨在提供一个灵活的工具，将结构骨架与领域知识结合，系统化地解决这一问题。

Method: 提出基于特征曲线（CC）的骨架方法，允许用户定义与ODE族相关的结构骨架，并迭代添加领域知识先验来精化模型。该方法具有模块化特性，可集成神经网络、符号回归和稀疏回归等多种方程发现范式。PyCC库实现了这一框架。

Result: PyCC库作为灵活工具，使研究者能够无缝定义骨架和假设，从时间序列数据中发现ODE。部分骨架具有可证明的结构可辨识性，可用于验证或剔除候选模型。

Conclusion: PyCC库将基于特征曲线骨架的假设驱动方法工程化为实用工具，为数据驱动ODE发现提供了灵活、可扩展的解决方案，有效缓解了逆问题的不适定性。

Abstract: Data-driven equation discovery is fundamentally an inverse problem that seeks to infer the governing differential equations of a system directly from time-series measurements. A known issue is the ill-conditioned nature of the inverse problem, which frequently produces multiple mathematical models that fit the data similarly well. One path to address this issue is by incorporating known hypotheses and constraints into the training phase beforehand. While this approach effectively reduces the search space, it still results in multiple candidate models, forcing practitioners to rely on post-hoc manual filtering based on their own domain expertise. A recent approach incorporates structural `skeletons' inspired by characteristic curves (CCs), defining a hypothesis-driven methodology. In this methodology, practitioners define a skeleton, which is associated with a family of ordinary differential equations (ODEs), and then add their hypotheses and priors based on their domain knowledge to refine the obtained model iteratively. An important advantage of this approach is that some skeletons have demonstrable structural identifiability properties, which are useful for checking whether the skeleton is correct or should be discarded. Furthermore, this formalism enables the use of multiple equation discovery paradigms due to its modularity (such as neural networks, symbolic regression, and sparse regression). In this work, we present the Python library PyCC, which condenses these efforts into a flexible tool that allows researchers and engineers to seamlessly define their skeletons and hypotheses to discover ODEs from time-dependent data.

</details>


### [165] [Temporal Preference Concepts and their Functions in a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05194)
*Ian Rios-Sialer,Shantanu Darveshi,Shuai Jiang,Avigya Paudel,Anastasiia Pronina,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过因果定位方法在精简版LLM中发现了处理时间偏好的子图结构，揭示了模型对未来折扣率远低于人类，且这种偏好不稳定，可通过激活干预进行调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在需要权衡短期收益与长期后果的决策中日益重要，但其内部如何表征和解决这种时间权衡尚不清楚，因此需要理解其时间偏好的内部机制。

Method: 利用梯度归因和激活修补的收敛证据，在Qwen3-4B-Instruct精简版中因果定位了时间偏好的底层子图，分析了残差流中时间跨度的几何编码，并通过行为分析和激活干预进行验证。

Result: 识别出中层到上层节点构成的时间偏好子图；未干预的LLM对未来折扣率比人类低数倍，但该偏好跨上下文不稳定；发现引导向量可改变时间偏好。

Conclusion: 揭示了LLM时间偏好的神经基础，证明了机械可解释性有助于实现对LLM规划与推理的可靠控制，建议采用显式控制而非隐式依赖训练。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed to make decisions that require trading off near-term gains against long-term consequences, yet little is known about how they internally represent or resolve these tradeoffs. In this work, we causally localize an underlying subgraph for temporal preference in a distilled LLM (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), identifying mid-to-upper-layer nodes through converging evidence from gradient-based attribution and activation patching. We find that the geometry of time horizon is encoded in the residual stream at the expected localized layers. A behavioral analysis reveals that unintervened LLMs discount the future several times less steeply than humans, yet this preference is unstable across contexts, motivating explicit control rather than implicit reliance on training. Finally, we find suggestive evidence that steering vectors can shift temporal preference. Our work demonstrates how mechanistic interpretability can bring us closer to reliable control over how LLMs plan and reason

</details>


### [166] [State commitment learning: training language models to distinguish computation from memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05201)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出状态承诺学习（SCL）和反事实擦除强化学习（CERL），训练推理语言模型区分持久状态与临时计算，通过擦除隐藏推理路径后仍保持答案正确来减少对临时计算的依赖，在数学、长链逻辑、科学问答和多轮工具使用任务中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理语言模型将计算用的临时token与持久状态token不加区分，所有隐藏思考（包括失败路径、死胡同、私有草稿）都留在上下文中影响后续预测，导致下游推理可能依赖不应被可靠使用的临时信息。

Method: 1. 提出状态承诺学习（State Commitment Learning）训练目标，要求模型显式区分应提交为持久状态的信息与可丢弃的临时计算；2. 定义反事实准则——持久状态充分性（Persistent-State Sufficiency），使答案在隐藏思考被擦除后仍可用；3. 提出CERL，对同一前缀同时评估保留隐藏思考路径和擦除路径，仅当擦除路径正确时给予奖励。

Result: 在数学、长链逻辑、科学QA和多轮工具使用评估中，CERL显著降低了答案对隐藏思考的依赖，同时不牺牲准确性，持续优于仅正确性RL和长答案SFT基线。

Conclusion: 通过显式训练模型区分临时计算与持久状态，可以有效减少推理对隐藏思考的依赖，提升模型输出的可靠性和可解释性，且不影响最终性能。

Abstract: Reasoning language models do not distinguish tokens used for computation from tokens that constitute persistent state: once generated, all hidden thoughts remain in context and influence future predictions. As a result, downstream reasoning may depend on failed attempts, dead ends, and private scratch work that should not be safely relied on later. We recast this phenomenon as a new training objective, state commitment learning: training models to explicitly distinguish information that should be committed as persistent state from temporary computation that can be discarded. We define a counterfactual criterion, persistent-state sufficiency, which makes it trainable and measurable whether an answer remains usable after hidden thoughts are erased. We then propose Counterfactual Erasure RL (CERL), which evaluates, under the same prefix, both a path that keeps hidden thoughts and a path that erases them, and gives reward only when the erasure path remains correct. We also introduce the Erasure Dependence Protocol and show across mathematics, long-chain logic, scientific QA, and multi-turn tool-use evaluation that CERL substantially reduces answer dependence on hidden thoughts without sacrificing accuracy, consistently outperforming correctness-only RL and long-answer SFT baselines.

</details>


### [167] [Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Restores Symmetry in Deep Linear Networks with Multi-Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05219)
*Hee-Sung Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究深度线性网络中的多路径专业化现象，发现大步长离散梯度下降（GD）会导致与梯度流（GF）不同的结果：GF预测"赢家通吃"式的单路径专业化，而大步长GD在"稳定边界"震荡后会重新平衡，使信号分布到多条路径上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度流（GF）的理论预测深度线性网络中会出现单路径专业化（winner-takes-all），但实际训练中观察到的现象与之不符。本文旨在揭示离散梯度下降（GD）与大步长如何改变这一结论。

Method: 理论证明单路径解是尖锐极小值，而跨路径分布信号能降低锐度（sharpness），且降低幅度随路径数量和深度增加而增大；通过分析"稳定边界"（Edge of Stability）上的震荡行为，解释信号再平衡的机制。

Result: 早期训练确实再现了GF预测的深度驱动对称性破缺，但随后稳定边界上的震荡会覆盖此趋势，驱动网络进入再平衡阶段，使信号重新分布到多条路径上。

Conclusion: 深度影响路径竞争方式，大步长GD倾向于共享表示而非持久性的单路径主导，这解释了深度线性网络中多路径表示的成因。

Abstract: Recent analyses of multi-pathway Deep Linear Networks use Gradient Flow to predict a "winner-takes-all" specialization in which path symmetry breaks and each feature concentrates in a single pathway. In this work, we show that discrete Gradient Descent (GD) with a large step size tells a different story. We prove that single-path solutions are sharp minima, whereas distributing signals across pathways reduces sharpness by a factor that decreases with both the number of pathways and depth. Consequently, while early training reproduces the depth-driven symmetry breaking predicted by GF, oscillations at the Edge of Stability subsequently override this tendency and drive the network into a re-balancing phase, where signals redistribute across pathways. Together, these results clarify how depth shapes pathway competition and explain why large-step GD favors shared representations rather than persistent single-pathway dominance.

</details>


### [168] [Differentiable Efficient Operator Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05232)
*Xiaohuan Pei,Jiyuan Zhang,Yuanfan Guo,Weiguo Feng,Tao Huang,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高效算子搜索（EOS）框架，将多种手动设计的视觉token缩减算子（剪枝、合并、池化、自适应重加权）统一到共享算子空间，并通过可微分方式联合搜索缩减位置、保留数量和信息处理方式，在多模态基准上实现竞争性的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态基础模型依赖人工设计的token缩减算子，这些算子虽然形式各异，但缺乏统一的视角和自动化的优化方法。

Method: 提出可微分的高效算子搜索框架，统一参数化层激活、保留预算和算子行为，在单边预算和成本约束下优化任务性能。

Result: 该框架能恢复代表性手工基线作为特例，并发现超越孤立手工设计的混合算子；在多模态基准上，尤其在高比例视觉token缩减下达到竞争性的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 高效多模态推理可以从手工算子设计转向可微分算子搜索。

Abstract: Efficient multimodal foundation models often rely on manually designed token-reduction operators, such as pruning, merging, pooling, and adaptive reweighting. Although these operators appear different, we show that they can be interpreted as distinct regimes of a shared operator space. Based on this view, we introduce Efficient Operator Search, a differentiable framework that jointly searches where to reduce tokens, how many tokens to retain, and how reduced token information should be processed. The proposed search space parameterizes layer activation, retention budget, and operator behavior, while the search policy optimizes task performance under one-sided budget and cost constraints. This formulation recovers representative hand-designed baselines as special cases and further discovers hybrid operators beyond isolated manual designs. Experiments on multimodal benchmarks show that the searched operators achieve competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs, especially under aggressive visual-token reduction. These results suggest that efficient multimodal inference can be reframed from manual operator design to differentiable operator search.

</details>


### [169] [DiffSlack: Learning under Nonlinear Inequality Constraints via Learnable Slack Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05247)
*Ziqian Wang,Chenxi Fang,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffSlack提出了一种可微投影层，通过可学习的松弛变量将非线性不等式约束转化为等式约束，结合阻尼高斯-牛顿投影实现端到端可微的硬约束满足，在车辆路径规划（200个非线性约束）中取得了更高的成功率和约束满足度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络硬约束方法在应对大量耦合的非线性不等式约束时存在结构限制或计算开销大的问题，亟需一种可扩展且实用的硬约束嵌入方法。

Method: 1) 将不等式约束重写为带可学习松弛变量的等式约束；2) 松弛变量作为网络增强输出的一部分，为阻尼高斯-牛顿投影提供数据驱动的热启动；3) 投影层将原始预测映射到增强可行流形上，保持端到端可微性；4) 两阶段课程学习稳定训练并改善约束满足。

Result: 在含200个非线性约束（碰撞避免、曲率限制、路径点间距）的车辆路径规划任务中，相比现有基线方法，DiffSlack在相当推理预算下实现了更高的规划成功率和更严格的几何约束满足；消融实验表明硬投影层降低了对监督质量的敏感性；CARLA闭环跟踪和实车实验验证了轨迹的可执行性。

Conclusion: DiffSlack为工程应用中向神经网络嵌入硬不等式约束提供了一种实用且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Enforcing nonlinear inequality constraints in neural networks remains challenging, especially when the output is subject to many coupled constraints. Existing hard constraint methods often impose structural restrictions on the constraint set or introduce substantial computational overhead for large-scale nonlinear problems. Here, we propose DiffSlack, a differentiable projection layer for nonlinear inequality-constrained neural prediction. DiffSlack reformulates inequalities as equalities with learnable slack variables, which are predicted as part of the augmented network output and provide a data-driven warm start for damped Gauss-Newton projection. The projection layer maps raw predictions onto the augmented feasible manifold while preserving end-to-end differentiability. A two-stage curriculum further stabilizes training and improves constraint satisfaction. We evaluate DiffSlack on vehicle path planning with 200 nonlinear inequality constraints from collision avoidance, curvature limits, and waypoint spacing. Compared with existing learning-based baselines, DiffSlack achieves a higher planning success rate and stronger geometric constraint satisfaction under a comparable inference budget. Ablation studies further show that the hard projection layer reduces sensitivity to supervision quality. Closed-loop tracking in CARLA and real-world vehicle experiments confirms the executability of the generated trajectories. These results demonstrate that DiffSlack provides a practical and scalable approach to embedding hard inequality constraints into neural networks for engineering applications.

</details>


### [170] [Alpha-RTL: Test-Time Training for RTL Hardware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05253)
*Peilong Zhou,Zhirong Chen,Cangyuan Li,Haoyu Gao,Kaiyan Chang,Ziming Qu,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TTT-RTL框架，首个在测试时通过EDA反馈对LLM策略进行每设计训练的RTL优化方法，通过PUCT索引状态池、熵策略梯度和自适应KL预算控制，在45nm和Sky130工艺上分别实现65.1%和59.4%的PPA/ADP降低，显著优于冻结策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成RTL的方法要么训练通用生成器后部署，要么使用冻结策略进行测试时搜索，无法在测试时根据具体RTL问题的EDA反馈自适应优化策略。

Method: 1) 采样候选实现并通过语法检查和仿真验证；2) 使用综合导出的PPA积对有效设计评分；3) 通过PUCT索引的设计状态池重用高奖励变体；4) 使用熵策略梯度目标更新策略；5) 引入自适应KL预算控制器，利用参考KL、有效样本量和奖励饱和信号调整熵约束以稳定策略更新。

Result: 在RTLLM v2.0 (Nangate 45nm)下，几何平均PPA积降低65.1%，优于最强冻结策略基线（26.1%）。在XuanTie C910 FPU领先零预测单元 (Sky130)下，实现59.4%的ADP降低。消融实验证实策略自适应、状态重用和KL预算控制均有贡献。

Conclusion: 测试时训练与可执行EDA反馈的结合能够将LLM基础的RTL生成从功能正确性推进到物理优化的硬件设计。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown increasing promise in generating
  functionally correct register-transfer-level (RTL) hardware designs.
  Recent systems improve further through EDA-integrated reinforcement
  learning with syntax, simulation, and PPA rewards, but train a general
  RTL generator before deployment while test-time approaches search with
  a frozen policy. We instead perform reinforcement learning at test time,
  allowing the LLM policy to adapt to executable EDA feedback for the
  specific RTL problem at hand. We propose TTT-RTL, to our knowledge the
  first per-design test-time training framework that closes the loop
  between an LLM policy and an EDA pipeline for RTL optimization. TTT-RTL
  samples candidate implementations, verifies them through syntax checking
  and simulation, scores valid designs using synthesis-derived PPA product,
  reuses high-reward variants through a PUCT-indexed design-state pool,
  and updates the policy with an entropic policy-gradient objective. To
  stabilize policy updates under sparse or plateaued rewards, we introduce
  an adaptive KL-budget controller that adjusts the entropy constraint
  using reference KL, effective sample size, and reward saturation signals.
  On RTLLM v2.0 under Nangate 45nm, TTT-RTL reduces the geometric-mean
  PPA product by 65.1% over the reference, outperforming the strongest
  published frozen-policy agent baseline at 26.1%. On an industrial
  XuanTie C910 FPU leading-zero-anticipation unit under Sky130, TTT-RTL
  achieves a 59.4% ADP reduction, and ablations confirm that policy
  adaptation, state reuse, and KL-budget control each contribute. These
  results suggest that test-time training with executable EDA feedback can
  move LLM-based RTL generation beyond functional correctness toward
  physically optimized hardware.

</details>


### [171] [Flash-WAM: Modality-Aware Distillation for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05254)
*Arman Akbari,Ci Zhang,Arash Akbari,Lin Zhao,Yixiao Chen,Weiwei Chen,Xuan Zhang,Geng Yuan,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Flash-WAM，一种面向世界-动作模型（WAM）的模态感知步蒸馏框架，通过为视频和动作流分别设计匹配其噪声 regime 的一致性函数参数化（线性梯度缩放+方差保持），将推理压缩到单步，实现23倍加速（8.1秒→348毫秒），在保持任务成功率的同时支持实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型WAM需要数十步去噪推理，延迟过高无法用于实时控制；现有的步蒸馏方法无法处理视频和动作流使用不同SNR偏移噪声调度导致的非对称边际噪声分布问题。

Method: 基于一致性蒸馏的模态感知步蒸馏框架Flash-WAM：通过结构分析一致性函数族，为动作流的低噪声区设计线性梯度缩放参数化，为视频流的高噪声区设计方差保持参数化，使每模态只需单步推理。

Result: 在LingBot-VA上实现单步推理；RoboTwin 2.0上每块延迟从8.1秒降至348毫秒（23倍加速）；模拟基准任务成功率85.5%（RoboTwin 2.0）和95.7%（LIBERO）；真实Unitree G1人形机器人上平均成功率60%，而简单一致性蒸馏在相同步数下仅24%。

Conclusion: Flash-WAM有效解决了多模态步蒸馏中因不同噪声调度导致的非对称噪声分布问题，在极大加速WAM推理的同时保留了任务性能，为实时机器人控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) jointly generate future video and robot actions through iterative diffusion, achieving strong performance on manipulation benchmarks but requiring tens of denoising steps, a cost that precludes real-time control. Step distillation has emerged as the natural remedy, but off-the-shelf methods break down in the joint video-action setting because video and action streams use different SNR-shifted noise schedules and reach training with substantially different marginal noise distributions, an asymmetry that single-modality distillation methods cannot accommodate. We introduce \textbf{Flash-WAM}, a modality-aware step-distillation framework inspired by consistency distillation that selects the consistency function for each modality to match its noise regime: a linear-gradient-scaling parametrization for the action stream's low-noise regime, paired with a variance-preserving parametrization for the video stream's high-noise regime, grounded in a structural analysis of the consistency-function family that characterizes the achievable gradient scaling under the consistency boundary condition. Instantiated on LingBot-VA, Flash-WAM compresses inference to a single step in each modality. On RoboTwin 2.0, this reduces per-chunk latency from $8.1$ seconds to $348$ ms on NVIDIA L40S, a $23{\times}$ speedup that enables real-time inference. Flash-WAM preserves task success on simulation benchmarks ($85.5\%$ RoboTwin 2.0, $95.7\%$ LIBERO) and substantially recovers real-world performance ($60\%$ average on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot), while naive consistency distillation drops to $24\%$ at the same step budget.

</details>


### [172] [Scaling Laws for Behavioral Foundation Models over User Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05257)
*Rickard Brüel Gabrielsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究行为基础模型（behavioral foundation models）的计算最优缩放规律，发现小嵌入器（约2%参数）是计算最优的，且训练目标与评估指标之间的不一致性会随计算量缩放，评价指标本身应被视为缩放规律的一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基础模型越来越多地用于用户行为序列（推荐、支付、欺诈、电商），但缺乏类似语言模型中缩放规律（scaling laws）那样的计算校准（compute calibration）指导。

Method: 对约600次真实交互数据实验，在$10^{15}$-$10^{19}$训练FLOPs范围内，联合变化四个部署相关轴：两部分的参数分配、关键批量大小、模型/数据分配以及冻结嵌入器后的负采样数量。

Result: 小嵌入器（约2%参数）在所有计算预算下均为计算最优；计算最优训练在低计算量时数据比文本更多，但随计算量增加向Chinchilla启发式趋近；训练目标与评估指标的不一致随计算量缩放；更大预算下负采样偏好更多负样本。

Conclusion: 在行为基础模型中，评估指标本身就是缩放规律的一部分：改变评估指标会改变计算最优配方。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly trained on sequences of user actions in recommendation, payments, fraud, and commerce, but these models still lack the kind of compute calibration that scaling laws provide for language models. We study a common two-part behavioral-model architecture: a feature-based event embedder maps each multi-modal item to a vector, and a decoder-only transformer predicts the next event from the resulting sequence. Across roughly 600 runs on real interaction data, spanning $10^{15}$-$10^{19}$ training FLOPs, we jointly vary four deployment-relevant axes: the two-part parameter split, critical batch size, model/data allocation, and the number of sampled negatives used after freezing the embedder. A small embedder ($s^{\star}\!\approx\!2\%$ of parameters) is compute-optimal at every budget we test because embedder parameters are both more expensive per step and exposed to far more repeated items than contextualizer parameters. Compute-optimal training is data-heavy relative to text at low compute, but its $D/N$ ratio moves toward the Chinchilla heuristic as compute increases. The sampled training objective and deployed ranking metrics disagree in ways that themselves scale: critical batch size, optimal negative count after freezing, and the agreement between loss and ranking quality all shift with compute and with the chosen evaluation metric. For negative sampling, larger budgets increasingly prefer more negatives; by $10^{19}$ FLOPs the active constraint is candidate-axis memory rather than FLOPs. In behavioral foundation models, the evaluation metric is therefore part of the scaling law: changing it can change the compute-optimal recipe.

</details>


### [173] [Policy-Conditioned Counterfactual Credit for Verifiable Reinforcement Learning of Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05263)
*Renwei Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CVT-RL算法，通过反事实验证奖励、干预有效性门控和策略条件反事实贡献估计器，显著提升长周期语言智能体的推理可靠性和证据一致性，降低作弊行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法中，长周期语言智能体仍存在学习未经证实的证据链、信念漂移和满足终端检查的捷径行为；现有过程奖励大多是相关的而非因果的，无法估计步骤是否在指定干预下对最终验证成功有贡献。

Method: CVT-RL包含：(1) 密集可验证奖励；(2) 干预有效性门控；(3) 策略条件反事实贡献(PCCC)估计器。具体设计四种受控干预（删除、语义替换、证据替换、工具输出扰动）并通过冻结参考策略采样续写，使用选择调整的双重稳健估计器增强优势函数。信念控制仅使用前缀可观测标签，增广拉格朗日方法约束未支持的声明、跳过验证、工具篡改和不安全调用。

Result: 在长上下文QA、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld和Web/工具任务上，CVT-RL将平均任务成功率从非因果RL的71.8%和信息匹配反事实基线75.4%提升至78.9%；证据F1从78.9提升至82.8；测得作弊率从7.2%降至3.9%；人工审计作弊率4.6%（基线8.1%），自适应检测规避攻击下仅升至7.1%。所有主要指标的Holm校正后p<0.01。

Conclusion: 审慎设定的反事实信用分配，结合有效性门控、诊断方法和可验证约束，为更可靠的长周期语言智能体强化学习提供了一条可复现的路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards improves reasoning and tool use, yet long-horizon language agents still learn unsupported evidence chains, belief drift, and shortcut actions that satisfy terminal checks. Existing process rewards are mostly correlational: they reward retrieval-, reflection-, or verification-like steps without estimating whether the step contributes to final verified success under a specified intervention. We propose CVT-RL, a constrained policy-gradient algorithm with dense verifiable rewards, intervention-validity gating, and a policy-conditioned counterfactual contribution (PCCC) estimator. Deletion, semantic substitution, evidence substitution, and tool-output perturbation define separate controlled interventions; continuations are sampled from a frozen reference policy, and a selection-adjusted doubly robust estimator augments the advantage. Belief control uses only prefix-observable labels, while an augmented Lagrangian constrains unsupported claims, skipped verification, tool tampering, and unsafe calls. On long-context QA, ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and web/tool tasks, CVT-RL improves average task success from 71.8% for compute-matched non-causal RL and 75.4% for an information-matched counterfactual-process baseline to 78.9%, improves evidence F1 from 78.9 to 82.8 over the information-matched baseline, and reduces measured hacking from 7.2% to 3.9%. Independent human audit estimates 4.6% hacking for CVT-RL versus 8.1% for the information-matched baseline, and adaptive detector-evasion attacks raise hacking only to 7.1%. Stratified bootstrap and mixed-effects tests give p<0.01 after Holm correction for all primary metrics. Carefully scoped counterfactual credit, paired with validity gating, diagnostics, and verifiable constraints, provides a reproducible route toward more reliable long-horizon RL for language agents.

</details>


### [174] [REGEN: Reference-Guided Synthetic Multivariate Time Series Generation for Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05264)
*Moulik Gupta,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReGeN是一种参考引导的生成管道，将观测序列分解为相位对齐的周期性骨干、逐变量随机残差和滞后感知的跨变量依赖，可在低数据场景下生成结构可控、分布覆盖广的多变量时间序列，显著优于现有生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列生成器无法解决真实世界中观测序列稀少的问题：基于先验的方法生成领域无关样本，数据驱动方法将参考序列视为黑盒监督，缺乏对周期结构、局部变异和跨变量动态的显式控制。

Method: ReGeN将每个参考序列分解为三个可解释组件：(1) 相位对齐的周期性骨干（捕获领域主导形态）；(2) 基于深度核高斯过程的逐变量随机残差；(3) 通过结构因果模型及拟合耦合系数注入的滞后感知跨变量依赖。通过可控温度采样各组件以拓宽分布覆盖。

Result: ReGeN生成的数据可一致替代真实兄弟数据，且预测退化最小；在强周期领域（如交通）中甚至超越真实源数据。基于ReGeN语料库预训练的Foundation模型优于基于先验和数据驱动替代方案预训练的模型。

Conclusion: 在低数据场景下，如何结构性利用参考数据与拥有多少数据同等重要。

Abstract: Training robust multivariate time series forecasting models requires large, diverse corpora, yet many real-world domains provide only a handful of observed sequences. Existing generators fail to resolve this mismatch: prior-based approaches (e.g., CauKer, TimePFN) produce domain-agnostic samples, while data-driven methods (e.g., TimeGAN) treat references as black-box supervision, forfeiting explicit control over periodic structure, local variability, and cross-variable dynamics.
  We propose ReGeN, a reference-guided generative pipeline that treats observed sequences not as examples to imitate, but as structural scaffolds for controllable synthesis. ReGeN decomposes each reference into three interpretable components: a phase-aligned periodic backbone capturing dominant domain morphology; per-variable stochastic residuals modeled with a deep-kernel Gaussian process; and lag-aware cross-variable dependencies injected through a structural causal model with fitted coupling coefficients. Sampling these components at controllable temperature broadens distributional coverage while preserving domain-grounded structure.
  We show that ReGeN-generated data consistently substitutes for real sibling data with minimal forecasting degradation, and in strongly periodic domains such as traffic, can outperform the real source itself. We further show that a foundation model pretrained on ReGeN corpora outperforms those pretrained on prior-based and data-driven synthetic alternatives. This suggests that in low-data regimes, how reference data is structurally exploited can matter as much as how much data is available.

</details>


### [175] [Data-efficient flood depth prediction through domain-aware coreset selection and tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05265)
*Lipai Huang,Adithi Srinath,Manas Singh,Junwei Ma,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种领域感知的核心集构建流程，通过在推理时条件化表格基础模型，仅用0.7%的训练数据即可实现接近全监督方法98.5%的洪水深度预测精度（R²=0.663 vs 0.673），并能跨流域迁移而无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近实时洪水深度预测需要替代模型兼具准确性、快速性和跨流域可迁移性。有监督替代模型虽然精度可与物理模拟器媲美，但每个流域需要数百万训练样本，且无法外推至原始网格之外。

Method: 提出领域感知的核心集构建流程：按回归周期和受影响最大的流域对风暴分层，然后使用目标感知的空间选择器采样六边形。该流程在推理时条件化表格基础模型，仅需每个流域0.7%的训练数据。

Result: 在休斯顿地区九个流域上，平均R²达到0.663，达到有监督参考模型（R²=0.673）的98.5%。无需特定任务重新训练即可迁移到未见的流域，优于核心集训练的有监督基线。在真实风暴中，在远分布外情形下超过有监督参考，在近分布内情形下略逊。

Conclusion: 领域感知的核心集构建使表格基础模型能够实现数据高效、跨流域可迁移的洪水预测，无需每个流域单独训练。

Abstract: Near-real-time flood depth prediction demands surrogate models that are accurate, fast, and transferable across watersheds. Supervised surrogates can match physics-based simulators in accuracy but need millions of training rows per watershed and cannot extrapolate beyond their original mesh. We propose a domain-aware coreset construction pipeline that conditions a tabular foundation model at inference time. The pipeline stratifies storms by return period and most-affected watershed, then samples hexagons with a target-aware spatial selector. With 0.7% of the per-watershed training pool, the model attains a mean $R^2$ of 0.663 across nine Houston-area watersheds, within 98.5% of the supervised reference ($R^2$ = 0.673). It transfers to held-out watersheds without task-specific retraining, staying ahead of a coreset-trained supervised baseline. On real storms it exceeds the supervised reference on a far out-of-distribution case and trails it on a mostly in-distribution one. Domain-aware coreset construction lets tabular foundation models deliver data-efficient, watershed-transferable flood predictions without per-watershed training.

</details>


### [176] [Sharp Low-Degree Thresholds for Planted-vs-Planted Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05266)
*Anda Skeja,Daniel Gutiérrez Espinoza,Fiona Skerman,Alexander S. Wein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为 planted-vs-planted 设置中的低度多项式检验建立了尖锐阈值，在 planted submatrix 和 planted dense subgraph 模型中给出了匹配的低度上下界，并发现该阈值与已知的低度恢复阈值在精确常数上一致，而弱检验则呈现平滑转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 planted-vs-planted 设置中，目标是以渐近可忽略的误差判断数据由两种结构化 planted 机制中的哪一种生成，此前缺乏对这一检验问题的尖锐阈值的理论刻画。

Method: 基于低度恢复中的潜变量展开框架，开发了新的 planted-vs-planted 检验方法，通过识别和剪枝非信号贡献来建立低度多项式的匹配上下界。

Result: 在 planted submatrix 和 planted dense subgraph 模型中，低度检验的阈值与已知的低度恢复阈值在精确常数上一致；弱检验（优于随机猜测）则呈现平滑转变而非尖锐阈值。

Conclusion: 建立了 planted-vs-planted 设置中低度多项式检验的尖锐阈值理论，揭示了检验与恢复任务之间的深层联系，并区分了强检验（尖锐阈值）与弱检验（平滑转变）的本质差异。

Abstract: We establish the first sharp thresholds for low-degree polynomial tests in planted-vs-planted settings, where the goal is to determine with vanishing error which of two structured planted mechanisms generated the observed data. We prove matching low-degree upper and lower bounds for counting communities in the planted submatrix and planted dense subgraph models. The resulting testing threshold coincides, down to the sharp constant, with the known low-degree recovery threshold. In contrast, the task of weak testing, where the goal is to outperform random guessing, does not have a sharp threshold but rather a smooth transition, which we identify. To prove our results, we develop a framework for planted-vs-planted testing that builds on a latent-variable expansion originating in low-degree recovery and employs new methods to identify and prune non-signal contributions.

</details>


### [177] [Learning Manifold and Itô Dynamics with Branched Neural Rough Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05272)
*Luke Thompson,Dai Shi,Lequan Lin,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分支神经粗糙微分方程（B-NRDEs），通过Hopf代数框架将NRDE的log-ODE步骤重解为状态空间流形上的几何数值积分，使驱动代数匹配控制演算（Grossman-Larson树用于欧几里得Itô动力学，Munthe-Kaas-Wright树用于流形上的有序协变导数），并引入分支签名核目标使二次变分项在训练中可见，从而在粗糙Bergomi波动率、SO(3)动力学和SPD协方差动力学等任务上实现统一有效的随机与流形值动力学建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经粗糙微分方程（NRDEs）在非规则采样下保持精度且积分步数更少，但其依赖洗牌代数（Stratonovich演算的代数对应），无法暴露Itô动力学所需的二次变分项，也无法处理流形上带联络的Itô流所需的有序协变导数，限制了其在随机和流形值动力学中的应用。

Method: 引入分支神经粗糙微分方程（B-NRDEs），基于Hopf代数框架：将NRDE的log-ODE步骤重解为流形上的几何数值积分，驱动代数匹配控制演算类型（Grossman-Larson根树用于Itô、Munthe-Kaas-Wright平面根树用于流形、洗牌代数用于Stratonovich）；提出分支签名核目标以在训练中暴露二次变分项。

Result: 在粗糙Bergomi波动率建模、仿真到真实SO(3)动力学预测、SPD协方差动力学三个任务上，B-NRDEs展现出统一有效的性能，超越了欧几里得-Stratonovich设置下的传统方法。

Conclusion: B-NRDEs通过代数-几何统一的框架，扩展了神经粗糙微分方程到Itô随机动力学和流形值动力学场景，为超越传统欧几里得-Stratonovich框架的随机建模提供了通用且有效的方法。

Abstract: Neural rough differential equations (NRDEs) stay accurate under irregular sampling while taking far fewer integration steps than standard neural differential equations, summarising a finely sampled driver by its log-signature and advancing the hidden state over coarse intervals using the log-ODE method. This efficiency rests on the shuffle algebra, the algebraic counterpart of Stratonovich calculus. This reliance means NRDEs cannot expose the quadratic-variation terms Itô dynamics require, nor the ordered covariant derivatives that govern Itô flows on connection-equipped manifolds. Ameliorating this, we introduce Branched Neural Rough Differential Equations (B-NRDEs), a Hopf-algebraic framework that recasts the NRDE log-ODE step as geometric numerical integration on the state-space manifold, matching the driving algebra to the governing calculus: Grossman--Larson rooted trees for Euclidean Itô dynamics, Munthe-Kaas--Wright planar rooted trees for ordered covariant derivatives on manifolds, and the shuffle algebra in the classical Stratonovich case. This yields intrinsic coarse-step dynamics that exactly preserve manifold constraints. Finally, we introduce a branched signature-kernel objective to enable Itô-consistent law matching by making quadratic-variation terms visible during training. On rough Bergomi volatility, sim-to-real $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ dynamics forecasting, and SPD covariance dynamics, B-NRDEs offer a unified, effective approach to stochastic and manifold-valued dynamics beyond the Euclidean--Stratonovich setting.

</details>


### [178] [Anomaly Detection for Electro-Hydrostatic Actuators using LSTM Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05274)
*Nehal Afifi,Abdelmonem Elhendawi,Felix Leitenberger,Nadine Piat,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LSTM自编码器的离线异常检测框架，用于电静液作动器（EHA）传感器信号（温度和压力），在多种故障注入场景下实现了99.0%的平均准确率、最高100%的精确率、90.2%-99.6%的召回率和93.1%-99.8%的F1分数，展示了数据驱动方法在EHA异常检测中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHA在航空航天和工业系统中广泛应用，其传感器数据的异常检测对安全可靠运行至关重要。然而，EHA传感器数据量大、采样频率高，传统统计方法和经典机器学习方法（如Z-score、IQR、MAD、孤立森林、高斯混合模型、k-means）无法捕捉时间依赖性，导致检测精度低、误报率高。同时，针对EHA系统的数据驱动异常检测方法的系统性评估仍然缺乏。

Method: 采用基于重构的LSTM自编码器作为核心方法，利用验证集的重构误差分布进行校准和评估。实验数据来自受控测试台收集的EHA单变量温度与压力传感器信号。性能评估使用准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数，并在多种故障注入场景和不同运行条件下进行敏感性分析。

Result: LSTM自编码器在所有评估传感器上平均准确率达到99.0%，精确率最高达100%，召回率在90.2%-99.6%之间，F1分数在93.1%-99.8%之间，展示了高检测灵敏度和极低的误报率。

Conclusion: 研究证明了数据驱动离线异常检测在EHA系统中的可行性。未来工作将集中于将所开发框架适配到在线（实时）环境中。

Abstract: Electro-Hydrostatic Actuators (EHAs) are widely used in aerospace and industrial systems, where timely detection of sensor anomalies is essential to ensure safe and reliable operation. However, the large volume and high sampling frequency of EHA sensor data pose challenges for accurate and efficient anomaly detection. Conventional statistical and classical machine-learning methods such as Z-score, Interquartile Range (IQR), Median Absolute Deviation (MAD), Isolation Forest, Gaussian Mixture, and k-means often fail to capture the temporal dependencies inherent in EHA signals, resulting in limited detection accuracy and elevated false-alarm rates. Furthermore, systematic evaluations of data-driven anomaly detection approaches for EHA systems remain scarce, particularly under varying operational conditions. This study presents an offline anomaly-detection framework for univariate EHA sensor signals, focusing on temperature and pressure data collected from a controlled test bench. The method employs a reconstruction-based Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) autoencoder, calibrated and evaluated using validation-set reconstruction-error distributions. Performance is assessed across multiple fault-injection scenarios using accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score, complemented by sensitivity analyses under varying operating conditions. The LSTM autoencoder achieved an average accuracy of 99.0\%, precision up to 100\%, recall between 90.2\% and 99.6\%, and F1-scores from 93.1\% to 99.8\%, demonstrating high detection sensitivity and a very low false-alarm rate across all evaluated sensors. These results highlight the feasibility of data-driven offline anomaly detection for EHAs. Future work will focus on adapting the developed framework for an online (real-time) environment.

</details>


### [179] [Agentic Monte Carlo: Simulating Reinforcement Learning for Black-Box Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05296)
*Dae Yon Hwang,Raunaq Suri,Valentin Villecroze,Anthony L. Caterini,Jesse C. Cresswell,Noël Vouitsis,Brendan Leigh Ross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Monte Carlo (AMC)方法，利用强化学习与贝叶斯推断的等价性，通过序贯蒙特卡洛直接从黑盒LLM智能体的最优策略中采样，在不修改模型参数的情况下实现类似RL的策略优化，在AgentGym基准上显著优于提示基线，甚至超越GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒LLM智能体（如闭源商业模型）只能通过API访问，无法进行参数级别的强化学习优化，现有RL方法不适用。作者希望在不修改模型参数的前提下，实现对黑盒智能体的策略优化。

Method: 利用RL与贝叶斯推断的等价性，将最优策略定义为轨迹的后验分布，其中先验分布由固定的黑盒LLM智能体定义。采用序贯蒙特卡洛（Sequential Monte Carlo）方法从后验分布中采样，通过学习一个价值函数来引导智能体行为，同时保持底层黑盒模型不变。

Result: 在AgentGym基准的三个不同环境中验证，AMC显著优于提示基线（prompting baselines），并且在扩展测试时计算量时，性能甚至超越了GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）。

Conclusion: AMC证明了在不修改黑盒LLM智能体参数的前提下，进行原则性的RL风格优化是可行的，为黑盒智能体的策略优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: LLM agents operate in two distinct regimes: open-weight agents amenable to reinforcement learning (RL) and black-box agents whose behaviour must be controlled purely at test time. Although black-box agents are often backed by state-of-the-art proprietary LLMs, API-only access precludes parameter-level optimization, rendering most RL methods inapplicable. To address this limitation, we turn to a known equivalence between RL and Bayesian inference. We propose Agentic Monte Carlo (AMC) to directly sample from the optimal policy of a black-box agent rather than training it through RL. The optimal policy is a posterior over trajectories whose prior we define as the fixed black-box LLM agent. We employ Sequential Monte Carlo to sample from this posterior by learning a value function to steer the agent while leaving the underlying black-box model unchanged.
