We validate AMC on three diverse environments from the AgentGym benchmark, demonstrating significant improvements over prompting baselines and even outperforming Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) as we scale the test-time compute of our method. AMC demonstrates the feasibility of performing principled RL-style optimization of black-box LLM agents. Code is available at https://github.com/layer6ai-labs/Agentic-Monte-Carlo

</details>


### [180] [Statistically Reliable LLM-Based Ranking Evaluation via Prediction-Powered Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05308)
*Abhishek Divekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRECISE框架，将预测驱动的推理（PPI）扩展到排序评估指标中，通过结合少量人工标注和大量LLM判断来生成无偏的偏差校正估计。该方法将输出空间计算复杂度从O(2^|C|)降低到O(2^K)，使其适用于Precision@K等层次化指标。在ESCI基准上，30个人工标注结合Claude 3评估使Precision@4标准误差降低21%；在生产系统中，100个人工标签和2小时专家标注即可正确识别最佳系统变体，经A/B测试验证该排名带来+407 bps的日销售额提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在排序评估中，人工标注成本高昂且难以大规模获取，而LLM自动判断虽然廉价但可能存在系统性偏差。现有方法无法在保证无偏估计的同时高效处理Precision@K等层次化指标（指标在查询级别，而标注在文档级别），亟需一种既能利用LLM大规模评估优势又能消除其偏差的方法。

Method: 将Prediction-Powered Inference (PPI)框架扩展到排序评估场景，通过少量人工标注数据对大量LLM判断结果进行偏差校正。针对层次化指标Precision@K的计算问题，将输出空间从O(2^|C|)约简至O(2^K)，使计算可行。核心思想是利用LLM作为代理标注器，再用人工标注校正其误差，实现无偏估计。

Result: 在ESCI基准上，30个人工标注+Claude 3评估使Precision@4的标准误差从4.45降至3.50（相对降低21%）。在生产系统中，仅需100个人工标签和2小时领域专家标注即可正确识别三个系统变体中的最佳方案，A/B测试验证该排名带来+407 bps（基点）的日销售额提升。

Conclusion: PRECISE框架成功将PPI应用于排序评估，在保证无偏性的前提下显著降低了估计方差，并能以极少量人工标注在真实生产环境中有效识别最佳系统。该方法有望成为低成本、高可信度的排序系统评估标准范式。

Abstract: With PRECISE, we extended Prediction-Powered Inference to produce bias-corrected estimates of ranking evaluation metrics by combining a small human-labeled set with a large LLM-judged set. PPI is provably unbiased regardless of the LLM judge's error profile. We make it applicable to hierarchical metrics like Precision@K, where annotations are per-document but the metric is per-query, by reducing the output-space computation from O(2^|C|) to O(2^K). On the ESCI benchmark, augmenting 30 human annotations with Claude 3 Sonnet judgments reduces the standard error of Precision@4 estimates from 4.45 to 3.50 (a 21% relative reduction). In a production system, our framework correctly identified the best of three system variants from 100 human labels and 2 hours of domain-expert annotation; A/B testing confirmed this ranking with +407 bps in daily sales.

</details>


### [181] [Mamba-Assisted Non-Markovian Closure for Reduced-Order Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05371)
*Zhi-Feng Wei,Saad Qadeer,Panos Stinis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Mamba-Assisted Closure (MAC)框架，利用基于Mamba的序列模型逼近Mori-Zwanzig形式中的非马尔可夫闭包项，通过状态空间模型的双重表示（卷积形式训练、递归形式推理）实现高效长轨迹训练和恒定步长推理成本，在粘性Burgers方程和混沌两尺度Lorenz '96系统上显著优于马尔可夫降阶模型、GRU序列模型和Wilks方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维动力系统的降阶建模常受非马尔可夫闭包项（表示未解析变量对解析变量的影响）的阻碍，传统马尔可夫近似无法准确捕捉闭包的记忆效应。

Method: 将闭包建模转化为序列建模问题，提出MAC框架：用Mamba序列模型从已解析轨迹预测闭包项，再通过数值积分器与降阶控制方程耦合推进时间演化。利用状态空间模型的双重表示，训练时用卷积形式实现序列到序列学习，推理时用递归形式实现自回归推演，兼顾训练效率与推理速度。

Result: 在粘性Burgers方程和混沌两尺度Lorenz '96系统上，MAC模型在预测精度和长时间推演稳定性上显著超越马尔可夫降阶模型、GRU序列模型和Wilks方法。

Conclusion: MAC框架有效解决了降阶建模中的非马尔可夫闭包问题，兼具高效训练与恒定推理成本，为高维动力系统降阶提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reduced-order modeling of high-dimensional dynamical systems is often hindered by the non-Markovian closure term that represents the effect of unresolved variables on the resolved dynamics. Inspired by the Mori--Zwanzig formalism, in which the closure takes the form of a memory functional of the resolved trajectory, we recast closure modeling as a sequence modeling problem and propose the Mamba-Assisted Closure (MAC) framework: a Mamba-based sequence model, trained to predict the closure from the resolved trajectory, is coupled with the reduced-order governing equations through a numerical integrator to advance the resolved variables in time. A key feature of the framework is its exploitation of the dual representation of state-space models -- the model is trained in a sequence-to-sequence fashion via the convolutional form, and deployed for step-by-step autoregressive rollout via the recurrent form, yielding both efficient long-trajectory training and constant per-step inference cost. On the viscous Burgers' equation and the chaotic two-scale Lorenz '96 system, the MAC model substantially outperforms the Markovian reduced-order model, the GRU-based sequence model, and the Wilks method in predictive accuracy and long-time rollout stability.

</details>


### [182] [SHALA-LLM: Smartly Handling Ambiguous Labels in Aligning LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05376)
*Jingyao Wu,Ashley Wang,Keane Ong,Paul Pu Liang,Rosalind Picard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SHALA-LLM算法，通过强化学习框架让LLM从标注分布中学习并动态优先处理高歧义样本，在NLI和ER任务上将JS距离降低62.1%，同时F1提升16.7%，证明建模标注者分歧能同时提升歧义处理能力和分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对齐方法假设单一正确标签，忽略了真实场景中因多解释导致的标注歧义和标注者分歧，未能有效利用这种歧义信息来改善模型行为。

Method: 提出SHALA-LLM算法，一种强化学习框架，让LLM直接从标注者分布中学习，并在优化过程中动态优先处理高歧义样本。

Result: 在ChaosNLI、GoEmotions、MSP-Podcast等基准上，SHALA-LLM显著降低与标注者标签分布的JS距离（ChaosNLI上降低62.1%），同时提升F1分数（最高达16.7%）。

Conclusion: 建模标注者分歧不仅能更好地处理歧义输入，还能提升分类性能，为LLM对齐中处理标注歧义提供了有效新范式。

Abstract: Many human-centered tasks, including natural language inference (NLI) and emotion recognition (ER), have multiple plausible interpretations, leading to label ambiguity and challenging disagreements across human annotators. As LLMs are increasingly deployed in real-world settings, faithfully modeling such ambiguity is essential to identify contested inputs, preserve variability in ambiguous cases, and capture the full distribution of human judgments. Yet, existing LLM alignment approaches have predominantly assumed a single correct label, excluding annotator disagreement during optimization. Instead of treating this ambiguity as noise, we show how to treat it as information that improves model behavior through a new algorithm called SMARTLY HANDLING AMBIGUOUS LABELS IN ALIGNING LLMS (SHALA-LLM). This reinforcement learning framework provides a new way for LLMs to learn directly from annotator distributions while dynamically prioritizing highly ambiguous samples during optimization. Experiments on ambiguity-sensitive NLI and ER benchmarks, including ChaosNLI, GoEmotions, and MSP-Podcast, demonstrate that SHALA-LLM improves agreement with annotator label distributions, e.g. on ChaosNLI, it reduces Jensen-Shannon Distance by up to 62.1%. At the same time, SHALA-LLM improves F1 by up to 16.7%, showing that modeling annotator disagreement can also strengthen classification performance.

</details>


### [183] [Mitigating the Curse of Dimensionality in Uniform Convergence of Deep Neural Networks via Smooth Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05599)
*Yizhe Ding,Runze Li,Jia Liu,Lingzhou Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了光滑激活深度神经网络估计器的一致收敛理论框架，证明了光滑DNN能够通过自适应利用目标函数的低维层次组合结构来缓解ReLU网络在一致收敛中面临的维度灾难问题，并在多种统计任务中提供了非渐近的一致收敛率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ReLU网络在L^2(P)范数下能达到极小化最优率，但在一致收敛行为中可能遭受维度灾难，这限制了其在需要最坏情况可靠性的下游任务中的应用。

Method: 建立了光滑DNN（包括前馈和残差结构）的伪维度上界、非渐近逼近保证和Hölder范数界；推导了Huber回归、最小二乘、分位数回归和逻辑回归等统计背景下光滑DNN估计器的非渐近一致收敛率。

Result: 光滑DNN可以通过自适应利用目标函数的低维层次组合结构来缓解一致收敛中的维度灾难问题；仿真研究和实际应用验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 光滑DNN在需要一致保证的统计学习任务中是ReLU网络在理论上和实践上可行的替代方案。

Abstract: This paper establishes a theoretical framework for the uniform convergence of smoothly activated deep neural network (DNN) estimators. While standard ReLU networks achieve minimax-optimal rates in the $L^2(P)$ norm for various nonparametric regression tasks, we establish a theoretical lower bound demonstrating that least-squares ReLU estimators can suffer from the curse of dimensionality in their uniform convergence behavior. Motivated by the need for reliable uniform guarantees in downstream tasks requiring worst-case reliability, we address this limitation by analyzing smoothly activated DNNs (smooth DNNs), encompassing both feedforward and residual structures. We establish novel pseudo-dimension bounds, non-asymptotic approximation guarantees, and Hölder-norm bounds for the approximators of these models. Leveraging these results, we derive non-asymptotic uniform convergence rates for smooth DNN estimators across multiple statistical contexts, including Huber, least-squares, quantile, and logistic regression. We prove that smooth DNNs can mitigate the {curse of dimensionality} in uniform convergence by adaptively exploiting the low-dimensional hierarchical composition structure of the target function. Supported by both simulation studies and a real-world application, our results position smooth DNNs as a theoretically grounded and practically viable alternative to ReLU networks for statistical learning tasks requiring uniform guarantees.

</details>


### [184] [Pattern Selectivity is Not Task-Causal Structure: A Cross-Architecture Mechanistic Study of Composed-Task Circuits in 1B-Class Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05378)
*Yongzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一的屏幕-消融实验协议，测试了三个1B级语言模型（Pythia 1B、OLMo 1B、OLMoE 1B-7B）在四项组合任务（间接宾语识别、大于比较、后继序列、变量绑定）中的注意力头电路机制。结果表明：没有两个（任务，模型）单元共享相同的主要因果屏幕——同一任务在不同模型中使用不同的注意力模式类型实现。本文提出了五类屏幕结果分类法（主因、次因、相关物、干扰物、零）和可证伪假设：MoE模型在基础的前一个token位置基座上构建组合任务电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证"单个屏幕-消融方案"在不同模型家族中是否产生一致的机械性解释声明，特别是探究同一任务在不同训练流水线模型中的实现机制是否一致。

Method: 采用统一的屏幕-消融协议，对3个1B级语言模型（含密集和MoE架构）的4项组合任务进行系统测试。使用匹配随机零假设（每单元10个种子）进行因果消融验证，并引入五类屏幕结果分类法和谱参与比信号作为通用指标。

Result: 12个（任务，模型）单元中没有任何两个共享相同的主要因果屏幕；MoE模型（OLMoE 1B-7B）在3/4的任务中以前一个token位置电路为主因屏幕；五类屏幕结果全部出现在面板中。

Conclusion: 任务特定的机械性解释声明需要结合任务模式筛选和逐模型因果验证，不能跨模型泛化；MoE模型可能通过共享的positional substrate构建组合任务电路。

Abstract: We test whether a single screen-and-ablate recipe -- identify attention-head circuits by task-pattern selectivity, then verify by causal ablation against a matched-random null -- produces consistent mechanistic claims across model families. The recipe ports across pipelines; the specific circuit it identifies does not. Across four composed tasks (indirect-object identification, greater-than, successor sequences, variable binding) and three 1B-class language models from distinct training pipelines (Pythia 1B / Pile / dense; OLMo 1B / DCLM / dense; OLMoE 1B-7B / DCLM / mixture-of-experts), we run a unified protocol with the matched-random null sampled across ten seeds per cell. The resulting 12 (task, model) cells contain no two that share the same primary causal screen at comparable effect size: the same task, with the same behavioral capability, is implemented through different attention-pattern types across models.
  We introduce a five-category screen-outcome taxonomy -- primary cause, secondary cause, correlate, interferer, null -- with quantitative thresholds, and show that all five outcomes appear in the panel. We propose a falsifiable hypothesis: the MoE model in our panel builds composed-task circuits on top of a foundational previous-token positional substrate (the prev-token-circuit ablation is the strongest causal screen on 3 of 4 tasks for OLMoE 1B-7B), with the IOI exception consistent with IOI being a final-position name-copying task whose structure directly probes a different pattern. The hypothesis comes with explicit predictions for other MoE language models.
  We frame the methodology honestly: the spectral participation-ratio signal from the companion methodology paper is a general indicator of specialized computation; what makes a finding task-specific is the task-pattern screen plus a per-model causal verification.

</details>


### [185] [Generalized TV--$\ell_p$ Structured Priors for Bayesian $T_1$ Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05381)
*Disi Lin,Martin Berggren,Tommy Löfstedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合全变分（TV）与ℓₚ范数的结构化空间先验族（TV-ℓₚ），用于贝叶斯回归框架下的T₁映射不确定性量化，采用NUTS进行后验推断。实验表明，该方法相比极大似然估计及多种贝叶斯先验，能获得更集中的后验密度、更低方差和更小（负）偏倚，提升空间一致性与不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在T₁映射中，现有方法缺乏有效的空间先验建模与不确定性量化能力，传统先验难以同时兼顾空间平滑性和参数估计可靠性。

Method: 提出TV-ℓₚ结构化先验族，证明其适定性，将其嵌入贝叶斯回归框架，采用No-U-Turn Sampler（NUTS）进行后验推断，实现T₁映射中的不确定性量化。

Result: 在合成脑部/心脏T₁数据集及真实乳腺T₁数据集上的实验表明：TV-ℓₚ先验获得更集中的后验密度（不确定性更低），方差更小，（负）偏倚更小，估计更可靠。

Conclusion: 将基于TV的结构化惩罚与ℓₚ范数嵌入贝叶斯先验中，能有效改善T₁图的空间连贯性并增强不确定性量化，为带不确定性的T₁映射提供了一种稳健方法。

Abstract: We propose an extended family of structured spatial priors that incorporates the total variation (TV) function with $\ell_p$ norms. The prior is proven to be proper and incorporated into a Bayesian regression framework to enable uncertainty quantification in $T_1$ mapping, with posterior inference performed using the No-U-Turn Sampler (NUTS). This TV--$\ell_p$ construction is proven to constitute a well-defined family of prior distributions, and it naturally enforces spatial consistency and smooth variations in the estimated parameter maps. The method was evaluated in comparison to maximum-likelihood estimation and several Bayesian alternative priors based on the uniform, Gamma, and bounded TV priors. The evaluation includes experiments on synthetic brain and cardiac $T_1$ mapping datasets, as well as a real in-vivo breast $T_1$ mapping dataset. The results show that the TV--$\ell_p$ prior yields more concentrated posterior densities, indicating reduced uncertainty. It also consistently achieves lower variance and smaller (negative) bias, leading to more reliable estimates. Overall, embedding a TV-based structured penalty along with $\ell_p$ norms in a prior in a Bayesian model improves spatial coherence in $T_1$ maps and enhances uncertainty quantification, offering a robust approach for $T_1$ mapping with uncertainties.

</details>


### [186] [Trust, but Don't Verify: Epistemic Blind Spots in LLM Source Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05403)
*Rohan N. Pradhan,Steve Goley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 语言模型具备检测虚假统计数据的能力，但在多源信息合成过程中无法调用该能力，而是基于方法论的形式风格（methodology-register）而非数值有效性来评估证据来源。这种"认知对齐"（epistemic alignment）问题不同于谄媚（sycophancy），它追踪的是来源是否呈现为分析可信，而非其主张是否内部一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在多源证据合成时，是否能评估证据质量，还是仅基于表面呈现方式聚合信息。特别关注模型能否区分真实与捏造的统计证据。

Method: 设计实验测试模型检测虚假统计的能力（单独检测方法论），以及在多源合成场景下是否仍能利用该能力。跨3个模型家族（Claude、Qwen、OLMo）、3个专业领域进行复现。使用因果追踪、线性探针、组件级归因等机制分析方法。

Result: 模型在孤立条件下检测虚假统计的正确率达0.76-1.00，但在多源合成时对虚假与真实统计给出相似的数值估计。来源影响力由methodology-register门控决定，而非数值有效性。探针显示methodology-register表征AUC 0.83-0.92，而数值有效性信号在多源合成时被抑制至随机水平。提示工程干预（包括oracle检查清单）仅导致全面怀疑而非选择性辨别。

Conclusion: 模型存在"认知对齐"问题——有能力但未部署，问题不在能力而在部署方式。后训练流程强化了风格捷径而非数值验证机制。

Abstract: Language models increasingly act as epistemic proxies, synthesizing evidence from multiple sources to inform decisions. Whether they evaluate the quality of that evidence, or merely aggregate it based on surface presentation, remains poorly understood. We show that models possess the capability to detect fabricated statistics (correct identification rates of 0.76-1.00 for methodology in isolation) but do not recruit this capability during multi-source synthesis, producing similar numeric estimates whether the statistics are fabricated or valid. Specifically, source influence is governed by a methodology-register gate that responds to the distributional register of analytical text but not to numeric validity: for example, statistically impossible confidence intervals receive the same weight as valid ones. The behavioral dissociation replicates across five models from three families (Claude, Qwen, OLMo) and three professional domains. Mechanistic analyses, including causal tracing, linear probes, and component-level attribution, converge on the same account: the model encodes and causally uses a methodology-register representation that transfers across domains (probe AUC 0.83-0.92), while numeric-validity signals, decodable in isolation, are suppressed to chance during multi-source synthesis. Prompting-based mitigations, even an oracle checklist naming the exact statistical checks, produce blanket skepticism rather than selective discernment, and the post-training pipelines we examine reinforce the stylistic shortcut without building numeric verification. Unlike sycophancy, which tracks user preference, this failure tracks whether a source presents as analytically credible, not whether its claims are internally consistent. We term this epistemic alignment: like preference and safety alignment, the question is not capability but deployment.

</details>


### [187] [CausalPOI: Spatio-Temporal Graph-Based Causal Modeling for Cold-Start POI Check-in Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05413)
*Zhaoqi Zhang,Miao Xie,Yi Li,Linyou Cai,Siqiang Luo,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalPOI框架，用于解决冷启动POI签到预测问题。该框架利用时空功能交互图建模POI间的语义和空间关系，并通过结构对齐的处理和控制图模拟事实与反事实场景，在真实数据集上显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有POI预测方法依赖基于邻近度的图和相关性驱动建模，忽视了POI间的功能依赖关系，且无法捕捉城市干预的因果效应。为此，本文引入冷启动POI签到预测这一新问题。

Method: 提出CausalPOI框架，包括：1）时空功能交互图，建模POI间语义与空间关系；2）结构对齐的处理图和控制图，分别模拟事实与反事实场景；3）基于时空图神经网络的因果表示学习。

Result: 在真实SafeGraph数据集上的实验表明，CausalPOI在时空预测、语义交互建模和因果效应估计方面全面显著优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: CausalPOI为城市干预分析提供了更可解释且可操作的基础，验证了因果表示学习在POI预测中的有效性。

Abstract: As urban environments continue to evolve rapidly, accurately modeling the dynamic behaviour of Points of Interest is essential for supporting data-driven urban planning and commercial decision-making. While recent advancements in spatio-temporal graph learning have improved POI forecasting, most methods rely on proximity-based graphs and correlation-driven modeling, which overlook the functional dependencies between POIs and fail to capture the causal effects of urban interventions. In this paper, we introduce a novel research problem -- cold-start POI check-in forecasting, which aims to predict the future check-in pattern of a newly introduced POI, by modeling its temporal evolution and functional interactions with nearby POIs in a structured urban spatial context. To address these challenges, we propose CausalPOI, a spatio-temporal graph-based causal representation learning framework. CausalPOI leverages Spatio-Temporal Functional Interaction Graph to model semantic and spatial relationships between POIs, and constructs structurally aligned treatment and control graphs to simulate factual and counterfactual scenarios. Extensive experiments on real-world SafeGraph datasets demonstrate that CausalPOI significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across the board, validating its effectiveness in spatio-temporal forecasting, semantic interaction modeling, and causal effect estimation, providing a more interpretable and actionable foundation for urban intervention analysis. Source code is available at Github.

</details>


### [188] [Selective-Advantage Entropy-Adaptive Horizon GRPO: Asymmetric Token-Level Discounting for Efficient Reinforcement Learning of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05434)
*Chirag Chawla,Rohan Charudatt Salvi,Madhav S. Baidya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种GRPO扩展方法：AH-GRPO（基于累积熵的自适应折扣）和SA-AH-GRPO（仅对负优势轨迹进行折扣）。在GSM8K基准上，SA-AH-GRPO在3B模型上达到Pass@1=0.858，训练方差降低3.6倍；在1.5B模型上达到0.686，优于基线。不对称折扣保留正梯度信号、防止熵坍塌并稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO对所有token位置和采样轨迹对称处理，未考虑模型不确定性在不同token和轨迹上的差异，导致训练不稳定和熵坍塌问题。

Method: (i) AH-GRPO：基于累积熵的折扣因子，在模型不确定时缩短有效horizon；(ii) SA-AH-GRPO：仅对负优势（失败）轨迹应用熵折扣，正优势（成功）轨迹保留完整梯度信号。

Result: 3B模型上SA-AH-GRPO峰值Pass@1=0.858，训练方差降至0.0246（比GRPO降低3.6倍）；1.5B模型上峰值Pass@1=0.686，优于零样本基线0.637。

Conclusion: 不对称折扣能保留正确解上的完整梯度信号，防止熵坍塌，显著稳定训练过程，为结构化生成任务中基于可验证奖励的强化学习提供了原则性的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimisation (GRPO) has emerged as an effective reinforcement-learning algorithm for aligning language models on reasoning tasks, but it treats every token position and every sampled rollout symmetrically. We introduce two complementary extensions: (i) Adaptive-Horizon GRPO (AH-GRPO), which weights each token's policy gradient using a cumulative entropy-based discount that reduces the effective horizon when the model is uncertain, and (ii) Selective-Advantage AH-GRPO (SA-AH-GRPO), which applies this discounting only to negative-advantage rollouts, leaving positive-advantage, successful trajectories unattenuated. We evaluate standard GRPO with alpha = 0, AH-GRPO with alpha = 0.5, and SA-AH-GRPO with alpha = 0.5 on the GSM8K mathematical reasoning benchmark using both Qwen 2.5-1.5B-Instruct and Qwen 2.5-3B-Instruct fine-tuned with LoRA. On the 3B model, SA-AH-GRPO achieves Pass@1 = 0.858 at its peak at step 30 and maintains 0.846 at 180 steps, with training variance reduced to 0.0246, a 3.6 times reduction relative to GRPO while matching its peak accuracy. On the 1.5B model, SA-AH-GRPO achieves a peak Pass@1 of 0.686, improving over the zero-shot baseline of 0.637. Our analysis shows that asymmetric discounting preserves the full gradient signal on correct solutions, prevents entropy collapse, and substantially stabilises training, suggesting a principled inductive bias for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards on structured generation tasks.

</details>


### [189] [DP-MacAdam: Differentially Private Mechanism with Adaptive Clipping and Adaptive Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05435)
*Naima Tasnim,Lalitha Sankar,Oliver Kosut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DP-MacAdam算法，统一使用梯度均值和方差估计来实现自适应裁剪和自适应动量，无需手动调参，在隐私保护机器学习中优于DP-SGD、AdaClip和DP-Adam基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私自适应裁剪算法（如AdaClip）利用均值和方差估计进行自适应裁剪，但未将这些估计用于动量加速训练；而DP-Adam虽利用类似Adam的动量加速训练，却未利用这些估计进行自适应裁剪。两者未结合，导致无法同时享受自适应裁剪和自适应动量的优势。

Method: 提出DP-MacAdam算法，将自适应裁剪（AdaClip）与自适应动量（Adam类）统一在同一框架下，使用相同的梯度均值和方差估计同时指导裁剪和动量更新，并保证方差估计的无偏性。

Result: 理论分析表明DP-MacAdam的梯度方差估计是无偏的；实验评估显示其在模型效用上优于DP-SGD、AdaClip和DP-Adam基线，且无需手动调节裁剪阈值。

Conclusion: 通过统一均值和方差估计服务于自适应裁剪和自适应动量，DP-MacAdam有效提升了差分隐私训练的模型效用，消除了裁剪阈值手动调参的需求。

Abstract: Differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD) has become the standard framework for privacy-preserving machine learning, yet its reliance on a fixed gradient clipping threshold to limit sensitivity remains a significant practical limitation. Adaptive clipping algorithms such as AdaClip shift and scale the gradient prior to clipping and adding noise so that the clipped gradient yields a more informative descent direction. The shift and scaling parameters are selected adaptively based on the empirical mean and variance. However, in existing adaptive clipping algorithms, these empirical estimates have not been also used for momentum to accelerate training itself. On the other hand, DP-Adam is an algorithm that exploits Adam-like momentum updates based on the gradient mean and variance to accelerate training, but does not exploit these estimates for adaptive clipping. In this work, we propose Differentially Private Mechanism with Adaptive Clipping and Adaptive Momentum (DP-MacAdam), a novel algorithm that combines these two approaches so as to use the same mean and variance estimates for both clipping and momentum. We perform an analysis showing that DP-MacAdam estimates the gradient variances in a bias-free manner. In addition, we empirically evaluate the privacy and accuracy of DP-MacAdam, demonstrating that it achieves improved model utility compared to DP-SGD, AdaClip, and DP-Adam baselines, without requiring manual tuning of the clipping threshold.

</details>


### [190] [Sharp First-Order Lower Bounds for Higher-Order Smooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05438)
*Dongruo Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了光滑非凸优化中，目标函数满足高阶光滑性假设时，寻找ε-稳定点的确定性一阶预言复杂度。作者证明了下界：在Hessian-Lipschitz情况下为Ω(ε^{-7/4})，在三阶光滑情况下为Ω(ε^{-5/3})，匹配已知上界。其构造基于一种“块链”机制。该下界构造借助ChatGPT 5.5 Pro辅助完成，并由作者验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶光滑非凸优化中，一阶方法的上界已被加速（如ε^{-7/4}和ε^{-5/3}），但匹配的下界一直未解决。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 提出一种基于“块链”（block-chain）机制的构造方法，在保持标量硬实例所需光滑性结构的同时，强制预言的块状揭示过程，从而得到无维度依赖的下界。

Result: 在Hessian-Lipschitz情形下得到匹配的Ω(ε^{-7/4})下界，在三阶光滑情形下得到匹配的Ω(ε^{-5/3})下界，且对任意有限光滑阶均成立。

Conclusion: 解决了高阶光滑非凸优化中一阶复杂度下界的公开问题，证明了已有上界的最优性。此外，该下界构造借助ChatGPT 5.5 Pro辅助完成。

Abstract: We study the deterministic first-order oracle complexity of finding \(ε\)-stationary points in smooth nonconvex optimization when the objective satisfies higher-order smoothness assumptions. While the classical \(ε^{-2}\) rate is optimal under only Lipschitz gradients, higher-order smoothness leads to accelerated first-order upper bounds, most notably the \(ε^{-7/4}\) rate under Lipschitz Hessians and the \(ε^{-5/3}\) rate under Lipschitz third derivatives. The matching lower bounds, however, have remained open. We resolve this gap by proving a new dimension-free first-order lower bound for higher-order smooth nonconvex functions, valid for every finite smoothness order. In particular, our construction gives a matching \(Ω(ε^{-7/4})\) lower bound in the Hessian-Lipschitz case and a matching \(Ω(ε^{-5/3})\) lower bound in the third-order-smooth regime. The hard instance is based on a \emph{block-chain} mechanism that enforces blockwise oracle revelation while preserving the smoothness structure needed for the scalar hard instance. The lower-bound construction was discovered with the assistance of ChatGPT 5.5 Pro and subsequently verified by the authors.

</details>


### [191] [GOTabPFN: From Feature Ordering to Compact Tokenization for Tabular Foundation Models on High-Dimensional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05441)
*Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib,Md Younus Ahamed,Prashnna Kumar Gyawali,Gianfranco Doretto,Donald A. Adjeroh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GO-LR（图引导排序与局部优化）方法处理高维小样本表格预测，将其等价为加权最小线性排列问题，并设计TSP路径代理求解器；在此基础上构建GOTabPFN模型，配合神经启发子单元压缩（NSC）将局部相邻有序特征池化为元特征，使TabPFN风格预测在HDLSS场景下可行，在严格token预算下提升稳定性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决小型表格基础模型在高维小样本（HDLSS）表格预测任务中无法有效工作的问题，无需重新训练大型骨干网络。

Method: 提出Graph-guided Ordering with Local Refinement (GO-LR)方法，证明其与加权最小线性排列问题的等价性，并设计TSP路径代理求解器；构建GOTabPFN模型，加入神经启发子单元压缩（NSC）模块，将局部相邻有序特征池化为元特征。

Result: 在表格基准测试中，GOTabPFN在严格token预算下提升了预测的稳定性和准确性。

Conclusion: GOTabPFN通过GO-LR排序和NSC压缩，使得TabPFN风格预测在HDLSS场景下变得实用有效。

Abstract: We investigate how to make small tabular foundation models effective for High-Dimensional, Low-Sample Size (HDLSS) tabular prediction without retraining large backbones. We introduce Graph-guided Ordering with Local Refinement (GO-LR), show its equivalence to weighted Minimum Linear Arrangement, and interpret the practical solver as a TSP-path-style surrogate. We propose GOTabPFN,which builds on GO-LR, and a Neuro-Inspired Subunit Compression (NSC) unit to pool locally adjacent ordered features into meta-features, yielding a compact representation that makes TabPFN-style prediction practical in HDLSS regimes. Across tabular benchmarks, GOTabPFN improves stability and accuracy under tight token budgets.

</details>


### [192] [Towards Unified and Data-Efficient Prognostics and Health Management with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05481)
*Raffael Theiler,Lev Telyatnikov,Leandro Von Krannichfeldt,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用表格基础模型（Tabular Foundation Models）通过上下文学习（in-context learning）处理工业时间序列数据，解决PHM任务中数据碎片化、标签稀疏等问题。实验表明，该方法在预后和诊断任务上优于序列模型、Transformer和梯度提升树，尤其在低数据场景下表现优异，且能有效保留时间上下文信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业PHM数据常存在碎片化、部分观测、标签质量差等问题，限制了监督学习的使用。现有时间序列基础模型主要面向长序列、规则采样的预测任务，难以直接应用于异构PHM场景。因此需要一种可复用、数据高效且适用于多种PHM任务的通用框架。

Method: 将原始单元级信号转换为表格行格式，然后利用表格基础模型（尤其是基于PFN（Prior-Data Fitted Networks）的模型）通过上下文学习进行PHM任务（预后与诊断）的建模。在统一评估协议下，与序列模型、Transformer基线和梯度提升树进行对比。

Result: 1) 表格基础模型在预后和诊断任务上取得最佳平均排名；2) PFN模型在低数据场景下具有竞争力；3) 时间上下文可通过表格表示得到保留；4) 模型性能依赖于在子采样条件下构建有代表性的上下文。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型为异构PHM问题提供了一种实用且通用的接口，能够有效克服工业数据质量限制，实现跨任务的复用和高效学习。

Abstract: Data-driven Prognostics and Health Management (PHM) uses time-varying condition-monitoring data to diagnose system states and estimate remaining useful life in engineered assets. These tasks are central to maintenance planning, but industrial PHM data are often fragmented, partially observed, and poorly labeled, which hinders supervised learning. Foundation models offer a route toward reusable predictive systems, yet most time-series foundation models are designed for forecasting and assume long, coherent, regularly sampled sequences. To address this gap, we propose a framework for applying Tabular Foundation Models to industrial time series using in-context learning, and we evaluate them on a variety of PHM tasks. By converting raw unit-level signals into tabular rows, we show that these models perform well across multiple tasks - including prognostics, and diagnostics - and are highly data efficient. We compare them directly with sequence models, transformer baselines, and gradient-boosted trees under a common evaluation protocol. The results indicate that tabular foundation models achieve the best average ranks across prognostic and diagnostic tasks. Our findings further show that PFN-based models are competitive in low-data regimes, that temporal context can be preserved in the tabular representation, and that performance depends on representative context construction under subsampling. These results demonstrate that tabular foundation models provide a practical and general interface for heterogeneous PHM problems.

</details>


### [193] [Learned Subspace Compression for Communication-Efficient Pipeline Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05484)
*Paul Janson,Edouard Oyallon,Eugene Belilovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MAPL方法，通过可学习的正交投影（在Stiefel流形约束下）实现流水线并行中的激活压缩，结合因子化锚点嵌入和残差向量量化，在LLaMA模型上实现了高压缩比且性能损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流水线并行中跨阶段激活通信在低带宽网络下成为瓶颈，现有固定正交投影方法性能下降严重且需要非标准优化约束，而可学习的低秩投影又难以维持正交性。

Method: 1) 在Stiefel流形约束下学习可微正交投影（MAPL）；2) 引入每阶段因子化锚点嵌入实现全秩激活重建；3) 提出残差向量量化与流式码本同步协议以进一步压缩。

Result: 在150M到1B参数的LLaMA模型上，MAPL可轻松应用于现有流水线，实现高压缩比且性能损失可忽略，在性能-压缩权衡上显著优于Subspace Networks。

Conclusion: MAPL通过流形约束的端到端学习方法有效解决了流水线并行中的激活通信瓶颈，提供了更好的性能-压缩权衡。

Abstract: Pipeline parallelism enables training of large language models that exceed single-device memory, yet inter-stage activation communication becomes the dominant bottleneck when trained on low-bandwidth networks. Recent work in this area has proposed using fixed orthogonal projections to compress activations. However, this still results in a significant performance degradation and requires a number of non-standard adaptations to constrain the optimization. A natural alternative is to learn a low rank projection for each pipeline stage, however maintaining the necessary orthogonality of these projectors during training remains a challenge. We present Manifold Aware Projection Learning (MAPL), a method that treats inter-stage compression as a learnable orthogonal projection under explicit Stiefel manifold (orthogonal matrices) constraints. Rather than prescribing a fixed global subspace, MAPL lets each pipeline stage discover and continuously adapt its own task-optimal compression subspace via manifold-constrained steepest descent. To recover token-specific signals at stage boundaries, we introduce per-stage factorized anchor embeddings that allow for full-rank activation reconstruction with negligible communication overhead. We further show that we can incorporate residual vector quantization after projection with a streaming codebook synchronization protocol that amortizes dictionary communication. Across LLaMA models from 150M to 1B parameters we show that MAPL can be easily applied to the existing pipeline and can achieve high compression with neglibile performance degradation with a drastically improved tradeoffs in performance vs. compression compared to Subspace Networks.

</details>


### [194] [LEVANTE-bench: Multi-Scale Comparison of VLMs to Children Using Cognitive Tasks (or, "Is Your VLM Smarter Than a 5th Grader?")](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05497)
*Alvin Wei Ming Tan,David Cardinal,Tania Lorido-Botran,Laura Bravo-Sanchez,Sunny Yu,Michael C. Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LEVANTE-bench基准，用于系统评估视觉语言模型（VLMs）与5-12岁儿童在六个认知任务上的对齐程度。研究发现，模型与儿童的对齐具有异质性：更大、更强的模型在任务和项目层面与儿童更一致，但在错误分布匹配上差异显著；小模型在某些任务上更能匹配年幼儿童的错误模式；所有VLM在矩阵推理和心理旋转任务上表现不佳，表明当前VLM架构仅部分对齐儿童的认知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类经验本质上是多模态的，视觉语言模型有望模拟人类认知发展，但缺乏系统比较VLM与儿童认知发展的工具，尤其是在跨任务、跨年龄和跨文化群体方面。

Method: 基于LEVANTE项目的数据，构建LEVANTE-bench基准，包含六个认知任务，评估多尺度VLM与1547名5-12岁儿童（来自三个国家）在总体准确率、任务/项目水平对齐以及试次级错误分布匹配上的表现。

Result: 模型与儿童的对齐具有异质性：更强模型在任务/项目层面与人类更一致，但错误分布匹配因任务而异；较小模型在某些任务上更匹配年幼儿童；所有VLM在矩阵推理和心理旋转任务上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 当前VLM架构仅部分对齐儿童的认知能力，模型规模与人类认知对齐之间的关系并非简单线性，需要进一步改进模型以更好地模拟人类认知发展。

Abstract: Given the inherently multimodal nature of human experience, vision-language models (VLMs) hold substantial promise for modeling human cognition as it grows and develops with experience. Realizing their potential requires tools for comparing VLMs with human cognitive development across tasks, ages, and populations. We present LEVANTE-bench, a benchmark based on tasks and data from the Learning Variability Network (LEVANTE), which distributes open-source tasks and data measuring children's cognition across languages and cultures. In LEVANTE-bench, we systematically assess VLMs on six tasks, comparing their alignment with children aged 5-12 ($N$ = 1547) across three countries. We compare models at multiple scales, assessing their overall accuracy, their task- and item-level alignment with children, and how well they match children's trial-level error distributions. Alignment was heterogeneous across scales: at the level of tasks and items, more capable models aligned better with humans. However, match to human error distributions varied widely across tasks, and for several tasks, smaller models matched younger children's errors better. In addition, even the best-performing VLMs struggled on matrix reasoning and mental rotation tasks. Thus, current VLM architectures align only partially with the cognitive abilities of children.

</details>


### [195] [Dominant-Layer ZO: A Single Layer Dominates Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05516)
*Wanhao Yu,Ziyan Wang,Zheng Wang,Abeer Matar Almalky,Yihang Zuo,Shuteng Niu,Sen Lin,Adnan Siraj Rakin,Deliang Fan,Li Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 零阶优化微调大模型时，性能提升高度集中于单个解码层（主导层）。仅微调该层即可匹配或超越全模型微调效果，该层具有任务无关、模型特定、可预先识别的特性，能带来4.52倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究零阶优化微调大模型时，各层对适应性能的贡献分布情况，澄清是否所有层都同等重要。

Method: 通过实验发现零阶微调存在单个主导解码层；利用激活异常值分析（首个激活异常层）在训练前识别该层；从扰动传播角度分析主导层的两种关键特性（高扰动敏感性和残差流中的早期位置）。

Result: 在LLaMA2-7B和Qwen3-8B上九个基准测试中，仅微调主导层在平均性能上超越全模型MeZO和基于LoRA的零阶微调，并实现高达4.52倍训练加速。

Conclusion: 零阶微调的性能提升几乎完全由单个解码层贡献，该层可通过简单的推理前分析识别，为高效大模型微调提供了新方向。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization enables memory-efficient fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) using only forward passes, but it remains unclear how useful adaptation is distributed across layers. In this work, we reveal a surprising phenomenon: ZO fine-tuning is sharply dominated by a single decoding layer. Across multiple LLM families and downstream tasks, fine-tuning this dominant layer alone consistently matches or even exceeds full-model ZO fine-tuning. We further show that the dominant layer is task-agnostic but model-specific, and can be identified before training through a simple inference-only analysis of activation outliers. Specifically, the dominant layer consistently aligns with the first activation-outlier layer in the pre-trained model. To explain this phenomenon, we analyze how perturbation effects propagate under ZO optimization. We find that the dominant layer combines two key properties: high perturbation sensitivity and early placement in the residual stream, allowing perturbation-induced effects to propagate and accumulate through remaining subsequent decoding layers. As a result, this layer produces disproportionately strong and stable optimization signals under forward-only updates. Extensive experiments on LLaMA2-7B and Qwen3-8B across nine benchmarks show that dominant-layer ZO fine-tuning improves average performance over full-model MeZO and LoRA-based ZO fine-tuning while achieving up to 4.52$\times$ training speedup.

</details>


### [196] [What Objects Enable, Not What They Are: Functional Latent Spaces for Affordance Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05533)
*Rohan Siva,Neel P. Bhatt,Yunhao Yang,Seoyoung Lee,Nishant Gadde,Christian Ellis,Alvaro Velasquez,Zhangyang Wang,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出A4D框架，通过将视觉观测映射到以功能（affordance）为中心的共享潜空间中进行规划，而非传统的外观特征空间。A4D包含功能推理和未知功能发现机制，在多个规划任务中推理准确率达94%，新功能推理准确率从70%提升至90%以上，且推理速度提升100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人规划系统基于外观特征进行推理，将视觉观测编码到围绕物体外观组织的潜空间中，但规划需要推理物体的任务相关功能（如是否"可移动"），外观潜空间无法捕获这些信息，导致泛化到新颖的机器人-物体交互时表现不佳。

Method: 提出A4D框架：(1) 将视觉观测映射到以功能（affordance）结构化的共享潜空间；(2) 通过测量观测在功能潜空间中的邻近性来推断物体的功能属性；(3) 引入功能发现机制，当潜空间中的邻近性量化不确定性较高时，选择性触发新功能的扩展。

Result: 在多个涉及多样化和未见功能的任务中评估：(1) 已有功能推理准确率94%，超越SOTA超过15个百分点；(2) 新功能推理准确率从70%提升至90%以上，仅需不到10%原始训练数据；(3) 推理速度提升100倍。

Conclusion: A4D通过基于功能推理而非外观推理的规划范式，有效提升了机器人系统在未知交互场景中的泛化能力，实现了高精度、数据高效且快速的推理。

Abstract: Existing robot planning systems rely on appearance-based reasoning, where visual observations are encoded into latent spaces organized around object appearances (e.g., recognizing a "cart" based on how it looks). However, planning requires reasoning about task-relevant functionalities of objects (e.g., whether an object is "movable"), which appearance-based latent spaces do not capture. As a result, existing approaches struggle to generalize to novel robot-object interactions. We address this limited generalizability through affordance reasoning, enabling planning based on task-relevant object functionalities instead of appearance alone. We introduce A4D, which maps visual observations into a shared latent space structured around affordances (e.g., "movable"). By projecting visual observations into this functional latent space and measuring their proximity to affordances, A4D infers functionalities relevant to the observed object. Furthermore, we introduce an affordance discovery mechanism that expands the latent space to handle unseen scenarios where existing affordances are insufficient. A4D uses proximity in the functional latent space to quantify uncertainty in affordance inference and selectively triggers affordance discovery. We evaluate A4D across several planning tasks involving diverse and unseen affordances. A4D achieves 94% inference accuracy on existing affordances outperforming state-of-the-art approaches by over 15% points, improves new-affordance inference accuracy from 70% to over 90% with fewer than 10% of the original training data, and enables 100x faster inference. Code, videos, and data available at: https://A4Dance-reasoning.github.io.

</details>


### [197] [Less is MoE: Trimming Experts in Domain-Specialist Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05538)
*Haoze He,Xinkai Zou,Xuan Jiang,Xingyuan Ding,Ao Qu,Juncheng Billy Li,Heather Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fisher-MoE方法，通过Fisher信息量识别MoE模型中FFN层的稀疏中间维度，在50%压缩率下保持模型能力，减少45%权重内存并提升21%推理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型压缩方法在通用基准测试中失效，原因是压缩粒度不当——关键能力分布在不同专家中，但集中在FFN稀疏中间维度上。

Method: 使用Fisher信息量评估FFN中间维度的重要性，替代基于激活值、路由分数或幅度的指标；在FFN内部按Fisher重要性排序并移除不重要的中间维度。

Result: Qwen1.5-MoE中仅移除135万路由FFN中间维度中的12个即可大幅降低GSM8K准确率；在50%压缩率下，Fisher-MoE保持模型能力，减少约45%权重内存，提升21%推理吞吐量。

Conclusion: FFN中间维度是MoE模型压缩和重要性排序的有效粒度，模型能力高度集中于此。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models achieve strong performance through conditional computation, but their large parameter footprint poses deployment challenges. Prior MoE compression approaches catastrophically fail when evaluated on general-purpose benchmarks beyond commonsense reasoning. We trace this failure to the granularity of compression: important capabilities are distributed across experts but concentrated in FFN sparse intermediate dimensions. To identify these dimensions, we use Fisher importance which outperforms activation-, router-score-, and magnitude-based alternatives, and identifies tiny sets of task-critical dimensions: in Qwen1.5-MoE, removing as few as 12 of 1.35M routed-FFN intermediate dimensions collapses GSM8K accuracy while largely preserving factual-knowledge performance. Building on this, we propose Fisher-MoE, which operates within FFN to remove intermediate dimensions ranked by Fisher importance. At the same 50% MoE compression ratio, Fisher-MoE preserves model capability, while reducing weight memory by ~45% and improving inference throughput by 21%. These findings suggest intermediate dimension granularity is an effective unit for both compression and ranking where capability concentrates in MoE models.

</details>


### [198] [Multimarginal flow matching with optimal transport potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05327)
*Raghav Kansal,David Crair,Nghia Nguyen,Scott Pope,Bradley Parry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OTP-FM方法，通过动态最优传输中的势项约束中间边际分布，将条件流匹配扩展到多边际场景，实现无模拟的高效训练，在单细胞RNA测序、海洋学和气象学数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配方法主要关注两个端点分布之间的传输，但许多科学领域（如动力学系统）中存在中间观测边际分布，这些中间分布可以约束端点间的流。然而，目前缺乏有效利用这些中间边际信息的多边际流匹配方法。

Method: 利用流匹配与动态最优传输之间的联系，在动态最优传输作用量中引入势项来软性引导流走向中间边际分布；扩展条件流匹配的学习目标以包含这些势项，从而推导出无模拟的高效多边际流匹配算法。

Result: 在单细胞RNA测序、海洋学和气象学数据集上，OTP-FM在性能和训练效率上均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: OTP-FM通过势项约束有效解决了多边际流匹配问题，在时空动力学上具有灵活性和高效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) has emerged as a powerful framework for learning dynamic transport maps between two empirical distributions. However, less explored is the setting with intermediate observed marginals that can help constrain the flows between the endpoints. This "multimarginal" regime is central to modeling temporal evolution in dynamical systems in many scientific domains that can sample sequential distributions. We tackle this problem with a novel approach that leverages the connection between FM and dynamic optimal transport (OT), softly steering the flow towards the intermediate marginals through potential terms in the dynamic OT action. By extending the conditional FM learning target to incorporate these potentials, we derive an efficient, simulation-free algorithm for multimarginal FM that offers considerable flexibility in the spatiotemporal dynamics of the learned flows. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and training efficiency of OT-potential FM (OTP-FM) on diverse single-cell RNA sequencing, oceanographic, and meteorological datasets. Our code is available at https://github.com/Bexorg-Inc/OTP-FM.

</details>


### [199] [Balancing Image Compression and Generation with Bootstrapped Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05552)
*Haozhe Chi,Jinghan Li,Hao Jiang,Wu Sheng,Yi Ma,Jing Wang,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SelfBootTok通过自引导学习将图像信息解耦为全局和局部token组，使生成器仅需全局token即可高效工作，在减少约40%计算量的同时实现更优的重建和生成质量，以64个token达到1.56的gFID新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像分词方法将不同粒度的信息混合在每个token中，导致token间存在冗余信息，且这种混合信息增加了生成器训练的复杂度。

Method: 通过自引导学习(self-bootstrapped learning)，模型仅从全局token预测局部细节，将视觉细节的负担从生成器转移到分词器，实现信息在全局和局部token组间的干净解耦。

Result: 生成器仅需全局token即可运行，计算量减少约40%，同时重建和生成性能更优；仅使用64个token即达到1.56的gFID分数，取得新SOTA。

Conclusion: 该方法具有良好的可扩展性，通过利用更多数据或参数来自监督局部表示学习，能够进一步提升性能，表明将信息解耦至tokenizer的设计范式是高效且可扩展的。

Abstract: Despite progress in image tokenization, standard methods encode redundant information by mixing all granularities within each token, thus redundancy persists between tokens. The mix of information of different granularity also complicates the training of generators. This paper introduces SelfBootTok, a method that resolves this by cleanly decomposing information into global and local token groups. Through self-bootstrapped learning, the model predicts local details exclusively from global tokens, shifting the burden of visual details from the generator to the tokenizer. Consequently, our generator is far more efficient, requiring only global tokens and reducing computation by approximately 40%, while delivering superior reconstruction and generation. Moreover, this paradigm scales elegantly: by leveraging more data or parameters to self-supervise local representation learning, SelfBootTok achieves a new state-of-the-art gFID score of 1.56 using only 64 tokens.

</details>


### [200] [A prism hierarchy of learning regimes in large linear autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05335)
*Eugene Golikov,Yaroslav Gusev,Dmitry Yarotsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统刻画了大型权重绑定线性自编码器在梯度下降下的五种极端学习机制（大数据、小数据、平均场、窄隐变量、自由），这些机制对应三角棱柱的2-面，并在前四种机制中导出了训练损失和总体损失演化的显式表达式，与实验结果吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型的理论研究通常考虑不同的极限学习机制，但缺乏对特定模型所有定性不同极端学习机制的系统性认识。本文旨在为大型权重绑定线性自编码器建立这样一个完整的极端学习机制图景。

Method: 基于形式损失展开层次（formal loss-expansion hierarchy），将极端机制与三角棱柱的面自然关联，识别出五种基本极端机制（对应棱柱的2-面），并在前四种机制下推导梯度流下训练损失和总体损失演化的显式表达式。

Result: 识别出五种极端机制：大数据、小数据、平均场、窄隐变量和自由机制。对前四种机制导出了训练和总体损失演化的解析表达式，实验验证吻合良好。

Conclusion: 本文为权重绑定线性自编码器提供了完整的极端学习机制系统化分类，并在可处理的情况下给出了解析解，为理解更复杂模型的极限行为提供了框架性参考。

Abstract: Theoretical studies of machine learning models commonly consider different limiting regimes in which the learning dynamics of gradient descent becomes theoretically tractable. It is, however, desirable to have a systematically obtained picture of all qualitatively different extreme learning regimes for a particular type of models. In this paper we propose such a picture for large weight-tied linear autoencoders characterized by input and latent dimensions, initialization magnitude, and training set size. This model is nonlinear in the weights and its gradient flow does not have a general theoretical solution. We show that at the level of the formal loss-expansion hierarchy, its extreme regimes are naturally associated with faces of a triangular prism. In particular, there are five basic extreme regimes associated with the 2-faces of the prism: (1) large-data, (2) small-data, (3) mean-field, (4) narrow-latent, and (5) free. For regimes (1,2,3,4), we derive explicit expressions for both train and population limiting loss evolutions under gradient flow, obtaining very good agreement with experimental results.

</details>


### [201] [Representation Learning Enables Scalable Multitask Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05555)
*Johan Obando-Ceron,Lu Li,Scott Fujimoto,Pierre-Luc Bacon,Aaron Courville,Pablo Samuel Castro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模型无关的MR.Q算法，通过将预测性表征学习与高容量值函数近似结合，无需规划即可在多任务连续控制中超越基于世界模型的方法，显著提升计算效率，并验证了表征学习是可扩展多任务强化学习的关键驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展的多任务强化学习究竟依赖模型预测控制还是表征学习尚不明确，现有模型-based方法依赖规划和复杂训练流程，难以确定核心驱动因素。

Method: 提出MR.Q算法：一种模型无关的actor-critic架构，结合辅助预测性目标（预测性表征学习）与高容量值函数近似，不依赖规划。

Result: 在多任务连续控制任务套件中，MR.Q优于基于世界模型的方法及多种深度RL基线，显著降低计算开销并提升实际运行效率，增加模型容量持续带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 可扩展多任务强化学习的主要驱动因素是表征学习而非模型预测控制，预测性表征学习对性能至关重要。

Abstract: Scaling reinforcement learning (RL) to diverse multitask settings remains a central challenge. While recent advances in model-based RL achieve strong performance, they rely on planning and complex training pipelines, making it unclear which components are essential for scalability. We revisit this question and argue that the primary driver of scalable multitask RL is not model-based control, but \emph{representation learning}. In particular, we show that combining predictive, model-based representations with high-capacity value function approximation is sufficient to achieve strong performance, even without planning. We evaluate a simple model-free algorithm, MR.Q, coupled with auxiliary predictive objectives into a scalable actor-critic architecture. This approach outperforms a recent world-model-based method and a range of deep RL baselines across a diverse suite of multitask continuous control tasks, while significantly reducing computational overhead and improving wall-clock efficiency. We observe consistent improvements with increased model capacity and show through ablations that predictive representation learning is critical for performance.

</details>


### [202] [Field Validation of a Multi-Resolution ConvLSTM Framework for Retaining Wall Deformation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05556)
*Jihoon Kim,Heejung Youn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并现场验证了一种多分辨率ConvLSTM堆叠集成框架，用于预测基坑开挖过程中的挡土墙变形。该框架仅使用高斯噪声增强的数值模拟数据训练，但在韩国11个开挖场地的34个测斜仪数据验证中，实现了平均MAE=1.4mm、R²=0.93的预测精度，证明了数值模拟训练的模型可有效泛化至实际工程条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基坑开挖过程中挡土墙变形的准确预测对施工安全至关重要，但现有方法多依赖数值模拟或简单机器学习模型，缺乏对多时间尺度时空特征的捕捉能力，且实际工程验证不足。本研究旨在开发一种能融合多分辨率时序信息、并经过充分现场验证的深度学习框架。

Method: 构建多分辨率ConvLSTM框架：对不同时间分辨率的变形时序数据分别训练ConvLSTM模型，然后通过堆叠集成策略（Stacking Ensemble）融合各分辨率模型的预测结果。训练数据来源于经高斯噪声增强的数值模拟数据集；验证数据来自韩国11个开挖场地的34个测斜仪实际监测数据。

Result: 在11个开挖场地上，框架预测后续最多5.0m额外开挖深度的挡土墙变形，平均MAE为1.4mm，R²为0.93。系统分析了时间变形不规则性和时空预测特征对模型性能的影响。

Conclusion: 该框架尽管仅使用数值模拟和增强数据训练，但能够有效泛化至多样化的现场开挖条件，在实际挡土墙变形预测中达到可靠的精度水平，验证了从仿真到现场的可迁移性。

Abstract: This study presents a comprehensive field validation of a multi-resolution Convolutional Long Short-Term Memory (ConvLSTM) framework for predicting retaining wall deformation during staged excavation. The framework is trained on Gaussian noise-augmented numerical simulations and integrates ConvLSTM models operating at different temporal resolutions through a stacking ensemble strategy. The proposed framework is validated using field monitoring data from 34 inclinometers across 11 excavation sites in South Korea. Site-wise prediction performance is systematically evaluated using multiple evaluation metrics, with analyses of the influence of temporal deformation irregularity and spatiotemporal prediction characteristics on model performance. The results demonstrate that the framework predicts retaining wall deformation associated with up to 5.0 m of additional excavation with an average mean absolute error of 1.4 mm and a coefficient of determination of 0.93 across the excavation sites. These results indicate that the framework, although trained exclusively on numerically simulated and augmented database, can be effectively applied to diverse field excavation conditions and achieve a reliable level of prediction accuracy in practical retaining wall deformation prediction.

</details>


### [203] [Autoregressive Diffusion World Models for Off-Policy Evaluation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05558)
*Kaixuan Liu,Guojun Xiong,Weinan Zhang,Shengpu Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADWM是一个基于自回归扩散世界模型的离线评估框架，通过预收集轨迹模拟多轮交互环境，无需在线执行即可评估新LLM代理策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮交互环境中评估LLM代理成本高且风险大（需要在线环境交互），现有离线策略评估方法（如联合扩散状态和动作）不适用于LLM代理离散文本动作的因果采样特性，而自回归世界模型存在累积误差问题。

Method: 学习一个潜在扩散世界模型，将每个转移建模为独立去噪过程（而非整条轨迹联合扩散），通过策略条件评分函数使待评估LLM代理直接引导每一步的扩散生成，实现世界模型与代理按因果顺序交替rollout。

Result: 在多种多轮代理任务上，ADWM实现了准确的价值估计和评估可靠性。

Conclusion: ADWM为离线LLM代理评估提供了一种实用的框架，避免了在线交互的成本和风险，解决了现有方法的根本性局限。

Abstract: Evaluating large language model (LLM) agents in multi-turn interactive environments is expensive and risky, as it requires online environment interaction. We propose ADWM (Autoregressive Diffusion World Model), an evaluation framework that estimates the performance of a new LLM agent policy purely from pre-collected trajectories. The core idea is to learn a latent diffusion world model that simulates how the environment responds to the evaluation policy, without ever executing it in the real environment. Existing diffusion-based OPE methods guide full trajectories in a single pass by jointly diffusing states and actions, an assumption that breaks down for LLM agents whose actions are discrete text that must be sampled from the policy after observing the environment. Unlike autoregressive world models that suffer from compounding errors, ADWM models each transition as an independent denoising process, enabling reliable step-by-step rollouts where the world model and agent alternate in causal order. Crucially, the LLM agent under evaluation directly guides the diffusion generation at each step via a policy-conditioned score function, ensuring that simulated trajectories accurately reflect its decision-making patterns. Empirically, ADWM achieves accurate value estimates and evaluation reliability across diverse multi-turn agent tasks, demonstrating its promise as a practical framework for offline LLM agent evaluation.

</details>


### [204] [CLaaS: Continual learning as a service for sample efficient online learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05559)
*Kion Fallah,Silen Naihin,Barak Widawsky,Qingqing Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CLaaS系统，使大语言模型智能体在部署后能通过在线持续学习从经验流中适应环境分布变化，利用经验回放缓冲区提高样本效率，实验证明参数更新优于上下文学习，且回放机制对样本效率至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署后的大语言模型智能体需要在动态环境中适应分布变化，但真实环境无法轻易重置，智能体在每个场景中只能采样一次动作和环境转换，因此需要一种能从累积经验中学习、保留先前能力并迁移到未来任务的在线持续学习方法。

Method: 提出CLaaS（Continual Learning as-a-Service）系统，通过聊天API抽象，使智能体在部署期间能够进行经验性在线持续学习。系统将rollout存储在经验回放缓冲区中，用于异步训练时的梯度重用，以提高样本效率。

Result: 在对抗性任务上的实验表明，参数更新相比上下文学习具有更好的前向迁移和更少的遗忘，其中经验回放是提高样本效率的关键选择。

Conclusion: CLaaS系统能有效支持智能体在部署后通过在线持续学习适应分布变化，参数更新比上下文学习更适合持续学习场景，经验回放机制对样本效率有重要贡献。

Abstract: Deployed large language model agents must adapt to distribution shift in dynamic environments. Ideally, adaptation can be performed from accumulated agent experiences and retain prior capabilities while transferring to future tasks. However, agent actions and environmental transitions can only be sampled once per scenario, as real-world environments cannot be trivially reset. To this end, we investigate an experiential and online continual learning setting in which agents learn from a stream of scenarios. We propose continual learning as-a-service (CLaaS), a system which enables agents to improve during deployment, abstracted behind a chat API. To increase sample efficiency, CLaaS stores rollouts in an experience replay buffer for gradient reuse during asynchronous training. We evaluate CLaaS on an adversarial task, demonstrating that parametric updates lead to superior forward transfer and less forgetting than in-context learning, with replay being a critical choice for sample efficiency.

</details>


### [205] [Zero-Copy Semantic Contagion: An In-Memory Streaming Architecture for Evolving Attention Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05733)
*Kabir Murjani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种异构Rust-Python流式架构，通过连续时间图注意力机制实现跨公司信息传播，在单CPU上实现~13ms端到端延迟，并在FNSPID语料库上获得1.70×（随机基线）和3.36×（同行业基线）的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单资产预测模型无法捕捉跨公司信息传播（如台湾晶圆厂 disruption 对苹果股价的影响），导致信号滞后。

Method: 采用Rust-Python异构流式架构：Rust端零拷贝解析新闻记录（~100 ns）并扫描股票池（~1.2 μs）；Python端使用多元神经霍克斯过程，包含每节点连续时间LSTM状态和双线性隐式投影来传播有向激发，并采用自适应剪枝规则控制动态邻域更新的计算成本。

Result: 端到端处理延迟~13 ms；在FNSPID语料库（638篇文章，47只股票）上，90分位次日回报阈值下，精度相较于随机基线提升1.70倍，相较于同行业基线提升3.36倍；移除图拓扑后精度归零。

Conclusion: 动态注意力网络是跨公司信号提取的唯一驱动力，验证了所提架构在金融时间序列预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Per-ticker forecasting models dominate financial time-series work yet remain blind to cross-company propagation: a foundry disruption in Taiwan does not register in a single-asset model until Apple's own price has already moved. To address this limitation, we introduce a heterogeneous Rust-Python streaming architecture that maps cross-company attention as a continuous-time graph driven directly from text. We show that on the ingestion side, a zero-copy Rust edge parses news records in $\sim$100 ns and scans the target equity universe in $\sim$1.2 $μ$s. On the inference end, a multivariate Neural Hawkes Process featuring per-node continuous-time LSTM states and a bilinear latent projection propagates directed excitation, while an adaptive pruning rule bounds the computational cost of dynamic neighborhood updates. Combining these stages, we demonstrate an end-to-end processing latency of $\sim$13 ms per incoming news record on a single commodity CPU. Evaluated on a one-month temporal holdout of the FNSPID corpus (638 articles across 47 tickers), the system delivers a $1.70\times$ precision lift over random at the 90th-percentile next-day return threshold, and $3.36\times$ over a same-sector baseline. Crucially, removing the graph topology collapses precision to zero, confirming that the dynamic attention network is the sole driver of cross-company signal in this architecture.

</details>


### [206] [AsyncWebRL: Efficient Multi-Step RL for Visual Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05597)
*Hao Bai,Rui Yang,Chenlu Ye,Spencer Whitehead,Aviral Kumar,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AsyncWebRL通过异步系统设计和算法改进（用常数1/k替代轨迹归一化器1/|τ_i|），在减少GPU空闲和轨迹冗余的同时，实现了2.9倍训练吞吐量提升，并在WebGym OOD测试集上取得新的SOTA（+5.8%相对提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步强化学习训练视觉语言Web代理存在两类效率瓶颈：（1）同步RL中GPU空闲；（2）轨迹使用过多步骤和token，导致计算浪费。

Method: 系统层面：采用异步设计，将 rollout、梯度更新和策略刷新重叠进行，并引入永久性rollout池和轻量级截图处理。算法层面：将多步GRPO中的逐轨迹归一化器1/|τ_i|替换为常数1/k，消除失败轨迹（通常更长）对负梯度权重的削弱，从而压缩冗余轨迹。

Result: 端到端训练吞吐量提升2.9倍；在WebGym OOD测试集上达到新SOTA（相对+5.8%），其中Medium难度+42%、Hard难度+48%。

Conclusion: 异步系统设计和算法归一化策略的联合优化，能有效解决Web智能体多步RL训练中的计算效率问题，并提升模型性能。

Abstract: Training vision-language web agents with multi-step RL is compute-intensive, with two dominant forms of inefficiency: idle GPUs in synchronous RL, and trajectories that use more steps and tokens than necessary. We present AsyncWebRL, which addresses both. On the system side, an asynchronous design overlaps rollout, gradient update, and policy refresh across iterations, paired with two web-agent-specific adaptations, namely an everlasting rollout pool and lightweight screenshot handling, that together deliver up to a $2.9\times$ end-to-end training-throughput speedup over the previously fastest open synchronous pipeline (WebGym). On the algorithmic side, we identify the per-trajectory normalizer $1/|τ_i|$ in multi-step GRPO as the root cause of trajectory-level and token-level inefficiency: because failures are systematically longer than successes, it down-weights the negative gradient on failed tokens, so the policy keeps producing verbose memory schemas. Replacing $1/|τ_i|$ with a constant $1/k$ breaks this coupling, contracting trajectories while preserving aggregate success. Together, these contributions set a new open-source state of the art on the WebGym out-of-distribution test split (+5.8% relative over the 42.9% prior best), with the largest gains on the harder slices (+42% relative on Medium, +48% relative on Hard).

</details>


### [207] [Causal Longitudinal Prior-Fitted Networks for Counterfactual Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05797)
*Amirhossein Zare,Amirhessam Zare,Herlock Rahimi,Reza Salarikia,Mohammad Kashkooli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalLongPFN，一种基于先验拟合的上下文预测模型，通过完全在合成数据上预训练，无需梯度更新或倾向性模型拟合即可在测试时进行纵向因果预测，在反事实基准和事实预测任务上与领域专用模型竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向治疗决策需要预测未来治疗方案下的潜在结局，但现有纵向因果估计方法通常需要为每个队列或模拟器重新训练模型，成本高昂且缺乏泛化性。作者希望开发一种预训练后冻结、无需领域特定训练的通用纵向因果预测器。

Method: 在广泛的时间结构因果模型先验上完全合成预训练，模型通过上下文学习（in-context learning）利用支持轨迹和查询历史，对给定的未来治疗方案序列进行单步预测，并通过递归方式实现多步预测。测试时模型冻结，无需梯度更新或拟合倾向性模型。

Result: 在分支癌症、HIV和华法林反事实基准上，CausalLongPFN与领域训练基线模型竞争力相当；在MIMIC-III ICU轨迹的事实预测中表现强劲。

Conclusion: 广泛的合成因果预训练可以作为一种有用的冻结替代方案，适用于领域特定训练成本高昂或不可行的场景。

Abstract: Longitudinal treatment decisions require predicting potential outcomes under future treatment sequences in the presence of time-varying confounding, heterogeneous patient dynamics, and limited domain-specific data. Existing longitudinal causal estimators typically train a new model for each cohort or simulator. We introduce Causal Longitudinal Prior-Fitted Networks (CausalLongPFN), a prior-fitted in-context predictor for longitudinal causal prediction. The model is pretrained entirely on synthetic episodes sampled from a broad prior over temporal structural causal models, exposing it to treatment-confounder feedback, latent heterogeneity, nonlinear state evolution, delayed effects, and cumulative treatment responses. At test time, CausalLongPFN is frozen: it conditions on support trajectories, a query history, and a proposed future treatment sequence, and returns a predictive distribution over future outcomes without gradient updates or propensity-model fitting. Multi-step predictions are obtained by recursively applying the one-step predictor under the specified treatment sequence. We evaluate on branchable cancer, HIV, and warfarin benchmarks with ground-truth counterfactual labels, and on factual-only rolling-origin prediction in MIMIC-III ICU trajectories. CausalLongPFN is competitive with domain-trained longitudinal baselines on counterfactual benchmarks and performs strongly on factual MIMIC-III prediction, suggesting that broad synthetic causal pretraining can provide a useful frozen alternative when repeated domain-specific training is costly or impractical.

</details>


### [208] [From Prediction to Self: Developmental Conditions for Agency in Minimal Neural Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05605)
*Evan Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过192维GRU模型在40个控制实验中追踪自因与世界因果区分能力的涌现，发现需满足四个严格顺序条件：持久状态吸引子、因果动作循环、本体感觉反馈、异步觉醒。提出代理增益（A=Err_world-Err_self）作为量化指标，并验证了12个被证伪的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究仅通过预测世界的系统如何发展出区分自身因果影响与外部世界变化的能力，理解自我表征的涌现机制。

Method: 使用192维GRU模型，通过40个控制的发育序列实验逐步添加组件，追踪系统区分自因与世界因果变化的能力，并提出代理增益（A=Err_world-Err_self）作为量化指标。

Result: 自感知预测器在周期性和混沌环境中始终优于自盲预测器；代理增益指标在消融所有辅助组件后仍然有效；仅前向采样动作选择产生有意义的代理增益，两种基于梯度的方法退化。12个假设被证伪。

Conclusion: 自我与世界区分的涌现需要满足四个严格顺序条件，仅靠预测编码不足以产生自我表征。

Abstract: How does a system that merely predicts the world come to distinguish its own causal influence from everything else? We trace this transition in a minimal 192-dimensional GRU through 40 controlled experiments arranged as a developmental sequence, adding components one at a time and tracking whether the system can distinguish self-caused from world-caused changes.
  The developmental path reveals four conditions that must be satisfied in strict order: (1) persistent state forming stable attractors, (2) a causal action loop linking output to input, (3) proprioceptive feedback that makes implicit causal knowledge explicit, and (4) asynchronous awakening - perceptual learning must consolidate before action learning begins. We propose agency gain (A = Err_world - Err_self), the predictive advantage of knowing one's own action, as a metric to track this process. The self-aware predictor consistently outperforms the self-blind predictor across periodic (sinusoidal) and chaotic (Lorenz) environments, and the metric survives ablation of all auxiliary components. Only forward-sampled action selection produces meaningful agency gain; two gradient-based alternatives degenerate.
  Equally significant are 12 falsified hypotheses mapping where development stalls: predictive coding alone does not produce self-represent

</details>


### [209] [Dead Directions: Geometric Singular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05957)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了奇异学习理论与信息几何之间的桥梁，通过"死方向"这一原始概念统一了两者：Fisher度量沿该方向退化，等价于解析奇异集的切线方向且具有确定的KL阶。核心贡献是证明KL阶可通过原始参数坐标中朝向奇点的方向Fisher曲率的衰减率恢复，无需Hironaka分解。进一步将这一衰减率扩展到多分量交叉、重数m、奇异涨落ν、先验RLCT偏移及温度后验。在深度网络中，通过多层K-FAC分解将Fisher块分解为激活侧和梯度侧衰减率的乘积，并建立了对偶关系。商定理将该衰减率传递到规范商空间Θ/G，适用于SGD但不适用于标准Adam，为此构建了DDCAdam预处理器。该桥梁提供了奇异几何的参数坐标处理方式、逐架构闭式预测，以及从单一检查点的前向和后向传播中读取Watanabe三重指标(λ, m, ν)的轨迹率方法，无需后验采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异学习理论与信息几何各自研究了相同的参数空间，但使用不同的词汇体系：前者在分解坐标中计算贝叶斯不变量，后者在原始坐标中工作但假设非退化条件，而过度参数化模型经常违反该条件。本文旨在通过"死方向"这一原始概念弥合两者之间的鸿沟，建立统一的框架。

Method: 1) 定义"死方向"为单位退化向量，兼具Fisher度量退化和解析奇异集切线双重含义，通过KL阶量化KL散度消失速率；2) 证明在原始参数坐标中，方向Fisher曲率朝向奇点的衰减率可恢复KL阶；3) 通过平滑纤维上的选择规则将该衰减率转化为Watanabe的单方向RLCT贡献；4) 扩展到多分量交叉、重数m、奇异涨落ν等；5) 在深度网络中使用多层K-FAC分解，将Fisher块分解为激活侧和梯度侧衰减率的乘积；6) 建立商定理将衰减率传递到规范商空间；7) 构建DDCAdam预处理器。

Result: 建立了奇异学习理论与信息几何的完整桥梁，在原始参数坐标中可恢复KL阶和Watanabe的三重指标(λ, m, ν)；实现了深度网络Fisher矩阵的分层分解和对偶关系；商定理适用于SGD但不适用于标准Adam；DDCAdam可恢复规范不变性；可在单一检查点通过前向和后向传播直接读出奇异几何指标。

Conclusion: 本文通过"死方向"这一原始概念统一了奇异学习理论和信息几何，提供了在原始参数坐标中直接处理奇异几何的方法，无需Hironaka分解即可计算贝叶斯不变量。该框架适用于现代深度学习架构，为理解深度网络的泛化行为提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Singular learning theory and information geometry have studied the same parameter spaces in mostly separate vocabularies: the former computes Bayesian invariants in resolved coordinates, the latter works in original coordinates under a non-degeneracy assumption that overparameterised models routinely violate. We bridge them through one primitive, the dead direction: a unit vector along which the Fisher metric degenerates, equivalently a tangent to the analytic singular set with a definite KL order, set by how fast the KL divergence vanishes. The two readings name the same vector; our central move shows its KL order is recoverable as the decay rate of the directional Fisher curvature approaching the singularity, in original parameter coordinates and without a Hironaka resolution. A selection rule on smooth fibres translates this rate into Watanabe's single-direction contribution to the real log canonical threshold, and we extend the recovery to multi-component crossings, multiplicity $m$, the singular fluctuation $ν$ (universal in the KL order for 1D directions), prior-RLCT shifts, and tempered posteriors. We then lift this rate to a deep network: a multi-layer K-FAC factorisation writes each Fisher block as a product of activation- and gradient-side rates with a duality between them, instantiated at modern-network primitives (residual streams, layer normalisation, attention). A quotient theorem carries the rate to the gauge quotient $Θ/G$ under gradient flow on a $G$-invariant metric; SGD qualifies, standard Adam does not, and we construct a $G$-equivariant Adam-family preconditioner (DDCAdam) that does. The bridge yields a parameter-coordinate handle on singular geometry, closed-form per-architecture predictions, and a trajectory-rate readout of Watanabe's triple $(λ, m, ν)$ from one checkpoint's forward and backward passes, without posterior sampling.

</details>


### [210] [Cross-Epoch Adaptive Rollout Optimization for RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05606)
*Yiming Zong,Yige Wang,Jiashuo Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CERO方法，通过贝叶斯后验估计每个提示的成功概率和方差，并在固定全局预算下自适应分配rollout资源，将问题建模为具有递减回报的在线资源分配，通过Fenchel对偶和在线梯度下降求解，在数学推理任务中优于GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM后训练RL方法对每个提示使用固定的rollout预算，忽略了不同提示提供的训练信号差异，导致样本效率低下。

Method: CERO为每个提示维护一个Beta后验分布估计成功概率，使用后验期望伯努利方差作为额外rollout价值的贝叶斯估计；构建凹饱和效用函数，通过Fenchel对偶和投影在线梯度下降求解全局预算约束下的跨提示跨轮次耦合的优化问题。

Result: 在固定提示效用下证明了$O(\sqrt{K})$遗憾界；在多个数学推理benchmark和开源LLM上，CERO一致优于GRPO，证明了自适应rollout预算分配能提升样本效率。

Conclusion: 自适应rollout分配策略可以更有效地利用有限的计算资源，显著提升LLM后训练阶段的样本效率。

Abstract: LLM post-training often relies on reinforcement learning methods that sample multiple rollouts per prompt, yet most existing approaches use a fixed rollout budget for every prompt, despite large differences in the training signal different prompts provide. In this paper, we study adaptive rollout allocation under a fixed global budget and formulate the problem as online resource allocation with prompt-level diminishing returns. Our method, CERO, maintains a Beta posterior over each prompt's success probability and uses the posterior expected Bernoulli variance as a Bayesian estimate of the value of additional rollouts. We use this estimate to construct a concave, saturating utility over cumulative allocations, yielding an objective in which decisions across prompts and epochs are coupled by the global budget. Since the resulting objective is temporally nonseparable, we derive a Fenchel-dual reformulation and update both prompt-level and budget-level dual variables via projected online gradient descent. Under fixed prompt utilities, we prove an $O(\sqrt{K})$ regret bound against the offline allocation benchmark. Experiments on mathematical-reasoning problems show that CERO consistently outperforms GRPO across multiple open-weight LLMs and benchmarks, demonstrating that adaptive rollout budgeting can improve sample efficiency.

</details>


### [211] [Adaptive state-action abstractions via rate-distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06123)
*Fernando E. Rosas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种动态调整强化学习抽象粒度的原则：当学习误差与抽象误差相当的时候，就提高抽象精度。通过分解值函数误差为贝尔曼残差（学习误差）和双模拟度量（抽象误差），并基于率失真原理构建连续可调的软状态-动作抽象，在表格环境下验证了该方法能够在高压缩比下接近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 婴儿学步时先解决粗粒度问题（保持直立、走向照护者），直到粗粒度学习不再有效时才细化问题。强化学习虽有多种构建简化任务的技术，但缺乏动态调整抽象粒度的通用原则。

Method: 提出一个性能证书（performance certificate），将值函数误差分解为两项：由贝尔曼残差捕捉的学习误差界，和由双模拟度量给出的抽象误差界。当两者可比时触发抽象细化。该切换策略通过基于率失真原理的软状态-动作抽象实现，可沿状态和动作维度连续调整分辨率。

Result: 在多种表格设置下验证，表明在状态和动作信息大幅有损压缩的情况下，仍能达到接近最优的性能。

Conclusion: 提出了一种有理论依据的动态抽象粒度调整原则，能在强化学习中实现高效的状态-动作空间压缩，同时保持接近最优的性能。

Abstract: When learning to walk, infants seem to address a coarse version of the problem first - stay upright, reach the caregiver - and refine it only when further practice at that resolution stops paying off. Reinforcement learning offers multiple techniques for building simple versions of complex tasks, but lacks general principles for how to dynamically adjust the granularity of these abstractions during learning. This paper proposes one such principle: refine the abstraction as soon as the learning error within it becomes comparable to the error induced by the abstraction itself. Here, we investigate one way of formalising this principle via a performance certificate that decomposes value error into two terms: a learning error bound captured by a Bellman residual, and an abstraction error bound given by a bisimulation metric. The resulting switching strategy is implemented by soft state-action abstractions built from rate-distortion principles, whose resolution along state and action axes can be continuously adjusted. We validate this construction in a range of tabular settings, showing that near-optimal performance can be achieved under substantial lossy compression of state and action information.

</details>


### [212] [StableRCA: Robust Graph-Agnostic Mechanism-Level Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05636)
*Xiaoyu Lin,Nicholas Tagliapietra,Kehan Li,Lavdim Halilaj,Juergen Luettin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StableRCA 提出了一种无需全局因果图的局部机制级根因分析方法，通过估计局部马尔可夫边界并检测其中的条件分布偏移来识别干预目标，理论上具有指数级收敛保证，实验表明其对图误设鲁棒、支持多目标、可扩展且跨域可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有根因分析面临瓶颈：基于图的方法依赖已知或准确估计的因果图，而无需图的方法要么定位的是边缘异常而非结构性原因，要么对图结构或函数形式施加了严格假设。

Method: StableRCA 通过估计局部马尔可夫边界并检测其中的条件分布偏移来识别干预目标，基于独立因果机制原理，在忠实马尔可夫边界恢复和非退化机制偏移条件下，识别概率随样本量呈指数级收敛。

Result: 在合成基准和五个真实数据集上，StableRCA 对图误设鲁棒、在多干预目标下有效、可扩展至大规模系统，并在多个应用领域表现可靠。

Conclusion: StableRCA 作为一种局部机制级 RCA 框架，避免了全局图发现的瓶颈，在理论和实验上均证明了其有效性、鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Root-Cause Analysis (RCA) seeks to identify the variables responsible for abnormal system behavior in complex domains such as manufacturing, cloud computing, and healthcare. Existing approaches face a critical bottleneck: graph-based causal methods can identify intervention targets but typically require a known or accurately estimated causal graph, while graph-free statistical methods either localize marginal anomalies rather than structural causes, or rely on restrictive assumptions about graph structure or functional form. We propose StableRCA, a local mechanism-level RCA framework that avoids global graph discovery by estimating local Markov boundaries and detecting conditional distribution shifts within them. Leveraging the Independent Causal Mechanism principle, we show that intervention targets can be identified with probability converging exponentially in sample size under faithful Markov boundary recovery and non-degenerate mechanism shifts. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks and five real-world datasets demonstrate that StableRCA is robust to graph misspecification, effective under multiple intervention targets, scalable to large systems, and reliable across diverse application domains. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/StableRCA-E362

</details>


### [213] [PAC-Bayesian Adversarially Robust Generalization for Message Passing Graph Neural Networks: A Sensitivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06293)
*Ziling Liang,Xinping Yi,Qingsong Wen,Shi Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对图神经网络（GNN）对抗鲁棒性泛化分析问题，将基于敏感度感知的PAC-Bayesian框架从深度神经网络扩展到消息传递GNN（MPGNN），通过推导输出关于权重的Jacobian矩阵并利用其秩至多为K的特性构造各向异性高斯后验，从而得到更紧的鲁棒泛化界，将主导维度因子从隐藏层宽度降低为类别数K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN鲁棒泛化分析多采用各向同性高斯后验并在全参数空间控制权重扰动，无法捕捉异构参数敏感性，且依赖于隐藏层宽度相关的复杂度项，导致泛化界不够紧。

Method: 1) 推导不同参数块输出关于权重的Jacobian矩阵，量化参数敏感性；2) 利用K类图分类中Jacobian矩阵秩至多为K的性质，构造Jacobian对齐的敏感度矩阵；3) 使用具有优化协方差的各向异性高斯后验来紧凑上界KL散度；4) 细化学习权重的谱范数依赖，将主导维度因子从隐藏层宽度降至类别数K。

Result: 得到了比现有方法更紧的MPGNN鲁棒泛化保证，将维度依赖从隐藏层宽度项降至类别数K，显著提升了泛化界的紧致性。

Conclusion: 敏感性感知的PAC-Bayesian框架可成功扩展至MPGNN，通过利用Jacobian矩阵的低秩特性和各向异性后验设计，能够获得更紧的鲁棒泛化界，为提升GNN对抗鲁棒性提供理论指导。

Abstract: Whilst the vulnerability of graph neural networks (GNNs) to adversarial attacks poses a critical threat to graph representation learning, the understanding of the robust generalization behavior remains a fundamental challenge in the adversarial setting. Recently, PAC-Bayesian margin-based generalization analysis substantially advances this line of research by providing a flexible and data-dependent analytical framework. However, existing robust analyses often rely on isotropic Gaussian posteriors and control weight perturbations in the full parameter space, which limits the ability to capture heterogeneous parameter sensitivity yet hinges on hidden-width-dependent complexity terms, resulting in not-tight-enough generalization bounds. In this paper, we extend a recently proposed sensitivity-aware PAC-Bayesian framework from deep neural networks to message passing GNNs (MPGNNs) and derive a tighter robust generalization bound in the adversarial setting. Specifically, we first quantify how sensitive the perturbations across different parameter blocks are to the network outputs by deriving the output Jacobians with respect to the weight parameters. Exploiting the fact that these Jacobian matrices have rank at most $K$ in $K$-class graph classification, we then construct Jacobian-aligned sensitivity matrices and use anisotropic Gaussian posteriors with optimized covariances to upper bound the KL divergence in a tight way. Notably, by refining the spectral-norm dependence on the learned weights and reducing the leading dimension factor from hidden-width-dependent terms to the number of classes $K$, our analysis yields much tighter robust generalization guarantees for MPGNNs, thereby guiding their designs to enhance adversarial robustness.

</details>


### [214] [Q-GNN: Query-Conditioned Graph Neural Networks with Type Awareness for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05639)
*Dongxiao He,Ruqiong Zhang,Zhizhi Yu,Ling Ding,Di Jin,Guangquan Xu,Zhiyong Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对GNN-based KGC方法仅利用查询关系而忽略查询实体信息的不足，本文提出Q-GNN，从结构上下文（实体邻域结构编码）和语义类型（大语言模型推断）两方面引入查询实体信息，使推理同时受查询关系和查询实体引导，实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN-based KGC方法在查询中心子图上进行消息传递时，仅以查询关系作为引导信号，查询实体仅作为子图提取的结构锚点，其蕴含的信息未被用于指导推理过程。

Method: 1) 结构上下文：通过专用上下文编码器对查询实体周围的邻域结构和关系模式进行编码，用于调制消息传递；2) 语义类型：利用大语言模型推断查询实体的语义类型，将其融入注意力计算和最终评分，提供类型级别的先验约束。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上的实验结果表明，所提出的Q-GNN方法能够有效提升知识图谱补全性能。

Conclusion: 将查询实体信息从结构上下文和语义类型两个维度融入推理过程，可使GNN-based KGC方法同时受查询关系和查询实体双重引导，从而更充分地利用查询中的信息进行推理。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) aims at predicting missing triplets from incomplete knowledge graphs, which is crucial for downstream applications. Recently, Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based methods have achieved remarkable success by performing message passing over query-centered local subgraphs. However, in practice, a query is jointly defined by both the entity and the relation, with both carrying information indispensable for reasoning, yet these methods rely solely on the query relation as the guiding signal, while the information inherent in the query entity is not leveraged to guide inference - the entity serves merely as a structural anchor for subgraph extraction. To this end, we incorporate query entity information into the reasoning process from two perspectives: the first is structural context, i.e., the neighboring structure and relation patterns around the entity, which is encoded by a dedicated context encoder and used to modulate messages; the second is semantic type of the entity, inferred by a large language model, which is incorporated into attention computation and final scoring to provide type-level prior constraints. Together, these two sources of information enable the reasoning process to be guided by both the query relation and the query entity. Experimental results on standard benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed Q-GNN.

</details>


### [215] [Efficient Mean Curvature Computation on High-Dimensional Data Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06329)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对高维数据局部平均曲率计算复杂度高（O(m^4)）的问题，提出了两项互补贡献：一是通过代数恒等式将复杂度降至O(m^2)；二是通过截断SVD和零空间近似将总复杂度降至O(k^2 m + k m p^2)，实验验证加速50-300倍且精度损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据集的局部平均曲率估计是几何感知机器学习（如MCBP方法）的关键，但原始实现基于k近邻局部形状算子，需显式构造矩阵H并通过迹形式计算，每点复杂度高达O(m^4)，无法处理特征维度超过几十的数据集。

Method: (1) 利用协方差矩阵特征向量的正交性和迹算子的循环性，推导出精确代数恒等式，消除矩阵H的显式构造，将每点成本降至O(m^2)（特征分解后）；(2) 针对局部协方差矩阵秩最多k-1≪m的特性，用k×m中心化数据矩阵的截断SVD替代全特征分解（O(k^2 m)），并基于Haar测度下零空间特征向量外积的期望值推导解析近似，最终总复杂度为O(k^2 m + k m p^2)，其中p=k-1。

Result: 在真实数据集上，相比原始实现获得50至300倍的加速，快速估计器替代原始版本时精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 所提方法提供了可扩展、数据驱动的局部曲率估计，使曲率成为从经典到现代深度学习管道中广泛机器学习任务的实用几何特征。

Abstract: Estimating local mean curvature at each point of a high-dimensional dataset is a key ingredient of geometry-aware machine learning algorithms, such as the Mean Curvature Boundary Points (MCBP) method. The naive implementation of this computation, based on a local shape operator approximated from k-nearest neighbor patches, involves an explicit construction of a matrix $H$ whose trace form yields an $O(m^4)$ cost per point, rendering the approach intractable for datasets with more than a few dozen features. This paper introduces two complementary contributions that together reduce this cost by several orders of magnitude. The first contribution is an exact algebraic identity. This identity, derived from the orthogonality of the eigenvectors of the covariance matrix and the cyclicity of the trace operator, eliminates $H$ entirely and reduces the per-point cost to $O(m^2)$ after the eigendecomposition. The second contribution addresses the remaining $O(m^3)$ bottleneck of the full eigendecomposition. Since the local covariance matrix has rank at most $k-1 \ll m$, we replace it with a truncated SVD of the $k \times m$ centered data matrix, an $O(k^2 m)$ operation, and derive an analytical approximation for the contribution of the null-space eigenvectors based on the expected value of their outer product under the Haar measure. The resulting estimator has total cost $O(k^2 m + k m p^2)$, where $p = k-1$. Experiments on real-world datasets confirm speedups of 50 to 300 times relative to the original implementation, with negligible loss when the fast estimator is used to replace the original version. By providing a scalable and data-driven estimate of local curvature, the proposed method establishes curvature as a practical geometric feature for a broad range of machine learning tasks, from classical to modern deep learning pipelines.

</details>


### [216] [Two-Way Is Better Than One: Bidirectional Alignment with Cycle Consistency for Exemplar-Free Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05675)
*Hongye Xu,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BiCyc，一种双方向投影器对齐方法，通过循环一致性目标解决原型类增量学习中现有单向投影的系统性偏差问题，有效减少灾难性遗忘并提升分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无样本类增量学习（EFCIL）中，原型漂移问题严重。现有基于单向投影的漂移补偿方法存在系统性偏差：要么回溯性地扭曲当前特征几何结构，要么仅局部对齐旧类别，导致跨任务的循环不一致性累积。

Method: 提出BiCyc方法，采用双向投影器对齐（旧到新、新到旧两个映射）并引入循环一致性目标。通过停止梯度（stop-gradient）门控机制，使传输与表示共同演化。理论分析表明循环损失在白化空间中收缩奇异谱趋于单位值，且改进的类均值和协方差传输能减小分类对数几率（log-odds）的扰动。

Result: 在标准EFCIL基准上，BiCyc在从头训练（from-scratch）设置中显著减少了遗忘并提升了精度，在预训练微调（pretrained fine-grained）场景中也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 双方向投影器对齐与循环一致性约束能有效克服单向投影的偏差，为原型类增量学习提供更鲁棒的漂移补偿机制。
